### [1] [MotionMERGE: A Multi-granular Framework for Human Motion Editing, Reasoning, Generation, and Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18956)
*Bizhu Wu,Jinheng Xie,Wenting Chen,Zhe Kong,Jianfeng Ren,Linlin Shen,Ruibin Bai,Rong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionMERGE是一个统一框架，通过显式建模身体部位和时序级别的运动、设计跨粒度协同预训练策略，并构建细粒度运动编辑数据集，弥合了运动-语言模型中粗粒度与细粒度控制之间的鸿沟，实现了更精确的运动生成、理解、编辑及零样本泛化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有运动-语言模型虽统一了理解与生成任务，但操作粒度粗糙，缺乏对身体部位的细粒度理解和精确控制，根本原因在于模型无法聚焦局部运动模式，且训练数据缺少细粒度监督信号。

Method: (1) 在单一LLM内显式建模部位级别和时序级别的运动，实现细粒度语言引导的运动控制（理解+局部编辑）；(2) 提出Reasoning-Aware Granularity-Synergy预训练策略，联合监督跨粒度对齐、时序定位、局部对齐、运动一致性和运动接地链式推理(CoT)；(3) 构建MotionFineEdit大规模数据集（83.7万原子三元组+14.4万复杂三元组），包含细粒度时空纠错指令和运动接地CoT标注。

Result: 大量实验表明MotionMERGE在运动生成、理解和编辑任务上实现了更精确的性能，并在其他复杂运动任务上展现出令人瞩目的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文朝着以更细粒度和类人推理方式与运动交互的模型迈出了重要一步。

Original Abstract: Recent motion-language models unify tasks like comprehension and generation but operate at a coarse granularity, lacking fine-grained understanding and nuanced control over body parts needed for animation or interaction. This stems from fundamental issues in both the model and the data, in which the model can't focus on motion's localized pattern, and the training data lacks fine-grained supervision. To tackle this, we propose MotionMERGE, a unified framework that bridges the granularity gap. First, we pioneer the study of fine-grained languageguided motion control, including detailed understanding and localized editing, by explicitly modeling motion at part and temporal levels within a single LLM, thereby endowing the model with robust priors for precise control. Second, we design ReasoningAware Granularity-Synergy pre-training, a novel strategy that employs joint supervision for cross-granularity alignment, temporal grounding, localized alignment, motion coherency, and motion-grounded chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. This equips the model with fine-grained motion-language alignment, crossgranularity synergy, and explicit reasoning ability. Third, we curate MotionFineEdit, a large-scale dataset (837K atomic + 144K complex triplets) with the first fine-grained spatio-temporal corrective instructions and motion-grounded CoT annotations, establishing a new benchmark for fine-grained text-driven motion editing and motion-grounded reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate the capability of MotionMERGE for more precise motion generation, understanding, and editing, and compelling zero-shot generalization to other complex motion tasks. This work represents a significant step toward models that interact with motion in finer granularity and human-like reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的运动-语言模型统一了理解与生成等任务，但其操作粒度粗糙，缺乏动画或交互所需的细粒度理解和身体部位的精确控制。这源于模型和数据两方面的根本问题：模型无法关注运动的局部模式，且训练数据缺少细粒度监督。为解决这一问题，我们提出MotionMERGE，一个弥合粒度鸿沟的统一框架。首先，我们开创性地研究了细粒度语言引导的运动控制，包括细粒度理解和局部编辑，通过在单一LLM内显式建模部位级别和时序级别的运动，赋予模型用于精确控制的强先验知识。其次，我们设计了推理感知的粒度协同预训练（Reasoning-Aware Granularity-Synergy Pre-training），这是一种新颖的策略，通过联合监督跨粒度对齐、时序定位、局部对齐、运动一致性和运动接地链式推理（CoT），使模型具备细粒度运动-语言对齐、跨粒度协同和显式推理能力。第三，我们精心构建了MotionFineEdit大规模数据集（83.7万原子三元组+14.4万复杂三元组），首次包含了细粒度时空纠错指令和运动接地CoT标注，为细粒度文本驱动的运动编辑和运动接地推理建立了新基准。大量实验表明，MotionMERGE能够实现更精确的运动生成、理解和编辑，并在其他复杂运动任务上展现出令人瞩目的零样本泛化能力。这项工作代表着向以更细粒度和类人推理方式与运动交互的模型迈出了重要一步。

</details>


### [2] [Artifact-Bench: Evaluating MLLMs on Detecting and Assessing the Artifacts of AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18984)
*Yuqi Tang,Yang Shi,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Xuehai Bai,Yue Ding,Ruizhe Chen,Bohan Zeng,Xinlong Chen,Xuanyu Zhu,Bozhou Li,Yuran Wang,Yifan Dai,Chengzhuo Tong,Xinyu Liu,Yiyan Ji,Yujie Wei,Yuhao Dong,Shilin Yan,Fengxiang Wang,Yi-Fan Zhang,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Artifact-Bench基准，系统评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在AI生成视频中感知和推理伪影（如时间不一致、结构失真、语义不连贯）的能力。实验表明主流MLLMs在该任务上表现接近甚至低于随机水平，且与人类感知偏好存在显著偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI视频生成模型虽大幅提升真实感，但仍存在多种伪影；MLLMs虽具备视觉理解能力，但其对视频伪影的感知与推理能力尚不明确；现有基准缺乏对伪影感知的系统和细粒度诊断评估，尤其缺少涵盖非写实视频（如动画、CG风格）的全面评测。

Method: 构建三级层次化伪影分类体系，涵盖写实、动画、CG风格三类视频；基于该分类定义三个互补任务：真实vs.AI生成视频分类、成对真实感比较、细粒度伪影识别；在19个主流MLLMs上进行系统评估。

Result: 大多数MLLMs在伪影感知与推理任务上表现接近甚至低于随机水平；模型判断与人类感知偏好之间存在显著不一致，表明MLLMs作为AI生成视频真实感通用评估器的可靠性有限。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在AI生成视频伪影的细粒度感知与推理方面存在严重不足，尚不能可靠地替代人类评估视频真实感，该领域亟需进一步改进。

Original Abstract: Recent video generative models have greatly improved the realism of AI-generated videos, yet their outputs still exhibit artifacts such as temporal inconsistencies, structural distortions, and semantic incoherence. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong visual understanding capabilities, their ability to perceive and reason about such artifacts remains unclear. Existing benchmarks often lack systematic evaluation of artifact-aware perception and fine-grained diagnostic reasoning, especially across diverse AI-generated video domains beyond photorealistic content. To address this gap, we introduce Artifact-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on AI-generated video artifact detection and analysis. We first establish a three-level hierarchical taxonomy of realism artifacts, covering photorealistic, animated, and CG-style videos. Based on this taxonomy, Artifact-Bench defines three complementary tasks: real vs. AI-generated video classification, pairwise realism comparison, and fine-grained artifact identification. Experiments on 19 leading MLLMs reveal substantial limitations in artifact perception and reasoning, with many models approaching random or even below-random performance in challenging settings. We further observe significant misalignment between MLLM judgments and human perceptual preferences, highlighting their limited reliability as general evaluators for AI-generated video realism.

中文摘要翻译: 近期视频生成模型大幅提升了AI生成视频的真实感，但其输出仍存在时间不一致、结构失真和语义不连贯等伪影。尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）展现出强大的视觉理解能力，但它们感知和推理此类伪影的能力尚不明确。现有基准通常缺乏对伪影感知和细粒度诊断推理的系统性评估，尤其是在涵盖非写实内容的多样化AI生成视频领域。为解决这一问题，我们引入了Artifact-Bench，一个用于评估MLLMs在AI生成视频伪影检测与分析方面能力的综合基准。我们首先建立了真实感伪影的三级层次化分类体系，涵盖写实视频、动画视频和CG风格视频。基于该分类，Artifact-Bench定义了三个互补任务：真实视频与AI生成视频分类、成对真实感比较以及细粒度伪影识别。在19个主流MLLMs上的实验揭示了其在伪影感知与推理方面的显著局限性，许多模型在具有挑战性的设置下表现接近甚至低于随机水平。我们进一步观察到MLLM判断与人类感知偏好之间存在显著偏差，凸显了其作为AI生成视频真实感通用评估器的可靠性有限。

</details>


### [3] [EgoTraj: Real-World Egocentric Human Trajectory Dataset for Multimodal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19004)
*Ahmad Yehia,Abduallah Mohamed,Tianyi Wang,Jiseop Byeon,Kun Qian,Junfeng Jiao,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTraj是一个从自我中心视角记录的真实世界多模态导航数据集，包含75条城市路线、同步RGB视频、6自由度头部姿态、3D眼动视线和场景标注，用于提升自我中心轨迹预测任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自我中心轨迹预测研究受限于真实世界自我中心轨迹数据集的稀缺，阻碍了在人形机器人、可穿戴传感和辅助导航等应用中的进展。

Method: 构建EgoTraj数据集，使用Meta Quest Pro在真实城市环境中采集多穿戴者的导航数据，提供RGB视频、头部姿态、3D眼动视线和场景标注等同步多模态信息，并基于该数据集对多种自我中心轨迹预测方法进行基准测试和消融实验。

Result: 在EgoTraj数据集上对多种SOTA自我中心轨迹预测方法进行了基准测试，并通过消融实验分析了视线、场景和运动线索的贡献，验证了数据集在AR感知、导航和辅助系统中的实用价值。

Conclusion: EgoTraj数据集填补了真实世界自我中心多模态导航数据的空白，为自我中心轨迹预测及相关AR应用提供了重要资源，实验表明视线、场景和运动信息对预测均有贡献。

Original Abstract: Accurately forecasting human trajectories from an egocentric perspective plays a central role in applications such as humanoid robotics, wearable sensing systems, and assistive navigation. However, progress in this direction remains limited due to the scarcity of egocentric trajectory datasets collected in real-world environments. Addressing this need, we introduce EgoTraj, an egocentric multimodal open dataset recorded using Meta Quest Pro (MQPro). EgoTraj contains 75 sequences of human navigation collected from multiple MQPro wearers in real-world urban environments. Each recording provides synchronized RGB video along with ground-truth data, including continuous time-synchronized 6-degree-of-freedom head poses, per-frame 3D eye gaze vectors, scene annotations. To the best of our knowledge, EgoTraj differs from typical egocentric trajectory datasets by capturing long-horizon, self-directed navigation across diverse urban routes with broad participant diversity. To demonstrate the potential of the dataset, we benchmark several state-of-the-art methods for egocentric trajectory prediction and conduct ablation studies to analyze the contributions of gaze, scene, and motion cues. The results highlight the utility of EgoTraj for AR-based perception, navigation, and assistive systems. The EgoTraj dataset, code, and EgoViz Dashboard are publicly available at https://github.com/yehiahmad/EgoTraj.

中文摘要翻译: 准确预测自我中心视角下的人类轨迹在人形机器人、可穿戴传感系统和辅助导航等应用中发挥着核心作用。然而，由于在真实环境中收集的自我中心轨迹数据集稀缺，该方向的进展仍然有限。为应对这一需求，我们推出了EgoTraj——一个使用Meta Quest Pro（MQPro）录制的自我中心多模态开放数据集。EgoTraj包含从多位MQPro穿戴者在真实城市环境中采集的75段人类导航序列。每条记录提供同步RGB视频以及包括连续时间同步的六自由度头部姿态、逐帧3D眼动视线向量和场景标注在内的真实数据。据我们所知，EgoTraj不同于典型的自我中心轨迹数据集，其特点是在多样化的城市路线上捕获了长时程、自导向的导航行为，且参与者具有广泛的多样性。为展示该数据集的潜力，我们对多种最先进的自我中心轨迹预测方法进行了基准测试，并进行了消融实验以分析视线、场景和运动线索的贡献。结果凸显了EgoTraj在基于增强现实的感知、导航和辅助系统中的效用。EgoTraj数据集、代码和EgoViz仪表盘已在https://github.com/yehiahmad/EgoTraj公开提供。

</details>


### [4] [MedFM-Robust: Benchmarking Robustness of Medical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19027)
*Xiangxiang Cui,Tianjin Huang,Yifang Wang,Lijie Hu,Lu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文梳理了医学基础模型（MedFMs）的两大范式——医学视觉语言模型（Med-VLMs）和医学分割基础模型，指出其在临床应用中需进行严格的可靠性评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着MedFMs在多样化临床任务中的广泛应用，亟需在真实世界条件下对其可靠性进行系统评估，以确保临床部署的安全性和有效性。

Method: 本文采用综述性分析方法，将MedFMs分为Med-VLMs（如LLaVA-Med、MedGemma、GPT-4o、Gemini）和基于SAM的医学分割模型（如SAM-Med2D、MedSAM）两类，分别介绍其能力与应用场景。

Result: Med-VLMs在视觉问答、报告生成和视觉定位等任务中表现出色；SAM系列模型推动了医学分割模型的代际更新。

Conclusion: 医学基础模型在临床广泛部署前，必须进行严格的真实世界可靠性评估。

Original Abstract: Medical foundation models (MedFMs) have emerged as transformative tools in healthcare, demonstrating capabilities across diverse clinical applications. These models can be broadly categorized into two paradigms: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) and segmentation foundation models. Med-VLMs range from medical-specialized models such as LLaVA-Med and MedGemma, to general-purpose models like GPT-4o and Gemini, all capable of medical image understanding tasks including visual question answering (VQA), report generation, and visual grounding. Concurrently, the Segment Anything Model (SAM) has catalyzed a new generation of medical segmentation models, with adaptations like SAM-Med2D and MedSAM. The widespread clinical deployment of these models thus necessitates rigorous evaluation of their reliability under real-world conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 医学基础模型（MedFMs）已成为医疗领域变革性的工具，在多种临床应用中展现出强大的能力。这些模型大致可分为两种范式：医学视觉语言模型（Med-VLMs）和分割基础模型。Med-VLMs涵盖从医学专用模型（如LLaVA-Med和MedGemma）到通用模型（如GPT-4o和Gemini），均能完成医学图像理解任务，包括视觉问答（VQA）、报告生成和视觉定位。与此同时，Segment Anything Model（SAM）催生了一代新的医学分割模型，诸如SAM-Med2D和MedSAM等适配模型应运而生。因此，这些模型在临床中的广泛部署亟需在真实世界条件下对其可靠性进行严格评估。

</details>


### [5] [LiFT: Lifted Inter-slice Feature Trajectories for 3D Image Generation from 2D Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19060)
*Xinhe Zhang,Yuyang Zhang,Pengfei Jin,Arnau Marin-Llobet,Na Li,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiFT将3D医学图像生成分解为逐切片生成和切片间轨迹学习，通过特征空间中的有序轨迹建模和三分面漂移损失，以极低计算成本实现高分辨率、解剖一致的3D合成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全3D体积模型计算成本过高，而高效的2D切片生成器难以在第三维度保持解剖一致性。现有方法无法在效率和一致性之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 将3D体积视为特征空间中的有序轨迹，分解为逐切片图像生成和切片间轨迹学习。提出三分面漂移损失（tri-planar drifting loss）对齐生成切片轨迹与真实体积轨迹；在配对翻译中使用双向z-上下文混合器（bidirectional z-context mixer）提供跨平面一致性。

Result: 在BraTS 2023（无条件生成和缺失模态MR）和SynthRAD2023（MR-to-CT）上评估。保持逐切片质量，以约135倍更低的推理成本接近cWDM的缺失MR重建质量，在MR-to-CT中相比无映射消融提升了跨平面一致性。

Conclusion: 轻量级的切片间轨迹学习是实现高分辨率3D医学图像合成的可行路径，在不牺牲质量的前提下大幅降低计算成本。

Original Abstract: High-resolution 3D medical image generation remains challenging because fully volumetric models are computationally expensive, while efficient 2D slice generators often fail to preserve anatomical consistency across the third dimension. We propose LiFT, a framework for Lifted inter-slice Feature Trajectories that factorizes 3D volume synthesis into per-slice image generation and inter-slice trajectory learning. Rather than modeling the volumetric distribution end-to-end, LiFT treats a volume as an ordered trajectory in feature space, capturing how anatomical structures appear, transform, and disappear across depth. A tri-planar drifting loss aligns the trajectory of generated slices with the trajectories of real volumes, enabling distributional learning over inter-slice progressions in unconditional generation; in paired translation, a bidirectional $z$-context mixer trained against the registered target supplies through-plane coherence while preserving per-slice fidelity. We evaluate LiFT on BraTS 2023 (unconditional and missing-modality MR) and SynthRAD2023 (MR-to-CT). Across these settings, LiFT preserves per-slice quality, approaches the reported cWDM missing-MR reconstruction quality at $\sim$$135\times$ lower inference cost (without formal equivalence testing), and improves through-plane coherence on MR-to-CT relative to a no-mapper ablation, demonstrating that lightweight inter-slice trajectory learning is a viable route to high-resolution 3D medical synthesis.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率3D医学图像生成仍然具有挑战性，因为全体积模型计算成本高昂，而高效的2D切片生成器往往无法在第三维度保持解剖一致性。我们提出LiFT，一个基于提升的切片间特征轨迹（Lifted inter-slice Feature Trajectories）框架，将3D体积合成分解为逐切片图像生成和切片间轨迹学习。LiFT不进行端到端的体积分布建模，而是将体积视为特征空间中的有序轨迹，捕捉解剖结构在深度方向上的出现、变形和消失过程。三分面漂移损失（tri-planar drifting loss）将生成切片的轨迹与真实体积的轨迹对齐，从而在无条件生成中实现切片间进程的分布学习；在配对翻译中，针对配准目标训练的双向z-上下文混合器（bidirectional z-context mixer）提供跨平面一致性，同时保持逐切片保真度。我们在BraTS 2023（无条件生成和缺失模态MR）和SynthRAD2023（MR-to-CT）上评估LiFT。在这些设置中，LiFT保持了逐切片质量，以约135倍的更低推理成本接近报告的cWDM缺失MR重建质量（未进行正式等价性检验），并在MR-to-CT任务中相对于无映射消融提升了跨平面一致性，表明轻量级的切片间轨迹学习是实现高分辨率3D医学合成的可行途径。

</details>


### [6] [Probability-Conserving Flow Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20079)
*Parsa Esmati,Junha Hyung,Amirhossein Dadashzadeh,Jaegul Choo,Majid Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive Manifold Guidance (AdaMaG)，一种通过连续方程分析引导机制、约束散度项和分数平行项的自适应引导方法，在不增加推理成本的情况下提高生成质量、减少幻觉并控制饱和度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无分类器引导（CFG）和外推方法对速度/分数进行启发式线性组合，忽略了生成流形的几何结构，导致概率不守恒，强引导下样本偏离学习到的流形。需要一种几何感知的引导方法。

Method: 通过连续方程分析引导机制，证明其效应可分解为散度项和分数平行项；证明散度项在采样接近数据流形时结构性地爆炸，由此提出时间依赖的调度策略配合分数平行衰减，形成即插即用的自适应流形引导（AdaMaG）。

Result: 在图像生成基准上，AdaMaG提高了生成真实感，减少了幻觉，并在高引导强度下实现了受控的饱和度降低。

Conclusion: 大多数用于减少饱和度或改善生成质量的启发式方法可直接对应到分解中的两个项；AdaMaG通过几何约束实现了更优的引导效果。

Original Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative models dominate visual synthesis, with guidance aligning samples to user input and improving perceptual quality. However, Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) and extrapolation-based methods are heuristic linear combinations of velocities/scores that ignore the generative manifold geometry, breaking probability conservation and driving samples off the learned manifold under strong guidance. We analyse guidance through the continuity equation and show its effect decomposes into a divergence term and a score-parallel term defined invariantly across parameterisations. We prove the divergence term blows up structurally as sampling approaches the data manifold, motivating a time-dependent schedule alongside score-parallel attenuation. The resulting plug-and-play rule, Adaptive Manifold Guidance (AdaMaG), bounds both terms at no additional inference cost. Finally, we show that most empirical heuristics for reducing saturation or improving generation quality correspond directly to the two terms in our decomposition. Across image generation benchmarks, AdaMaG improves realism, reduces hallucinations, and induces controlled desaturation in high-guidance regimes.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散和流基生成模型主导着视觉合成领域，引导机制使样本与用户输入对齐并提升感知质量。然而，无分类器引导（CFG）和外推方法对速度/分数进行启发式线性组合，忽略了生成流形的几何结构，破坏了概率守恒，并在强引导下驱使样本偏离已学习的流形。我们通过连续方程分析引导机制，证明其效应可分解为一个散度项和一个分数平行项，且该分解在不同参数化下保持不变。我们证明了当采样接近数据流形时，散度项会发生结构性爆炸，这促使我们提出一种时间依赖的调度策略，配合分数平行衰减。由此产生的即插即用规则——自适应流形引导（AdaMaG），在不增加推理成本的情况下约束了这两个项。最后，我们证明了大多数用于减少饱和度或改善生成质量的经验性启发式方法直接对应于我们分解中的两个项。在图像生成基准上，AdaMaG提高了生成真实感，减少了幻觉，并在高引导强度下实现了受控的饱和度降低。

</details>


### [7] [CRAFT: Critic-Refined Adaptive Key-Frame Targeting for Multimodal Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19075)
*Mahesh Bhosale,Abdul Wasi,Vishvesh Trivedi,Pengyu Yan,Akhil Gorugantu,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT，一个面向真实新闻事件的多视频问答流水线，通过动态关键帧选择、ASR、混合批评家循环迭代验证和引用合并，在MAGMaR 2026上取得最佳结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有系统在处理基于真实新闻事件的多视频问答时，难以从异构视频档案中高效检索查询相关证据，且无法将每个主张归因到其来源。

Method: CRAFT流水线包含：动态关键帧选择、基于多语言回落的逐视频ASR、混合批评家循环（集成UNLI时序蕴涵、DeBERTa-v3跨主张筛选和Llama-3.2-3B裁决器）迭代验证与修复，最后通过引用合并阶段将每个事实与所有支持源标识符一并输出。

Result: 在MAGMaR 2026上，CRAFT取得最佳整体平均分(0.739)、参考召回率(0.810)和引用F1(0.635)。在WikiVideo转换的52个非重叠事件查询上也表现强劲(0.823 Avg)。消融实验表明原子化主张、ASR和批评家循环是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过主张中心的证据聚合方式，在多个基准上展现了优异的泛化能力，其模块化设计有效提升了多视频问答的可靠性和可归因性。

Original Abstract: Grounded multi-video question answering over real-world news events requires systems to surface query-relevant evidence across heterogeneous video archives while attributing every claim to its supporting source. We introduce CRAFT (Critic-Refined Adaptive Key-Frame Targeting), a query-conditioned pipeline that combines dynamic keyframe selection, per-video ASR with multilingual fallback, and a hybrid critic loop to iteratively verify and repair claims before consolidation. The pipeline integrates UNLI temporal entailment, DeBERTa-v3 cross-claim screening, and a Llama-3.2-3B adjudicator, with a final citation-merging stage that emits each fact once with all supporting source identifiers. On MAGMaR 2026, CRAFT achieves the best overall average (0.739), reference recall (0.810), and citation F1 (0.635). We further evaluate on a MAGMaR-style conversion of WikiVideo with 52 non-overlapping event queries, where CRAFT also performs strongly (0.823 Avg), showing that its claim-centric evidence aggregation generalizes beyond MAGMaR. Ablations show that atomic claims, ASR, and the critic loop drive the main gains over the vanilla query-conditioned baseline. Code and implementation details are publicly available at https://github.com/bhosalems/CRAFT.

中文摘要翻译: 基于真实新闻事件的多视频问答要求系统能够在异构视频档案中找出与查询相关的证据，同时将每个主张归因于其支撑来源。我们提出了CRAFT（批评家精炼自适应关键帧定位）——一种基于查询条件的流水线，它结合了动态关键帧选择、带多语言回落的逐视频自动语音识别（ASR）以及混合批评家循环，在合并前迭代验证和修复主张。该流水线集成了UNLI时序蕴涵、DeBERTa-v3跨主张筛选和Llama-3.2-3B裁决器，并包含一个最终的引用合并阶段，将每个事实连同所有支撑来源标识符一并输出。在MAGMaR 2026上，CRAFT取得了最佳整体平均分（0.739）、参考召回率（0.810）和引用F1分数（0.635）。我们还在WikiVideo的MAGMaR风格转换版本上进行了评估，包含52个非重叠事件查询，CRAFT同样表现强劲（平均分0.823），表明其以主张为中心的证据聚合方式能够泛化到MAGMaR之外。消融实验表明，原子化主张、ASR和批评家循环是相较于原始基于查询条件的基线带来主要增益的原因。代码和实现细节已公开于https://github.com/bhosalems/CRAFT。

</details>


### [8] [Knowing When Not to Predict: Self Supervised Learning and Abstention for Safer DR Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19133)
*Muskaan Chopra,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Jan H. Terheyden,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了自监督预训练时长对医学图像模型校准置信度与基于置信度弃权行为的影响，发现准确率饱和后选择性性能仍会显著变化，且更长的预训练并不一致提升可靠性，强调应将预训练时长作为可靠性相关的设计选择。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SSL研究主要用下游准确率或AUROC评估性能，但在糖尿病视网膜病变分级等安全关键筛查任务中，模型还需知道何时其预测不可靠并能弃权不确定病例。因此需探究SSL预训练时长如何影响模型的校准置信度与基于置信度的弃权能力。

Method: 在固定微调协议下评估多个SSL预训练检查点，从校准置信度、覆盖率、选择性准确率和选择性宏F1等多个维度进行评测，对比不同数据集和数据规模下SSL预训练与从头训练的选择性预测表现。

Result: SSL预训练相比从头训练能提升选择性预测表现；但准确率饱和后，选择性性能在不同检查点间仍会显著变化；更长的预训练并不一致地提升可靠性。

Conclusion: 强调了弃权感知评估的重要性，并建议预训练时长应被视为重要的可靠性相关设计选择，而不仅仅是计算细节。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is now a standard way to pretrain medical image models, but performance is still mostly judged by downstream accuracy. For safety-critical screening tasks such as diabetic retinopathy grading, this is not enough: a model must also know when its predictions are unreliable and defer uncertain cases for clinical review. In this work, we examine how the length of SSL pretraining influences calibrated confidence and confidence-based abstention. We evaluate multiple SSL checkpoints under a fixed fine-tuning protocol and assess calibrated confidence, coverage, selective accuracy, and selective macro-F1. Across datasets and data regimes, SSL pretraining improves selective prediction compared to training from scratch. Unlike prior SSL studies that primarily evaluate downstream accuracy or AUROC, we analyze how SSL pretraining duration influences confidence behavior under calibrated confidence-based abstention. However, once accuracy saturates, selective performance can still change markedly across checkpoints, and longer pretraining does not consistently improve reliability. These results underscore the importance of abstention-aware evaluation and suggest that pretraining length should be treated as an important reliability-related design choice rather than only a computational detail. Code is available at GitHub.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督学习(SSL)现已成为预训练医学图像模型的标准方式，但性能仍主要依据下游准确率来评判。对于糖尿病视网膜病变分级等安全关键筛查任务，这远远不够：模型还必须知道其预测何时不可靠，并将不确定病例交由临床复查。本文研究了SSL预训练时长如何影响校准置信度与基于置信度的弃权行为。我们在固定微调协议下评估多个SSL检查点，并从校准置信度、覆盖率、选择性准确率和选择性宏F1等维度进行评测。跨数据集和数据规模，与从头训练相比，SSL预训练均能改善选择性预测。与以往主要评估下游准确率或AUROC的SSL研究不同，我们分析了SSL预训练时长如何在基于校准置信度的弃权条件下影响置信度行为。然而，一旦准确率饱和，选择性性能在不同检查点间仍会显著变化，且更长的预训练并不一致地提升可靠性。这些结果强调了弃权感知评估的重要性，并表明预训练时长应被视为重要的可靠性相关设计选择，而不仅是一个计算细节。代码已发布于GitHub。

</details>


### [9] [Towards Data-Efficient Video Pre-training with Frozen Image Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19137)
*Svetlana Orlova,Niccolò Cavagnero,Gijs Dubbelman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 探索通过冻结预训练图像基础模型、仅训练循环时序模块来处理流式视频的轻量级范式，证明无需大规模视频预训练即可获得强大的时序性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视频基础模型需要在大规模视频数据集上进行端到端预训练，数据量和计算成本极高。而图像基础模型已具备强大的空间表征能力，因此本文探索是否可以通过复用这些空间表征、仅预训练时序推理模块来构建有竞争力的视频模型，从而大幅降低资源需求。

Method: 冻结预训练图像基础模型作为空间编码器，仅训练一个循环时序模块（recurrent temporal module）来处理流式视频输入。该时序模块在冻结的图像模型之上进行轻量级预训练，以捕获视频帧间的时序依赖关系。

Result: 在多个视频理解任务上的实验结果表明，无需大规模视频预训练，仅通过训练时序模块即可获得强大的时序性能，验证了该轻量级训练范式的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法有望显著减少视频预训练所需的数据量和计算资源，为未来研究在冻结图像基础模型上预训练时序模块以构建循环视频基础模型提供了有力依据。

Original Abstract: Video foundation models achieve strong performance across many video understanding tasks, but typically require large-scale pre-training on massive video datasets, resulting in substantial data and compute costs. In contrast, modern image foundation models already provide powerful spatial representations. This raises an important question: can competitive video models be built by reusing these spatial representations and pre-training only for temporal reasoning? We take initial steps toward exploring a lightweight training paradigm that freezes a pre-trained image foundation model and trains only a recurrent temporal module to process streaming video. By reusing an image foundation model as a spatial encoder, this approach could significantly reduce the amount of video data and compute required compared to end-to-end video pre-training. In this work, we explore the feasibility of this approach before investing in computing for video pre-training. Our empirical findings across multiple video understanding tasks suggest that strong temporal performance can emerge without large-scale video pre-training, motivating future work on recurrent video foundation models obtained by pre-training a temporal module on top of a frozen image foundation model. Code: https://github.com/tue-mps/towards-video-image-frozen .

中文摘要翻译: 视频基础模型在众多视频理解任务上取得了优异的性能，但通常需要在大规模视频数据集上进行预训练，从而导致巨大的数据和计算成本。相比之下，现代图像基础模型已经提供了强大的空间表征。这引出了一个重要问题：是否可以通过复用这些空间表征、仅预训练时序推理模块来构建有竞争力的视频模型？本文初步探索了一种轻量级训练范式：冻结预训练图像基础模型，仅训练一个循环时序模块来处理流式视频。通过将图像基础模型复用作空间编码器，与端到端视频预训练相比，该方法有望显著减少所需的视频数据和计算量。在本文中，我们在大规模投入视频预训练计算之前，先探索了该方法的可行性。跨多个视频理解任务的实验结果表明，无需大规模视频预训练即可获得强大的时序性能，这为未来在冻结图像基础模型之上预训练时序模块以构建循环视频基础模型的研究提供了动力。代码地址：https://github.com/tue-mps/towards-video-image-frozen。

</details>


### [10] [Smartphone-based Circular Plot Sampling for Forest Inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19213)
*Su Sun,Jui-Cheng Chiu,Nabin Khanal,Songlin Fei,Yingjie Victor Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅需消费级智能手机和便携支架的单次行走视频完成圆形样地树木测量（DBH和空间位置）的轻量级方法，集成了单目深度估计、树木实例分割和SLAM框架，在人工林和天然林中分别达到1.51 cm和2.30 cm的平均绝对误差，精度可与传统方法媲美但大幅降低了成本和操作复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统森林样地调查中，树木胸径和空间位置的精确测量要么依赖昂贵的地面激光雷达系统，要么依赖耗时的卡尺和罗盘人工方法，在大规模环境中可扩展性和可及性受限。因此，需要一种低成本、易操作且能保持精度的替代方案。

Method: 该方法集成了预训练的单目深度估计和树木实例分割模型，结合同时定位与地图构建（SLAM）框架，在视频序列中联合优化相机轨迹和深度。通过融合SLAM导出的相机位姿与分割深度图恢复树木位置和DBH估计，绝对真实世界尺度通过校准的参考长度锚定。

Result: 在人工林样地和天然林样地分别实现了1.51 cm（MARE 3.98%）和2.30 cm（MARE 5.69%）的平均绝对误差；在不同起始方向和位置下性能保持稳定；跨视频一致性分析表明，不同起始位置测量的树木定位稳定且可重复。

Conclusion: 该方法在达到与现有实地方法相当的精度的同时，大幅降低了设备成本和操作复杂度，使专业研究人员和非专业森林管理者都能在多种操作场景中使用。

Original Abstract: Circular sample plots are a cornerstone of forest inventory, yet accurate measurement of tree diameter at breast height (DBH) and spatial location within such plots remains challenging. Conventional approaches rely either on costly terrestrial LiDAR systems or labor-intensive manual methods involving calipers and compass bearings, limiting their scalability and accessibility in large scale environments. We present a lightweight, smartphone-based pipeline that enables complete plot sampling based tree measurement from a single walkthrough video, requiring no specialized hardware beyond a consumer smartphone mounted on a portable stand. The proposed method integrates pretrained monocular depth estimation and tree instance segmentation with a simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) framework to jointly refine camera trajectories and depth across the video sequence. Tree positions and DBH estimates are recovered by fusing SLAM-derived camera poses with segmented depth maps, with absolute real-world scale anchored via a calibrated reference length.
  The system was evaluated in both managed forest plots and natural forest plot, achieving a mean absolute error of 1.51 cm (MARE 3.98%) and 2.30 cm (MARE 5.69%) respectively, with consistent performance across varying starting directions and positions. Cross-video consistency analysis further demonstrated stable and reproducible tree localization across measurements initiated from different starting positions. The proposed approach achieves accuracy comparable to established field methods while substantially reducing equipment cost and operational complexity, making it accessible to both professional researchers and non-expert forest managers in diverse operational settings.

中文摘要翻译: 圆形样地是森林调查的基石，但在此类样地中精确测量树木胸径（DBH）和空间位置仍然具有挑战性。传统方法要么依赖昂贵的地面激光雷达系统，要么依赖涉及卡尺和罗盘方位的劳动密集型人工方法，限制了其在大规模环境中的可扩展性和可及性。我们提出了一种轻量级、基于智能手机的流水线方法，能够仅通过单次行走视频实现完整的样地采样树木测量，除了安装于便携支架上的消费级智能手机外，无需任何专用硬件。该方法将预训练的单目深度估计和树木实例分割与同时定位与地图构建（SLAM）框架相结合，在视频序列中联合优化相机轨迹和深度。通过融合SLAM导出的相机位姿与分割深度图来恢复树木位置和DBH估计，绝对真实世界尺度通过校准的参考长度锚定。该系统在人工林样地和天然林样地中进行了评估，分别实现了1.51 cm（MARE 3.98%）和2.30 cm（MARE 5.69%）的平均绝对误差，在不同起始方向和位置下性能保持稳定。跨视频一致性分析进一步表明，从不同起始位置开始的测量中，树木定位稳定且可重复。该方法在达到与现有实地方法相当的精度的同时，大幅降低了设备成本和操作复杂度，使专业研究人员和非专业森林管理者都能在多种操作场景中使用。

</details>


### [11] [Rotation-Aligned Key Channel Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19218)
*Beomseok Kang,Dongwon Jo,Jiwon Song,Donghwee Son,Jae-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RotateK，一种基于旋转的结构化Key通道剪枝框架，在不显著损失精度的前提下减少视觉语言模型的KV缓存压力，支持联合令牌-通道剪枝以在相同缓存预算下保留更多视觉令牌。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型推理时KV缓存压力大，令牌剪枝虽能缓解但会永久丢失视觉内容，损害细粒度感知任务。通道剪枝可在固定缓存预算下保留更多视觉令牌，但现有方法面临结构权衡：令牌级通道剪枝灵活但非结构化且慢，头级剪枝硬件友好但不够鲁棒。

Method: RotateK框架通过在线PCA旋转将令牌相关的通道重要性对齐到共享的低维子空间，实现轻量级头级掩码下的精确剪枝；同时设计了融合Triton注意力核，直接在稀疏通道的Key上高效解码。

Result: 在两个代表性VLM骨干上，RotateK在准确率和解码延迟上均一致优于先前Key通道剪枝方法；在相同KV缓存预算下，联合令牌-通道剪枝优于仅令牌剪枝的基线。

Conclusion: 通过旋转实现的结构化Key通道剪枝能有效解决令牌级与头级剪枝的权衡问题，提升效率和鲁棒性，且与令牌剪枝互补可进一步增强性能。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models suffer severe KV cache pressure at inference, as a single image often encodes into thousands of tokens. Most existing methods exploit token sparsity through token pruning, but permanently discarding visual content causes substantial degradation on fine-grained perception tasks. This motivates a complementary axis, feature sparsity: under a fixed KV cache budget, compressing the channel dimension preserves more visual tokens at the same memory cost. Prior Key channel pruning methods, however, face a structural trade-off: token-wise channel pruning is expressive but unstructured and slow, while head-wise approach is hardware-friendly but less robust. We resolve this with RotateK, a rotation-based structured Key channel pruning framework. RotateK applies an online PCA-based rotation that aligns token-dependent channel importance into a shared low-dimensional subspace, enabling accurate pruning under lightweight head-wise masks; a fused Triton attention kernel operates directly on sparse-channel Keys for efficient decoding. Experiments on two representative VLM backbones show that RotateK consistently outperforms prior Key channel pruning in both accuracy and decoding latency, while joint token-channel pruning improves over token-only baselines at matched KV cache budgets.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型在推理时面临严重的KV缓存压力，因为单张图像通常编码为数千个令牌。大多数现有方法通过令牌剪枝利用令牌稀疏性，但永久丢弃视觉内容会严重损害细粒度感知任务。这激发了一个互补的维度——特征稀疏性：在固定的KV缓存预算下，压缩通道维度可以在相同内存成本下保留更多视觉令牌。然而，先前的Key通道剪枝方法面临结构上的权衡：令牌级通道剪枝表达力强但非结构化且速度慢，而头级方法硬件友好但不够鲁棒。我们通过RotateK——一种基于旋转的结构化Key通道剪枝框架——解决了这一问题。RotateK应用基于在线PCA的旋转，将令牌相关的通道重要性对齐到共享的低维子空间，从而在轻量级头级掩码下实现精确剪枝；一个融合的Triton注意力核直接在稀疏通道的Key上高效解码。在两个代表性VLM骨干上的实验表明，RotateK在准确率和解码延迟上均一致优于先前的Key通道剪枝方法，而在匹配的KV缓存预算下，联合令牌-通道剪枝优于仅令牌剪枝的基线。

</details>


### [12] [HAVEN: Hierarchically Aligned Multimodal Benchmark for Unified Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19223)
*Mengqi Shi,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAVEN是一个分层对齐的多模态视频理解基准，通过引入帧、镜头、视频全粒度、全模态（视频+文本）的显式对齐数据集架构，系统评估了现有MLLMs在视频总结、时序推理、多模态定位和显著性排序上的不足，揭示了文本流畅性与多模态理解之间的根本差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在标准视频任务上表现强劲，但其忠实总结和推理复杂叙事的能力缺乏系统评估；已有的摘要基准将监督信号分散在孤立粒度（如关键帧、关键镜头或碎片化文本摘要）上，无法捕捉跨模态对齐的固有层次结构。

Method: 构建了一个全粒度（帧、镜头、视频三级）且全模态（视频与文本）的数据集架构，实现模态间的显式连续对齐；基于该统一标注范式，提出了一套包含总结、时序推理、多模态定位和显著性排序的综合评估套件。

Result: 对SOTA MLLMs的广泛基准测试揭示了表面文本流畅性与扎实的多模态理解之间持续存在的差距。

Conclusion: HAVEN将多模态系统评估推进到传统QA格式之外，为可解释、层次化的视频理解研究提供了严格、标准化的测试平台。

Original Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit strong performance on standard video tasks, their ability to faithfully summarize and reason over complex narratives remains poorly evaluated. Existing summarization benchmarks fragment supervision across isolated granularities, such as keyframes, key shots, or disjointed text summaries, failing to capture the inherently hierarchical structure of cross-modal alignment. To address this critical gap, we introduce HAVEN, a hierarchically aligned multimodal benchmark for unified video understanding. HAVEN pioneers a fully granular (frame, shot, and video levels) and fully multimodal (video and text) dataset architecture, complete with explicit, continuous alignment between modalities. Built upon this unified annotation paradigm, we propose a comprehensive evaluation suite spanning summarization, temporal reasoning, multimodal grounding, and saliency ranking. Extensive benchmarking of state-of-the-art MLLMs exposes a persistent gap between surface-level textual fluency and grounded multimodal understanding. Ultimately, HAVEN advances the evaluation of multimodal systems beyond traditional QA formats, offering a rigorous, standardized testbed to drive future research in interpretable, hierarchical video understanding. We publicly release the dataset, benchmark suite, and evaluation protocols.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在标准视频任务上表现出色，但它们忠实地总结和推理复杂叙事的能力仍未得到充分评估。现有的摘要基准将监督信号分散在孤立的粒度上，例如关键帧、关键镜头或脱节的文本摘要，未能捕捉跨模态对齐固有的层次结构。为填补这一关键空白，我们引入HAVEN——一个用于统一视频理解的分层对齐多模态基准。HAVEN开创了一种全粒度（帧、镜头和视频层级）且全模态（视频和文本）的数据集架构，并配有模态间显式、连续的对齐。基于这一统一的标注范式，我们提出了一套涵盖总结、时序推理、多模态定位和显著性排序的综合评估套件。对最先进MLLMs的广泛基准测试揭示了表面文本流畅性与扎实多模态理解之间持续存在的差距。最终，HAVEN将多模态系统的评估推进到传统问答格式之外，为可解释、层次化视频理解的未来研究提供了一个严格、标准化的测试平台。我们公开发布该数据集、基准套件及评估协议。

</details>


### [13] [Robust Mitigation of Age-Dependent Confounding Effects via Sample-Difficulty Decorrelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19230)
*Nikhil Cherian Kurian,Victor Caquilpan Parra,Abin Shoby,Luke Whitbread,Lyle J. Palmer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种鲁棒框架，通过解耦年龄与疾病相关的困难度趋势来缓解医学图像分类中的年龄依赖性能差异，避免传统不变性方法丢失有用年龄信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学图像分类中存在年龄依赖的性能差异：年龄作为混杂因子，将影像形态与疾病患病率关联，导致高患病率年龄区域过度诊断、低患病率年龄区域诊断不足，且在训练-测试年龄分布偏移下加剧。传统强制年龄不变性的方法会抑制年龄中编码的诊断相关信息。

Method: 1) 热身阶段后，以标签条件方式建模样本难度及其年龄依赖趋势；2) 使用Huber加权亲和权重将年龄与主导年龄难度趋势解耦，减弱混杂驱动的捷径，同时保留有临床意义的非线性年龄信息；3) 引入年龄覆盖分数（Age Coverage Score），根据小批量年龄方差缩放解耦惩罚项，确保年龄多样性有限时优化稳定。

Result: 在两个放射学数据集上，该方法在最小化AUC影响的前提下显著减少了年龄依赖的真阳性和假阳性差异，并对训练-测试年龄分布偏移保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效缓解医学图像分类中因年龄混杂导致的性能偏差，优于传统强制年龄不变性方法，且对年龄分布偏移具有鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Age dependent performance disparities in medical image classification often arise because age acts as a confounder, linking imaging morphology with disease prevalence. In practice, disparities can manifest as overdiagnosis at ages where disease prevalence is higher and underdiagnosis at ages where prevalence is lower, and can worsen under train test shifts in the age distribution. Conventional mitigation approaches that enforce strict age invariance may suppress diagnostically meaningful information encoded in age. We therefore propose a robust framework that mitigates the effects of age-dependent confounding by targeting spurious age linked trends rather than enforcing invariance. Following a warm-up phase, we characterize sample difficulty and model its age-dependent trends in a label-conditioned manner. We decorrelate age from dominant age difficulty trends using robust, Huber weighted affinity weights, attenuating confounding-driven shortcuts while preserving clinically meaningful, nonlinear age information. We further introduce an Age Coverage Score that scales the decorrelation penalty by minibatch age variance to ensure stable optimization under limited age diversity. Across two radiology datasets, our approach reduces age dependent true and false positive disparities with minimal AUC impact and remains robust to increasing train test age distribution shifts.

中文摘要翻译: 医学图像分类中常出现年龄依赖的性能差异，其根源在于年龄作为混杂因子，将影像形态与疾病患病率关联起来。在实践中，这种差异表现为在疾病患病率较高的年龄段过度诊断，在患病率较低的年龄段诊断不足，并且在训练-测试年龄分布偏移下会进一步恶化。传统的缓解方法强制要求年龄不变性，但这可能会抑制年龄中编码的有诊断意义的信息。因此，我们提出一个鲁棒框架，通过针对虚假的年龄关联趋势而非强制不变性来减轻年龄依赖混杂的影响。在热身阶段之后，我们以标签条件方式刻画样本难度并建模其年龄依赖趋势。我们利用鲁棒的Huber加权亲和权重将年龄与主导的年龄难度趋势解耦，从而削弱混杂驱动的捷径，同时保留有临床意义的非线性年龄信息。我们进一步引入年龄覆盖分数，根据小批量的年龄方差来缩放解耦惩罚项，以确保在年龄多样性有限的情况下优化稳定。在两个放射学数据集上，我们的方法在最小化AUC影响的前提下，减少了年龄依赖的真阳性和假阳性差异，并且对训练-测试年龄分布偏移的增加保持鲁棒。

</details>


### [14] [PhyWorld: Physics-Faithful World Model for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19242)
*Pu Zhao,Juyi Lin,Timothy Rupprecht,Arash Akbari,Chence Yang,Rahul Chowdhury,Elaheh Motamedi,Arman Akbari,Yumei He,Chen Wang,Geng Yuan,Weiwei Chen,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyWorld 提出两阶段后训练方法（流匹配微调 + 物理偏好DPO对齐），提升视频生成模型作为世界模拟器时的物理保真度和时序一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大型视频生成模型作为世界模拟器时，生成的视频需要保持物理状态一致性并遵循基本物理原理，但现有模型缺乏物理保真度。

Method: 两阶段后训练：第一阶段用流匹配微调提升视频到视频续接的视觉属性稳定性和运动动态连贯性；第二阶段用基于物理偏好对的直接偏好优化（DPO）对齐生成动态与物理原理。

Result: VBench上平均得分0.769（基线≤0.756），物理保真度基准上平均得分3.09（最强基线2.99）。

Conclusion: 对大型视频生成模型进行续接和物理偏好信号的后训练，可使其成为更有效的Physical AI世界模拟器。

Original Abstract: World simulators can provide safe and scalable environments for training Physical AI systems before real-world deployment. Large video generation models are emerging as a promising basis for such simulators because they can generate diverse and realistic visual futures. However, using them as world simulators requires physically faithful video continuations, namely, generated videos that preserve the physical state implied by the conditioning input, and evolve in ways consistent with basic physical principles. We propose PhyWorld, a video generation world model designed to produce temporally coherent and physically faithful scene continuations through two-stage post-training. In the first stage, we improve video-to-video continuation with flow matching fine-tuning, encouraging stable visual attributes and coherent motion dynamics across frames. In the second stage, we align generated dynamics with physical principles using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) over physics preference pairs, guiding the model toward outputs with higher physical plausibility. To evaluate PhyWorld, we use both standard video-quality benchmarks and a dedicated physical-faithfulness benchmark with per-law scoring. Experiments show that PhyWorld improves video consistency, achieving an average score of 0.769 on VBench compared with 0.756 or below for state-of-the-art baselines. PhyWorld also improves physical plausibility, reaching an average score of 3.09 on our physical-faithfulness benchmark compared with 2.99 for the strongest baseline. These results suggest that post-training large video generation models with continuation and physics-preference signals can make them more effective world simulators for Physical AI.

中文摘要翻译: 世界模拟器可以为物理AI系统在真实世界部署之前提供安全且可扩展的训练环境。大型视频生成模型正在成为此类模拟器有前景的基础，因为它们可以生成多样且逼真的视觉未来。然而，将其用作世界模拟器需要物理上保真的视频续接，即生成的视频既要保持条件输入所蕴含的物理状态，又要以符合基本物理原理的方式演化。我们提出PhyWorld，一种视频生成世界模型，通过两阶段后训练来生成时间连贯且物理保真的场景续接。在第一阶段，我们使用流匹配微调改进视频到视频的续接能力，促进跨帧的稳定视觉属性和连贯运动动态。在第二阶段，我们使用基于物理偏好对的直接偏好优化（DPO）将生成动态与物理原理对齐，引导模型输出更高物理合理性的结果。为评估PhyWorld，我们既使用标准视频质量基准，也使用一个带逐律评分的专用物理保真度基准。实验表明，PhyWorld提升了视频一致性，在VBench上达到平均分0.769，而最先进的基线模型为0.756或更低。PhyWorld还提升了物理合理性，在我们的物理保真度基准上达到平均分3.09，而最强基线为2.99。这些结果表明，对大型视频生成模型进行续接和物理偏好信号的后训练，可以使其成为更有效的Physical AI世界模拟器。

</details>


### [15] [Structuring Open-Ended NAS: Semi-Automated Design Knowledge Structuring with LLMs for Efficient Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19247)
*Yuiko Sakuma,Masakazu Yoshimura,Marcel Gröpl,Zitang Sun,Junji Otsuka,Atsushi Irie,Takeshi Ohashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FairNAD方法，通过半自动化结构化模型设计知识引导神经架构搜索，结合LLM构建多样化搜索空间和多类型变异策略，在多个数据集上超越SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有NAS方法受限于预定义的搜索空间，而LLM辅助的NAS方法因设计思路有偏或质量低导致探索效率低下。

Method: 首先定义高层架构属性的结构模板，然后利用LLM分析论文填充模板以构建富含结构化设计知识的搜索空间，最后提出FairNAD，采用多类型变异策略（公平想法采样、Pareto感知变异、LLM驱动迭代变异、细粒度反馈循环）实现高效探索。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet16-120上分别比当前SOTA提升0.84、2.17和2.35个百分点。

Conclusion: FairNAD通过结构化设计知识和多类型变异策略有效提升了NAS的搜索效率与架构性能。

Original Abstract: Current neural architecture search (NAS) methods are often limited by their predefined, restrictive search spaces. While recent large language model (LLM)-assisted NAS methods enable open-ended search spaces, they often suffer from inefficient exploration due to biased or low-quality design ideas. To address these issues, we propose to semi-automatically structure model design knowledge to guide the search process. Our approach first defines a high-level structural template of architectural attributes. An LLM then populates this template by analyzing papers, creating a rich and diverse search space that embodies this structured design knowledge. To efficiently explore this vast space, we introduce FairNAD, using a multi-type mutation that enables broad exploration through mutation with fair idea sampling, Pareto-aware mutation, LLM-driven iterative mutation, and a fine-grained feedback loop. We demonstrate the effectiveness of FairNAD in discovering high-performing architectures that yield 0.84, 2.17, and 2.35 points improvement on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet16-120, respectively, compared to current state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的神经架构搜索（NAS）方法通常受限于其预定义的、受限的搜索空间。虽然近期基于大语言模型（LLM）的NAS方法能够实现开放式的搜索空间，但它们常因设计思路有偏或质量低下而导致探索效率低下。为解决这些问题，我们提出半自动化地结构化模型设计知识以引导搜索过程。我们的方法首先定义了一个高层级的架构属性结构模板。然后，LLM通过分析论文来填充该模板，从而创建一个蕴含了这种结构化设计知识且丰富多样的搜索空间。为了高效地探索这一广阔空间，我们引入了FairNAD，采用多类型变异策略，通过公平想法采样实现广泛的变异探索、Pareto感知变异、LLM驱动的迭代变异以及细粒度的反馈循环。我们证明了FairNAD在发现高性能架构方面的有效性，与当前最先进的方法相比，在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet16-120上分别取得了0.84、2.17和2.35个百分点的提升。

</details>


### [16] [What Makes Synthetic Data Effective in Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19289)
*Jinjin Zhang,Xiefan Guo,Yizhou Jin,Nan Zhou,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩散模型生成的合成图像在视觉分割任务中的效用，发现密集场景组成和精细实例保真度的合成图像能产生更具判别性的空间表征，并据此提出统一框架SENSE，利用灵活可扩展的合成数据显著提升分割性能，在Cityscapes、COCO和ADE20K上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管现代扩散模型在逼真图像合成方面表现卓越，但其在复杂视觉分割任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索，因此本文旨在系统分析合成图像在分割任务中的效用并挖掘其优势。

Method: 提出SENSE统一框架，利用灵活且可扩展的合成数据增强分割性能；该框架与模型无关，兼容多种架构（如DPT和Mask2Former），并能有效扩展到不同参数规模的模型。

Result: 合成图像中密集场景组成和精细实例保真度能产生更具判别性的空间表征；SENSE在Cityscapes、COCO和ADE20K等数据集上验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 合成数据在视觉分割任务中具有显著潜力，SENSE框架通过灵活利用合成数据能有效提升分割性能，且具有良好的模型通用性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Driven by rapid advances in large-scale generative models, synthetic data has emerged as a promising solution for visual understanding. While modern diffusion models achieve remarkable photorealistic image synthesis, their potential in complex visual segmentation tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we conduct a systematic analysis of synthetic images from state-of-the-art diffusion models to uncover the factors governing their utility. In particular, synthetic images characterized by dense scene composition and fine instance fidelity demonstrate distinctive benefits, yielding significantly more discriminative spatial representations. Building on these insights, we propose SENSE, a unified framework that leverages flexible and scalable synthetic data to substantially enhance segmentation performance. Notably, SENSE is model-agnostic, compatible with diverse architectures (e.g., DPT and Mask2Former), and scales effectively across models with varying parameter capacities. Extensive experiments on Cityscapes, COCO, and ADE20K validate the effectiveness and generalization capability of our approach. Code is available at https://github.com/zhang0jhon/SENSE.

中文摘要翻译: 受大规模生成模型快速发展的推动，合成数据已成为视觉理解领域一种有前景的解决方案。虽然现代扩散模型在逼真图像合成方面取得了显著成就，但其在复杂视觉分割任务中的潜力仍未得到充分探索。本文对来自最先进扩散模型的合成图像进行了系统分析，以揭示影响其效用的关键因素。特别地，具有密集场景组成和精细实例保真度的合成图像展现出独特的优势，能够产生更具判别性的空间表征。基于这些发现，我们提出了SENSE——一个统一框架，利用灵活且可扩展的合成数据显著提升分割性能。值得注意的是，SENSE与模型无关，兼容多种架构（如DPT和Mask2Former），并能有效扩展到不同参数规模的模型。在Cityscapes、COCO和ADE20K上的大量实验验证了我们方法的有效性和泛化能力。代码可在https://github.com/zhang0jhon/SENSE获取。

</details>


### [17] [iGSP:Implicit Gradient Subspace Projection for Efficient Continual Learning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19301)
*Xuezhi Cui,Dongbo Zhou,Wang Guo,Zeyuan Wang,Ziyu Li,Gaozhi Zhou,Xian Li,Ling Zhao,Wentao Yang,Chao Tao,Haifeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出iGSP框架，通过隐式梯度子空间投影实现视觉-语言模型的高效持续学习，在不增加参数的前提下实现知识复用，在MTIL基准上达到SOTA精度，同时减少42.7%可训练参数和86.9%总参数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法存在两个问题：(1) 为每个任务分配独立模块导致参数爆炸；(2) 基于视觉相似性的共享机制错误地将表面视觉相似等同于底层对齐一致性，导致视觉相似但逻辑不同的任务间出现严重负迁移，同时无法利用视觉多样任务间的对齐复用。

Method: 提出iGSP框架，将对齐共享视为共享低秩子空间内优化轨迹重叠的几何问题。包含两个阶段：(1) 子空间识别阶段：利用MoE路由器的早期收敛建立子空间基，通过基预扩展引入候选专家，应用子空间约束正则化将新任务梯度隐式投影到历史子空间，以路由概率作为梯度流指示器精确剪枝冗余维度；(2) 正交子空间微调阶段：固定结构基并移除正则化，快速拟合任务特定残差损失。

Result: 在MTIL基准上达到SOTA准确率；相比现有最优方法，平均可训练参数减少42.7%；相比基线方法，最终总参数减少86.9%。

Conclusion: 证明了将知识共享建模为低秩子空间中的梯度轨迹重叠问题是一种有效且参数高效的方法，能同时缓解负迁移和参数爆炸问题。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models require efficient adaptation to continually emerging downstream tasks. While Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning mitigates catastrophic forgetting, assigning isolated modules per task leads to parameter explosion. Conversely, recent similarity-driven sharing mechanisms falsely equate superficial visual similarity with underlying alignment consistency. This fundamental mismatch triggers severe negative transfer between visually similar but logically distinct tasks and fails to exploit alignment reuse across visually diverse ones. We argue thatalignment sharing is fundamentally a geometric problem of overlapping optimization trajectories within shared low-rank subspaces. Grounded in this insight, we propose iGSP, a novel framework that achieves efficient adaptation via implicit gradient subspace projection. Leveraging the early convergence of MoE routers to establish the subspace basis, iGSP bifurcates the adaptation process into two phases. First, the Subspace Identification phase introduces candidate experts via basis pre-expansion, applies a novel subspace-constrained regularization to implicitly project new task gradients onto the historical subspace, and precisely prunes redundant dimensions by treating routing probabilities as gradient flow indicators, ultimately to maximize knowledge reuse. Second, the Orthogonal Subspace Fine-Tuning phase fixes this structural basis and removes the regularization to rapidly fit the task-specific residual loss. Extensive experiments on the MTIL benchmark demonstrate that iGSP achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while significantly improving training efficiency, reducing the average trainable parameters by 42.7\% compared to current SOTA methods, and decreasing the final total parameters by 86.9\% relative to counterparts. The source code is available at https://github.com/GeoX-Lab/iGSP.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型需要高效地适应不断涌现的下游任务。虽然参数高效微调缓解了灾难性遗忘，但为每个任务分配独立模块会导致参数爆炸。相反，近期基于相似性的共享机制错误地将表面视觉相似等同于底层对齐一致性。这种根本性不匹配导致视觉相似但逻辑不同的任务之间出现严重的负迁移，并且无法利用视觉多样化任务之间的对齐复用。我们认为对齐共享本质上是在共享低秩子空间内重叠优化轨迹的几何问题。基于这一见解，我们提出iGSP，一种通过隐式梯度子空间投影实现高效自适应的新框架。利用MoE路由器的早期收敛建立子空间基，iGSP将自适应过程分为两个阶段。首先，子空间识别阶段通过基预扩展引入候选专家，应用新型子空间约束正则化将新任务梯度隐式投影到历史子空间，并通过将路由概率视为梯度流指示器精确剪枝冗余维度，最终最大化知识复用。其次，正交子空间微调阶段固定此结构基并移除正则化，以快速拟合任务特定残差损失。在MTIL基准上的大量实验表明，iGSP在显著提升训练效率的同时达到了最先进的准确率，相比当前最优方法平均可训练参数减少42.7%，相比对比方法最终总参数减少86.9%。源代码可在https://github.com/GeoX-Lab/iGSP获取。

</details>


### [18] [MMGS: 10$\times$ Compressed 3DGS through Optimal Transport Aggregation based on Multi-view Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19304)
*Beizhen Zhao,Sicheng Yu,Ziran Yin,Dongxu Shen,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于全局最优传输（OT）的3D高斯泼溅压缩框架，通过几何分布匹配实现仅用10%的图元和10倍训练加速，同时保持SOTA渲染质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩方法依赖局部采样或固定剪枝阈值，难以在减少冗余图元与保持高保真渲染之间取得良好平衡。

Method: (1) 多视图3D高斯贡献排名机制，利用几何一致性过滤图元；(2) 基于全局最优传输的聚合算法，合并冗余图元同时保持底层几何结构；(3) 基于最优传输的稠密化算子，维持高斯分布属性以实现稳定优化。

Result: 仅需原始3DGS 10%的图元和1/10的训练时间，即可达到最先进的渲染质量。

Conclusion: 将高斯优化建模为全局几何分布匹配问题，结合最优传输理论，能有效平衡压缩率与渲染质量，显著提升3DGS的训练效率。

Original Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized 3D reconstruction, it suffers from significant overhead due to massive redundant primitives. Existing compression methods typically rely on local sampling or fixed pruning thresholds, which often struggle to balance redundancy reduction with high-fidelity rendering. To address this, we propose a novel framework that formulates Gaussian optimization as a global geometric distribution matching problem. Specifically, our approach integrates three components: (1) we introduce a multi-view 3D Gaussian contribution ranking mechanism that filters primitives using geometric consistency instead of local heuristics; (2) we propose a global Optimal Transport (OT)-based aggregation algorithm that merges redundant primitives while preserving the underlying geometry; and (3) we design an OT-based densification operator that maintains the Gaussian's distributional properties for stable optimization. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality with only \textbf{10$\%$} primitives and \textbf{10$\times$} accelerated training speeds compared to vanilla 3DGS.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）技术彻底改变了三维重建领域，但由于存在大量冗余图元，其计算开销十分巨大。现有的压缩方法通常依赖局部采样或固定的剪枝阈值，往往难以在减少冗余与保持高保真渲染之间取得平衡。针对这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的框架，将高斯优化形式化为一个全局几何分布匹配问题。具体而言，我们的方法包含三个组成部分：（1）引入了一种多视图3D高斯贡献排名机制，利用几何一致性而非局部启发式方法对图元进行过滤；（2）提出了一种基于全局最优传输（OT）的聚合算法，在合并冗余图元的同时保持底层几何结构；（3）设计了一种基于最优传输的稠密化算子，以维持高斯的分布特性从而实现稳定优化。我们的方法仅需使用原始3DGS **10%** 的图元和 **10倍** 的训练加速，即可达到最先进的渲染质量。

</details>


### [19] [MetaRA: Metamorphic Robustness Assessment for Multimodal Large Language Model-based Visual Question Answering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19307)
*Quanxing Xu,Yuhao Tian,Ling Zhou,Xian Zhong,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MetaRA，一种基于蜕变测试的多模态大语言模型VQA鲁棒性评估框架，可系统性地揭示模型在语言扰动、视觉线索依赖和多模态推理方面的脆弱性，比传统准确率指标提供更丰富的诊断信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VQA评估依赖静态数据集和准确率指标，无法捕获模型的鲁棒性、一致性和泛化能力，缺乏系统性评估模型脆弱性的方法。

Method: 基于蜕变测试（MT）提出MetaRA框架，利用蜕变关系（MRs）生成图像-问题输入的受控变体，系统性地探测多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在VQA任务中的脆弱性。

Result: 在多个MLLM-based VQA模型上应用MetaRA，揭示了模型对语言扰动敏感、过度依赖浅层视觉线索以及多模态推理能力不足等细微失败模式，提供了比传统准确率指标更丰富的诊断信息。

Conclusion: VQA系统需要系统性鲁棒性评估，蜕变评估可作为可扩展、模型无关的方法，推动可信多模态AI的发展。

Original Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA), as the representative multimodal task, serves as a key benchmark for evaluating the reasoning capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, existing evaluations largely rely on static datasets and accuracy-based metrics, which fail to capture robustness, consistency, and generalization. Inspired by Metamorphic Testing (MT), we propose Metamorphic Robustness Assessment (MetaRA), a testing framework that employs Metamorphic Relations (MRs) to systematically probe vulnerabilities in MLLM-based VQA systems. MetaRA generates controlled variations of image-question inputs based on specific MRs and evaluates models across diverse conditions. Applying MetaRA to multiple MLLM-based VQA models across different tasks reveals nuanced failure patterns, including sensitivity to linguistic perturbations, over-reliance on superficial visual cues, and deeper weaknesses in multimodal reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that MetaRA provides richer diagnostic insights than conventional accuracy metrics, exposing failure modes that remain hidden under standard benchmarks. Overall, this work highlights the need for systematic robustness evaluation in VQA and positions metamorphic assessment as a scalable, model-agnostic approach toward trustworthy multimodal AI.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉问答（VQA）作为代表性多模态任务，是评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）推理能力的关键基准。然而，现有评估主要依赖静态数据集和基于准确率的指标，无法捕获鲁棒性、一致性和泛化能力。受蜕变测试（MT）启发，我们提出蜕变鲁棒性评估（MetaRA），一种采用蜕变关系（MRs）系统性探测基于MLLM的VQA系统脆弱性的测试框架。MetaRA基于特定MRs生成图像-问题输入的受控变体，并在多样化条件下评估模型。将MetaRA应用于多个基于MLLM的VQA模型及不同任务，揭示了细微的失败模式，包括对语言扰动的敏感性、过度依赖浅层视觉线索，以及多模态推理中更深层次的缺陷。实验结果表明，MetaRA比传统准确率指标提供更丰富的诊断信息，暴露了标准基准下仍未被发现的失败模式。总体而言，本工作强调了VQA中系统性鲁棒性评估的必要性，并将蜕变评估定位为一种可扩展、模型无关的方法，以推动可信多模态AI的发展。

</details>


### [20] [SWEET: Sparse World Modeling with Image Editing for Embodied Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19319)
*Yiren Song,Yihan Wang,Xiyao Deng,Zhuoran Yan,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SWEET框架，利用图像编辑模型（而非视频生成模型）作为稀疏视觉世界模型，通过逐步图像编辑生成任务相关关键帧，再结合目标条件扩散动作预测器生成可执行动作，实现机器人操作的稀疏视觉规划，实验证明该方法在视觉保真度、推理成本和任务表现上优于视频生成方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 密集视频生成计算成本高且对于许多操作任务而言并非必要，因为操作进度可通过少量任务相关视觉状态来概括。因此，研究图像编辑模型能否作为稀疏视觉世界模型，通过预测任务级未来状态来实现机器人操作规划。

Method: 提出SWEET框架：(1) 使用图像编辑模型FLUX-Kontext逐步生成任务相关的操作关键帧序列，以语言指令和可选箭头式空间引导为条件；(2) 采用目标条件扩散动作预测器将相邻的想象关键帧转换为可执行的动作块；(3) 引入混合训练策略，使用过滤后的编辑目标来减少真实视觉子目标和编辑后子目标之间的不匹配。

Result: 在DROID和RoboMimic数据集上，SWEET在已见和未见场景中均提升了关键帧预测效果，并建立了从序列关键帧规划到可执行机器人动作的完整流水线。对比实验中，图像编辑模型比视频生成模型（Wan2.2）产生更可靠的任务级关键帧，具有更好的视觉保真度和更低的推理成本。

Conclusion: 图像编辑是有前景且尚未被充分探索的具身视觉预测方向，SWEET证明了利用图像编辑进行稀疏视觉规划可实现有效的机器人操作控制。

Original Abstract: Visual prediction has emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied control, where future observations are generated and then translated into actions. However, dense video generation is computationally expensive and often unnecessary for many manipulation tasks, whose progress can be summarized by a small number of task-relevant visual states. In this work, we study whether image editing models can serve as sparse visual world models for robot manipulation by predicting task-level future states without dense video rollout. We first conduct a controlled comparison between the video generation model Wan2.2 and the image editing model FLUX-Kontext under the same robotic data setting, and find that image editing produces more reliable task-level keyframes with better visual fidelity and substantially lower inference cost. Motivated by this observation, we propose SWEET, a one-shot sparse visual planning framework that progressively generates a sequence of task-relevant manipulation keyframes through successive image editing, conditioned on language instructions and optional arrow-based spatial guidance. A goal-conditioned diffusion action predictor then converts adjacent imagined keyframes into executable action chunks. To reduce the mismatch between real and edited visual subgoals, we further introduce a mixed-training strategy with filtered edited targets. Experiments on DROID and RoboMimic show that SWEET improves keyframe prediction across seen and unseen scenes and enables a full pipeline from sequential keyframe planning to executable robot actions, suggesting that image editing is a promising and underexplored direction for embodied visual prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉预测已成为具身控制的一种有前景的范式，即先生成未来观测再将其转化为动作。然而，密集视频生成计算成本高昂，且对于许多操作任务来说往往并非必要——这些任务的进展可以通过少量任务相关的视觉状态来概括。在这项工作中，我们研究图像编辑模型能否作为机器人操作的稀疏视觉世界模型，通过预测任务级未来状态而无需密集视频推演。我们首先在同一机器人数据设置下，对视频生成模型Wan2.2和图像编辑模型FLUX-Kontext进行了控制对比实验，发现图像编辑能生成更可靠的任务级关键帧，具有更好的视觉保真度和显著更低的推理成本。受此观察启发，我们提出SWEET——一种一次性稀疏视觉规划框架，通过连续的图像编辑，以语言指令和可选的箭头式空间引导为条件，逐步生成一系列任务相关的操作关键帧。然后，一个目标条件扩散动作预测器将相邻的想象关键帧转换为可执行的动作块。为减少真实视觉子目标与编辑后的子目标之间的不匹配，我们进一步引入了一种使用过滤后编辑目标的混合训练策略。在DROID和RoboMimic上的实验表明，SWEET在已见和未见场景中均提升了关键帧预测效果，并能实现从序列关键帧规划到可执行机器人动作的完整流水线，这表明图像编辑是具身视觉预测中一个前景广阔且尚未被充分探索的方向。

</details>


### [21] [TextAlign: Preference Alignment for Text Rendering with Hierarchical Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19320)
*Mingxuan Cui,Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Qian Jiang,Zhecheng Shi,Jiaming Wang,Zirui Song,Fajri Koto,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextAlign，一种基于偏好对齐的后训练框架，通过分层VLM奖励信号（全局/单词/字形级别）改进文本到图像生成模型中的文本渲染能力，无需修改生成器架构，在FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image-Turbo上取得一致提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型在忠实文本渲染方面存在持续弱点，同时需要语义指令遵循和细粒度字形结构；先前方法依赖特定架构模块或编码器修改，增加了跨基础模型的部署复杂性。

Method: 提出TextAlign非侵入式框架，保持生成器架构不变；核心是分层VLM奖励模型，将渲染错误分解为全局、单词和字形三个层次，将二元缺陷判断转化为标量偏好信号，支持GRPO和DPO两种偏好优化策略。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image-Turbo上的实验显示，基于OCR的文本准确率一致提升，且不降低通用生成质量；与SD3.5、Qwen-Image、AnyText、TextDiffuser等强基线和文本渲染模型相比，该方法在文本渲染改进上更具竞争力。

Conclusion: 奖励设计为改进文本渲染提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，优于模型重新设计；将文本渲染视为后训练偏好对齐问题具有有效性和通用性。

Original Abstract: Faithful text rendering remains a persistent weakness of large text-to-image generative models, as it requires both semantic instruction following and fine-grained glyph-level structure. Prior methods often improve this ability through architecture-specific modules or encoder modifications, which complicate deployment across foundation models. We study text rendering as a post-training preference-alignment problem and propose TextAlign, a non-invasive framework that keeps the generator architecture unchanged. The key component is a hierarchical vision-language model (VLM)-based reward that decomposes rendering errors into global, word, and glyph levels, then converts binary defect judgments into a scalar preference signal. The resulting signal supports both Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Z-Image-Turbo show consistent gains in OCR-based text accuracy without degrading general generation quality. Compared with strong foundation and text-rendering baselines, including SD3.5, Qwen-Image, AnyText, and TextDiffuser, these results indicate that reward design offers a scalable alternative to model redesign for improving text rendering.

中文摘要翻译: 忠实文本渲染仍然是大型文本到图像生成模型的一个持续弱点，因为它同时需要语义指令遵循和细粒度字形级别的结构。先前的方法通常通过特定架构的模块或编码器修改来提升这一能力，这增加了跨基础模型部署的复杂性。我们将文本渲染研究为一个后训练的偏好对齐问题，并提出了TextAlign——一个保持生成器架构不变的非侵入式框架。其关键组件是一个基于分层视觉-语言模型的奖励函数，它将渲染错误分解为全局、单词和字形三个层次，然后将二元缺陷判断转换为标量偏好信号。生成的信号同时支持组相对策略优化和直接偏好优化。在FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image-Turbo上的实验表明，基于OCR的文本准确率获得一致提升，且不降低通用生成质量。与包括SD3.5、Qwen-Image、AnyText和TextDiffuser在内的强基础模型和文本渲染基线相比，这些结果表明奖励设计为改进文本渲染提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，优于模型重新设计。

</details>


### [22] [RE-VLM: Event-Augmented Vision-Language Model for Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19329)
*Hanqing Liu,Mingjie Liu,Luoping Cui,Endian Lin,Donghong Jiang,Chuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RE-VLM是首个双流视觉-语言模型，通过联合RGB图像与事件流（事件相机数据）实现正常与恶劣条件下的鲁棒场景理解。它采用并行编码器、渐进训练策略，并通过图驱动流水线生成场景图以合成文本标注，在多个基准上优于现有RGB-only和event-only模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统视觉-语言模型在低光、高动态范围、快速运动等恶劣条件下性能严重下降，因为标准RGB图像在这些环境中质量退化。事件相机提供的异步逐像素亮度变化记录具有高时间分辨率和宽动态范围，能保留帧失效时的运动线索。作者旨在融合这两种互补模态，实现跨正常与挑战性场景的鲁棒视觉语言理解。

Method: 1) 提出RE-VLM双流架构，包含并行的RGB编码器和事件编码器，以及渐进式训练策略，对齐异构视觉特征与语言；2) 设计图驱动流水线，将同步的RGB-事件流转换为可验证的场景图，并从中合成描述文本和问答对；3) 构建两个数据集：PEOD-Chat（光照挑战场景）和RGBE-Chat（多样化场景）。

Result: 在描述生成和VQA基准上，RE-VLM在参数规模可比的情况下，持续优于最先进的RGB-only和event-only模型，在挑战性条件下提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 事件增强的视觉-语言模型（RE-VLM）能在各类真实环境中实现鲁棒的视觉语言理解，验证了融合事件模态对提升VLM在恶劣条件下表现的有效性。

Original Abstract: Conventional vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to interpret scenes captured under adverse conditions (e.g., low light, high dynamic range, or fast motion) because standard RGB images degrade in such environments. Event cameras provide a complementary modality: they asynchronously record per-pixel brightness changes with high temporal resolution and wide dynamic range, preserving motion cues where frames fail. We propose RE-VLM, the first dual-stream vision-language model that jointly leverages RGB images and event streams for robust scene understanding across both normal and challenging conditions. RE-VLM employs parallel RGB and event encoders together with a progressive training strategy that aligns heterogeneous visual features with language. To address the scarcity of RGB-Event-Text supervision, we further propose a graph-driven pipeline that converts synchronized RGB-Event streams into verifiable scene graphs, from which we synthesize captions and question-answer (QA) pairs. To develop and evaluate RE-VLM, we construct two datasets: PEOD-Chat, targeting illumination-challenged scenes, and RGBE-Chat, covering diverse scenarios. On captioning and VQA benchmarks, RE-VLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art RGB-only and event-only models with comparable parameter counts, with particularly large gains under challenging conditions. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of event-augmented VLMs in achieving robust vision-language understanding across a wide range of real-world environments. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/bupt-ai-cz/RE-VLM.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的视觉-语言模型（VLM）在解读恶劣条件（如低光、高动态范围或快速运动）下捕获的场景时表现不佳，因为标准RGB图像在此类环境中会发生退化。事件相机提供了一种互补模态：它能异步记录逐像素的亮度变化，具有高时间分辨率和宽动态范围，在帧失效时仍能保留运动线索。我们提出了RE-VLM，这是首个双流视觉-语言模型，它联合利用RGB图像和事件流，在正常和挑战性条件下实现鲁棒的场景理解。RE-VLM采用并行的RGB事件编码器，配合渐进式训练策略，将异构视觉特征与语言进行对齐。为解决RGB-事件-文本监督数据稀缺的问题，我们进一步提出了一种图驱动流水线，将同步的RGB-事件流转换为可验证的场景图，并从中合成描述文本和问答对。为开发和评估RE-VLM，我们构建了两个数据集：针对光照挑战场景的PEOD-Chat和覆盖多样化场景的RGBE-Chat。在描述生成和VQA基准上，RE-VLM在参数规模可比的情况下持续优于最先进的纯RGB和纯事件模型，在挑战性条件下提升尤为显著。这些结果证明了事件增强型VLM在各类真实环境中实现鲁棒视觉语言理解的有效性。代码和数据集发布在 https://github.com/bupt-ai-cz/RE-VLM。

</details>


### [23] [Selective, Regularized, and Calibrated: Harnessing Vision Foundation Models for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19340)
*Junyuan Ma,Xunzhi Xiang,Wenbin Li,Qi Fan,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HERA，一种三阶段分层范式表示适配方法，用于解决视觉基础模型在跨域小样本分割中面临的过拟合和跨域不一致问题，无需重新训练源域数据即可有效适应新领域，在多个基准上超越SOTA超过4.1 mIoU。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型在跨域小样本分割中存在两大挑战：(1) 每个新类别仅有的少量标注样本相对于VFM预训练规模过小，微调时易过拟合；(2) 目标域偏移在预训练中未充分体现，导致跨域不一致性和逐层敏感性。需要一种无需源数据重新训练、能从有限标签中有效学习并适应新领域的方法。

Method: 提出HERA框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 分层层级选择（HLS），通过基于数据的样本迁移风险（ETR）自适应选择最具信息量的VFM层；(2) 先验引导正则化（PGR），对所选表示进行交互正则化，生成结构良好的局部信号；(3) 像素级自适应校准（PAC），结合所选表示与精炼的交互图进行逐像素预测校准。整个pipeline仅微调不到2.7%的参数。

Result: 在多个跨域小样本分割基准上，HERA超越当前最先进方法超过4.1 mIoU。

Conclusion: 提出了HERA三阶段框架，通过分层的选择-正则化-校准pipeline，引导冻结的VFM特征适应新领域，仅微调极少参数即可显著提升跨域小样本分割性能。

Original Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) have achieved strong performance across various vision tasks. However, it still remains challenging to apply VFMs for cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CD-FSS), which segments objects of novel classes under domain shifts using only a few labeled exemplars. The challenge is mainly driven by two factors: (1) limited labeled exemplars per novel class relative to the scale of VFM pre-training, making the model prone to overfitting during retraining, and (2) target-domain shifts underrepresented during pre-training, inducing cross-domain inconsistency and layer-wise sensitivity. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Exemplar Representation Adaptation (HERA), a three-stage select-regularize-calibrate VFM-based segmentation framework that learns effectively from limited labels and adapts to novel domains without source-data retraining. We first design Hierarchical Layer Selection (HLS) to adaptively identify the most informative VFM layer using a data-dependent Exemplar Transfer Risk (ETR) computed for each candidate layer. Then, Prior-Guided Regularization (PGR) regularizes interactions on the selected representation, yielding well-structured local signals for the subsequent stage. Furthermore, Pixelwise Adaptive Calibration (PAC) combines the selected representation with the refined interaction maps to calibrate pixel-wise predictions, producing consistent masks. Together, these stages form a hierarchical select-regularize-calibrate pipeline that guides frozen VFM features in new domains while fine-tuning less than 2.7% of parameters at test time. Extensive experiments show that HERA surpasses the state of the art by more than 4.1 mIoU across multiple CD-FSS benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉基础模型在各种视觉任务上取得了强大性能。然而，将VFM应用于跨域小样本分割仍然具有挑战性，该任务需要在域偏移下仅使用少量标注样本对新颖类别的物体进行分割。这一挑战主要来自两个因素：(1) 每个新颖类别有限的标注样本相对于VFM预训练规模而言过少，使得模型在重新训练时容易过拟合；(2) 目标域偏移在预训练中未得到充分体现，导致跨域不一致性和逐层敏感性。为解决这些问题，我们提出分层范式表示适配（HERA），一种三阶段的选择-正则化-校准VFM分割框架，能够从有限标签中有效学习并适应新领域，无需源数据重新训练。我们首先设计分层层级选择，利用为每个候选层计算的基于数据的样本迁移风险自适应识别信息量最大的VFM层。然后，先验引导正则化对所选表示进行交互正则化，为后续阶段生成结构良好的局部信号。此外，像素级自适应校准将所选表示与精炼的交互图相结合以校准逐像素预测，生成一致的掩码。这些阶段共同构成一个分层选择-正则化-校准pipeline，在测试时仅微调不到2.7%的参数即可引导冻结的VFM特征适应新领域。大量实验表明，HERA在多个CD-FSS基准上超越当前最先进方法超过4.1 mIoU。

</details>


### [24] [Semantic-Enriched Latent Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19342)
*Tianrun Xu,Yue Sun,Qixun Wang,Jingyi Lu,Yuan Wang,Tianren Zhang,Longteng Guo,Fengyun Rao,Jing Lyu,Feng Chen,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLVR框架，通过两阶段学习（属性级语义监督 + 多查询组相对策略优化）丰富潜在表示语义，并构建SLV-Set数据集和SV-QA基准，提升了多模态潜在空间推理的鲁棒性和语义一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有潜在空间推理方法依赖视觉监督，学习到的潜在表示语义丰富度不足，难以支持多样化的区域级推理任务。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 在细粒度属性监督下学习语义丰富的区域中心潜在表示；(2) 提出M-GRPO（多查询组相对策略优化）对齐同一区域多个查询的潜在表示。同时构建SLV-Set数据集（约40万区域属性标注+80万多查询问答样本）和SV-QA基准。

Result: 实验表明SLVR相比现有基线方法在潜在视觉推理的鲁棒性和语义一致性上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入属性级语义监督和多查询对齐策略，能够有效增强潜在空间表示的语义丰富度，从而提升区域级推理任务的性能。

Original Abstract: Multimodal latent-space reasoning aims to replace explicit thinking with images by performing visual reasoning directly in a compact latent space. However, existing approaches largely rely on visual supervision and produce latent representations that lack sufficient semantic richness, limiting their ability to support diverse region-level reasoning tasks. In this work, we introduce Semantic-Enriched Latent Visual Reasoning (SLVR), a two-stage learning framework that enriches latent representations with attribute-level visual semantics and aligns them with diverse reasoning objectives. In the first stage, SLVR learns semantically enriched region-centric latents under fine-grained attribute supervision. In the second stage, we design Multi-query Group Relative Policy Optimization (M-GRPO) to align latent representations across multiple queries grounded in the same region. To support this framework, we construct SLV-Set, comprising approximately 400K region-level attribute annotations and 800K multi-query question answering samples, and introduce SV-QA, a benchmark that evaluates latent reasoning under semantic variation. Experiments demonstrate that SLVR improves the robustness and semantic consistency of latent visual reasoning compared to existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态潜在空间推理旨在通过在紧凑的潜在空间中直接进行视觉推理，替代使用图像的显式思考。然而，现有方法主要依赖视觉监督，学习到的潜在表示缺乏足够的语义丰富性，限制了其支持多样化区域级推理任务的能力。在本工作中，我们提出语义增强的潜在视觉推理（SLVR），这是一个两阶段学习框架，用属性级视觉语义丰富潜在表示，并将其与多样化的推理目标对齐。在第一阶段，SLVR在细粒度属性监督下学习语义丰富的区域中心潜在表示。在第二阶段，我们设计多查询组相对策略优化（M-GRPO），以对齐同一区域内多个查询的潜在表示。为支持该框架，我们构建了SLV-Set数据集，包含约40万条区域级属性标注和80万条多查询问答样本，并引入SV-QA基准，用于评估语义变化下的潜在推理能力。实验表明，与现有基线方法相比，SLVR提升了潜在视觉推理的鲁棒性和语义一致性。

</details>


### [25] [MAM-CLIP: Vision-Language Pretraining on Mammography Atlases for BI-RADS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19359)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态对比学习的 mammography 图像分析框架 MAM-CLIP，利用 2313 幅带标注的影像-文本对进行预训练，提升视觉编码器对 BI-RADS 分数的预测能力，在低标注样本场景下 F1 提升最高达 14%，并发现 2K 影像-文本对比 2K 标注样本更具信息量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 乳腺 X 光影像解读存在主观差异，仅依赖图像标签训练分类模型效果有限，因此需要引入文本描述信息来增强模型对影像发现的理解。

Method: 从两个乳腺 X 光图谱中收集 2313 幅影像及其对应文字描述，使用预训练 PubMedBERT 作为语言编码器，通过对比学习在影像-文本对上训练多模态模型，使视觉编码器吸收文本中的丰富信息，然后在两个数据集上微调视觉编码器进行 BI-RADS 分数预测。

Result: 在 3 类平均 F1 分数上提升 +1%（40K 训练样本）至 +14%（1K 训练样本）；2K 影像-文本对比 2K 标注样本在预测任务上平均高出 +1.1%（当训练样本超过 10K 时）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个乳腺 X 光影像-语言模型，证明了来自乳腺 X 光图谱的文本信息在提升预测性能方面的重要价值，并公开了预处理后的 TEKNOFEST 数据集及完整代码。

Original Abstract: Deep learning methods have demonstrated promising results in predicting BI-RADS scores from mammography images. However, the interpretation of these images can vary, leading to discrepancies even among radiologists. Given the inherent complexity of mammograms, training classification models solely on image labels often yields limited performance. To address this challenge, we curated 2313 mammogram images and their corresponding captions from two mammography atlases. Our proposed approach employs a multi-modal model that uses a pretrained PubMedBERT as the language component. By training this model on image-text pairs with contrastive learning, we enable the vision encoder to absorb the rich information contained in the captions, thereby improving its understanding of mammography findings. We then fine-tune the vision encoder on two datasets for BI-RADS prediction, achieving superior performance compared with models trained without this pretraining, particularly when labeled samples are scarce. The improvement in the 3-class average F1 score ranges from +1% to +14%: a +1% increase with 40K training samples, and a +14% increase with 1K samples. Furthermore, our experiments reveal that 2K image-text pairs from mammography atlases can be more informative than 2K labeled samples for label prediction, with an average margin of +1.1% when more than 10K training samples are available. Overall, our work provides a vision-language model for mammography and highlights the value of textual information from mammography atlases. In addition, we publicly release preprocessed mammography images of the TEKNOFEST dataset. The training code, pre-trained model weights, data extraction scripts, and the released dataset are publicly available at: https://github.com/igulluk/MAM-CLIP

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习方法在从乳腺X光影像中预测BI-RADS评分方面已显示出有前景的结果。然而，这些影像的解读可能存在差异，即使在放射科医生之间也会出现不一致。鉴于乳腺X光片的固有复杂性，仅依赖图像标签训练分类模型往往性能有限。为解决这一挑战，我们收集了来自两个乳腺X光图谱的2313幅乳腺X光影像及其对应的文字描述。我们提出的方法采用多模态模型，使用预训练的PubMedBERT作为语言组件。通过在影像-文本对上使用对比学习训练该模型，我们使视觉编码器能够吸收文字描述中包含的丰富信息，从而增强其对乳腺X光发现的理解。随后在两个数据集上对视觉编码器进行BI-RADS预测的微调，相比未经过此预训练的模型取得了更优的性能，尤其在标注样本稀缺时。3类平均F1分数的提升范围为+1%到+14%：在40K训练样本下提升+1%，在1K样本下提升+14%。此外，我们的实验表明，来自乳腺X光图谱的2K影像-文本对在标签预测方面比2K标注样本更具信息量，当训练样本超过10K时平均高出+1.1%。总体而言，本文提供了一个乳腺X光影像-语言模型，并强调了来自乳腺X光图谱的文本信息的价值。此外，我们公开发布了TEKNOFEST数据集的预处理乳腺X光影像。训练代码、预训练模型权重、数据提取脚本以及发布的数据集均可在以下地址公开获取：https://github.com/igulluk/MAM-CLIP

</details>


### [26] [Scalable, Energy-Efficient Optical-Neural Architecture for Multiplexed Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19360)
*Parnian Ghapandar Kashani,Shiqi Chen,Aydogan Ozcan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合数字-模拟深度伪造视频检测框架，通过数字前端结合空间复用光学解码后端，实现单次光学传播同时处理15条以上视频流，在Celeb-DF数据集上达到97.79%的平均检测准确率，兼具高吞吐量、低能耗和对抗鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的深度伪造检测方法依赖计算密集且高能耗的推理算法，限制了其可扩展性，亟需更高效、可信的检测方案。

Method: 提出一种混合数字-模拟深度伪造视频检测框架：采用轻量级数字前端进行初步处理，结合通过可编程空间光调制器实现空间复用光学解码后端，在单次光学传播中并行处理15条以上视频流，实现大规模并行模拟推理。

Result: 在使用可见光谱的空间复用实验装置上，对Celeb-DF视频数据集以单次光学通道并行测试15个视频，平均检测准确率97.79%，敏感性99.86%，特异性95.72%；系统对视频退化、噪声、压缩、实验失准和黑盒对抗攻击均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将光学计算集成到AI推理中，能够在吞吐量、能效和对抗鲁棒性三个方面同时获得提升，而这些特性在纯数字系统中难以兼得。

Original Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-generated visual media has created an urgent need for efficient, trustworthy deepfake detection systems. However, existing deep learning-based detection methods rely on computationally intensive and energy-demanding inference algorithms, limiting their scalability. Here, we present a hybrid digital-analog deepfake video detection framework that combines a lightweight digital front-end with a spatially multiplexed optical decoding back-end for massively parallel analog inference through a programmable spatial light modulator. By simultaneously processing 15 or more video streams within a single optical propagation pass, the system enables high-throughput and accurate video-level authenticity prediction at reduced computational cost compared with purely digital methods. We validated this hybrid deepfake video processor using different datasets spanning classical face-swapping, real-world deepfake recordings, and fully AI-generated videos. Using a spatially multiplexed experimental set-up operating in the visible spectrum, we achieved average deepfake detection accuracy, sensitivity and specificity of 97.79%, 99.86% and 95.72%, respectively, on the Celeb-DF video dataset with 15 videos tested in parallel in a single optical pass per inference. The multiplexed optical decoder also demonstrates resilience against various types of video degradation, noise, compression, experimental misalignments and black-box adversarial attacks. Our results show that integrating optical computation into AI inference enables simultaneous gains in throughput, energy efficiency, and adversarial robustness - three properties that are difficult to achieve together in purely digital systems.

中文摘要翻译: AI生成视觉媒体的快速扩散产生了对高效、可信的深度伪造检测系统的迫切需求。然而，现有的基于深度学习的检测方法依赖于计算密集且能耗高的推理算法，限制了其可扩展性。本文提出了一种混合数字-模拟深度伪造视频检测框架，该框架将轻量级数字前端与通过可编程空间光调制器实现空间复用光学解码后端相结合，用于大规模并行模拟推理。通过在单次光学传播通道中同时处理15条以上视频流，该系统相较于纯数字方法，能够以更低的计算成本实现高吞吐量和准确的视频级真实性预测。我们使用涵盖经典换脸、真实世界深度伪造录像和完全AI生成视频的不同数据集验证了该混合深度伪造视频处理器。在使用可见光谱的空间复用实验装置中，我们在Celeb-DF视频数据集上以单次光学通道每次推理并行测试15个视频，实现了平均深度伪造检测准确率97.79%、敏感性99.86%和特异性95.72%。该复用光学解码器还对各种类型的视频退化、噪声、压缩、实验失准和黑盒对抗攻击表现出鲁棒性。我们的结果表明，将光学计算集成到AI推理中能够在吞吐量、能效和对抗鲁棒性三个方面同时获得提升——而这些特性在纯数字系统中难以兼得。

</details>


### [27] [Multi-Scale Generative Modeling with Heat Dissipation Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19371)
*Jun Ma,Hanquan Zhang,Yanjun Qin,Haoyuan Guan,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出热耗散流匹配（HDFM），将连续模糊（热耗散）过程引入流匹配框架，通过插值热耗散路径解决不适定问题，并采用x预测缓解高维回归困难，在图像生成中优于多数基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于模糊的扩散模型局限于SDE框架，未整合到ODE框架（如流匹配）中；经典逆热耗散过程存在不适定挑战；在数据流形假设下，从高维噪声（或速度）空间回归模糊图像也很困难。

Method: 提出热耗散流匹配（HDFM），将连续模糊（热耗散）过程引入流匹配框架以注入多尺度先验；通过对齐插值热耗散路径解决不适定问题；采用x预测（而非v预测）缓解高维回归难度。

Result: 消融实验表明HDFM同时受益于模糊过程和x预测；在所有数据集上优于大多数基线方法。

Conclusion: HDFM成功将基于模糊的生成过程融入ODE框架，通过插值路径和x预测有效解决了不适定性和高维回归困难，验证了多尺度先验在生成建模中的价值。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models are widely used in image generation, with most relying on noise-based corruption and denoising. A distinct branch instead uses blur as the main corruption, preserving better color budgets and multi-scale detail by providing multi-scale priors. However, blur-based models remain in SDE-based frameworks and are not integrated into ODE-based frameworks, such as Flow Matching (FM). Meanwhile, in the blur-based formulation, the classical inverse heat-dissipation (IHD) process faces an ill-posed challenge. Moreover, under the data-manifold assumption, regressing blurred images from high-dimensional noise (or velocity) space is also difficult. We propose Heat Dissipation Flow Matching (HDFM), which introduces a continuous blurred (heat-dissipation) process into FM to inject multi-scale priors. HDFM aligns an interpolated heat-dissipation path to address ill-posedness and adopts $x$-prediction to mitigate high-dimensional regression difficulty. Toy experiments and ablation studies show that HDFM consistently benefits from both blur and $x$-prediction. The performance of HDFM outperforms most baseline methods on all datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型广泛应用于图像生成，大多数方法依赖于基于噪声的加噪与去噪。一个不同的分支则采用模糊作为主要退化方式，通过提供多尺度先验，更好地保留色彩预算和多尺度细节。然而，基于模糊的模型仍局限于SDE框架，尚未被整合到ODE框架（如流匹配）中。同时，在基于模糊的公式中，经典的逆热耗散过程面临不适定挑战。此外，在数据流形假设下，从高维噪声（或速度）空间回归模糊图像也十分困难。我们提出热耗散流匹配（HDFM），将连续模糊（热耗散）过程引入流匹配中以注入多尺度先验。HDFM通过对齐插值热耗散路径来解决不适定问题，并采用x预测来缓解高维回归困难。玩具实验和消融研究表明，HDFM一致地从模糊过程和x预测中获益。HDFM在所有数据集上的性能均优于大多数基线方法。

</details>


### [28] [Concept-Guided Noisy Negative Suppression for Zero-Shot Classification and Grounding of Chest X-Ray Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19374)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Chun-Ka Wong,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoNNS提出了一种基于层级概念本体论的噪声负对抑制框架，通过细粒度分解、噪声负对过滤和难负样本挖掘三步策略，结合概念感知NCE损失，有效缓解胸部X光片与放射学报告对比学习中的噪声负对问题，在多粒度零样本定位和五个零样本分类数据集上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准对比学习将不同患者的影像与报告简单地视为负对，但不同患者可能具有相似的临床发现，这引入了噪声负对，导致语义模糊并降低零样本理解任务的性能。因此需要一种方法来抑制这些噪声负对。

Method: (1) 利用大语言模型构建层级概念本体论，涵盖41个关键临床概念，显式建模存在性、属性（位置和特征）及文本（证据片段和存在陈述）；(2) 基于该本体论实施跨患者对重标记策略：细粒度分解、噪声负对过滤、难负样本挖掘；(3) 提出概念感知NCE损失，在对齐视觉特征与文本的同时抑制识别出的噪声负对。

Result: 在多粒度零样本定位任务和五个零样本分类数据集上的广泛实验表明，CoNNS优于现有的最先进模型。

Conclusion: 通过构建层级概念本体论和设计噪声负对抑制机制，CoNNS有效解决了对比学习中的语义歧义问题，显著提升了胸部X光片零样本理解性能。

Original Abstract: Vision-language alignment using chest X-rays and radiology reports has emerged as an advanced paradigm for zero-shot classification and grounding of chest X-ray findings. However, standard contrastive learning typically treats radiographs and reports from different patients simply as negative pairs. This assumption introduces noisy negatives, as different patients frequently exhibit similar findings. Such noisy negatives cause semantic ambiguity and degrade performance in zero-shot understanding tasks. To address this challenge, we propose CoNNS, a concept-guided noisy-negative suppression framework. To support the negative suppression mechanism, unlike previous methods that use raw reports or templatized texts, we construct a hierarchical concept ontology using large language models. The ontology structures 41 key clinical concepts by explicitly modeling presence, attributes (location and characteristics), and texts (evidential segment and presence statement). Leveraging this ontology, we implement a cross-patient pair relabeling strategy comprising three steps: (1) Fine-Grained Breakdown to categorize pairs based on finding presence; (2) Noisy Negative Filtering to resolve semantic conflicts by removing false negatives; and (3) Hard Negative Mining to identify subtle attribute discrepancies using a lightweight language model. Finally, we propose a Concept-Aware NCE loss to align visual features with text while suppressing the identified noisy negatives. Extensive experiments across multi-granularity zero-shot grounding tasks and five zero-shot classification datasets validate that CoNNS outperforms existing state-of-the-art models. The code is available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/conns.

中文摘要翻译: 利用胸部X光片和放射学报告进行视觉-语言对齐，已成为零样本分类和胸部X光发现定位的前沿范式。然而，标准对比学习通常将不同患者的影像与报告简单地视为负对。这一假设引入了噪声负对，因为不同患者常常表现出相似的发现。这些噪声负对导致语义模糊，并降低了零样本理解任务的性能。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了CoNNS——一种概念引导的噪声负对抑制框架。为支持负对抑制机制，不同于以往使用原始报告或模板化文本的方法，我们利用大语言模型构建了一个层级概念本体论。该本体论通过对存在性、属性（位置和特征）以及文本（证据片段和存在陈述）进行显式建模，结构化了41个关键临床概念。基于这一本体论，我们实施了一种跨患者对重标记策略，包含三个步骤：(1) 细粒度分解——根据发现的存在性对配对进行分类；(2) 噪声负对过滤——通过移除假负对来解决语义冲突；(3) 难负样本挖掘——使用轻量级语言模型识别细微的属性差异。最后，我们提出了一种概念感知NCE损失，在对齐视觉特征与文本的同时抑制已识别的噪声负对。跨多粒度零样本定位任务以及五个零样本分类数据集的广泛实验验证了CoNNS优于现有的最先进模型。代码可在 https://github.com/DopamineLcy/conns 获取。

</details>


### [29] [MatPhys: Learning Material-Aware Physics Parameters for Deformable Object Simulation from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19386)
*Yang Yang,Yiyan Wang,Zheming Liu,Naoya Iwamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MatPhys，一种材料感知的前馈框架，从单目视频中预测弹簧-质点参数，通过语义分解和材料码本实现异质材料建模和跨场景一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于物理驱动的可变形物体重建方法存在两个根本局限：1）通常假设物体材质是均匀的；2）场景特定的逆优化导致同一材料在不同场景或交互中参数不一致。

Method: 1）利用DINO特征将物体分解为语义上有意义的部件并查询部件级材料先验，为每个部件分配独立物理行为；2）引入可学习的材料码本作为外观与物理之间的桥梁，并结合部件级先验作为参考分布约束解码器，使相同材料在不同场景下输出一致参数。

Result: 与每场景优化基线在重建和未来预测任务上性能相当，且在未见交互和物体的泛化能力以及物理参数一致性上更强。

Conclusion: 将欠约束单目问题转化为基于共享、可复用材料概念的前馈推理，有效提升了可变形物体物理参数估计的准确性和一致性。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing simulation-ready deformable objects is important for vision, graphics, and robotics. Existing physics-driven methods can recover physical digital twins from videos, but they suffer from two fundamental limitations: they typically assume a homogeneous material across the whole object, and their scene-specific inverse optimization, combined with the inherent ambiguity of monocular observation, yields inconsistent parameters for the same material across different scenes or interactions. We propose MatPhys, a material-aware feed-forward framework that predicts spring-mass parameters from a single-view video, addressing these two issues with two coupled designs. To relax the homogeneous material assumption, we use DINO features to decompose the object into semantically meaningful parts and to query a part-level material prior, assigning each part its own physical behavior. To enforce cross-scene consistency, we introduce a learned material codebook of shared material embeddings as the bridge between appearance and physics, and further use the part-level prior as a reference distribution that constrains the decoder so that the same material yields consistent parameters across scenes and interactions. Together, these designs turn an under-constrained monocular problem into feed-forward inference grounded on shared, reusable material concepts. Experiments show that our method matches per-scene optimization baselines in reconstruction and future prediction, while achieving stronger generalization to unseen interactions and objects with more consistent physical parameters.

中文摘要翻译: 重建可支持仿真的可变形物体对于视觉、图形学和机器人学至关重要。现有的基于物理驱动的方法可以从视频中恢复物理数字孪生，但它们存在两个根本局限：它们通常假设整个物体具有均匀的材料属性，并且其场景特定的逆优化结合单目观测的固有模糊性，导致同一材料在不同场景或交互中参数不一致。我们提出MatPhys，一种材料感知的前馈框架，能够从单目视频中预测弹簧-质点参数，通过两个耦合设计解决上述问题。为放宽均匀材料假设，我们使用DINO特征将物体分解为语义上有意义的部件，并查询部件级材料先验，为每个部件分配其自身的物理行为。为强制实现跨场景一致性，我们引入一个可学习的材料码本，其中包含共享的材料嵌入，作为外观与物理之间的桥梁，并进一步使用部件级先验作为参考分布来约束解码器，使得相同材料在不同场景和交互中产生一致的参数。这些设计共同将欠约束的单目问题转化为基于共享、可复用材料概念的前馈推理。实验表明，我们的方法在重建和未来预测任务上与每场景优化基线相当，同时在未见交互和物体的泛化能力以及更一致的物理参数方面表现更强。

</details>


### [30] [LMM-Track4D: Eliciting 4D Dynamic Reasoning in LMMs via Trajectory-Grounded Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19390)
*Chaoyue Li,Yongxue Xu,Jie Feng,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹锚定的多轮时空对话任务，构建Track4D-Bench基准，并设计LMM-Track4D模型（含RTGE、TRK令牌和OSK-RA解码器）以增强大模态模型在4D时空动态推理上的能力。实验表明显式动态状态建模是提升LMMs 4D推理的有效设计原则。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大模态模型（LMMs）在图像和视频理解上能力日益增强，但在持续的4D时空动态推理方面仍存在明显不足。为系统研究这一能力差距，需要定义新的任务和基准。

Method: 提出LMM-Track4D模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) RTGE（Ray-Time Geometry Encoding，射线-时间几何编码）；(2) 专用流式状态令牌TRK用于长程动态传播；(3) Object-Slot Kinematic, Residual-Anchor (OSK-RA) 解码器，用于在遮挡和视角变化下进行稳定的4步3D状态估计。

Result: 在Track4D-Bench基准上的实验表明，LMM-Track4D相较于强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式动态状态建模是激发LMMs 4D动态推理能力的有用设计原则。

Original Abstract: Recent large multimodal models (LMMs) have become increasingly capable on image and video understanding, yet still struggle to sustain 4D continuous spatiotemporal dynamic reasoning. To study this capability gap, we formulate trajectory-grounded multi-turn spatiotemporal dialogue, a new task in which a model must answer spatiotemporal queries while returning structured 3D target trajectories over an entire short clip or a specified segment of a longer clip, and introduce Track4D-Bench, a benchmark with 526 clip-level dialogue samples spanning 23.5k frames and 7.5k object annotations, for training and evaluation.
