### [61] [GeoMamba: A Geometry-driven MambaVision Framework and Dataset for Fine-grained Optical-SAR Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19734)
*Tiantong Fang,Xiuwei Wang,Jing Xiao,Wujie Zhou,Liang Liao,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMamba提出了一种几何驱动框架，针对未对齐光学-SAR图像的跨模态细粒度检索问题，通过几何特征注入、几何一致性约束和深度监督策略，在自建FGOS-as数据集上达到63.3% mAP和77.0% Rank-1准确率，显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际场景中光学与SAR图像存在严重的模态差异、散斑噪声和结构不一致问题，且样本通常未配对或空间未对齐，导致传统跨模态检索方法难以学习鲁棒的共享表征。

Method: 提出GeoMamba框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 几何特征注入（GFI）模块，增强跨模态特征交互并引入结构先验；(2) 几何一致性约束（GCC）模块，结合深度监督（DS）策略，利用经典算子施加分层几何约束，保留目标结构信息。

Result: 在自建FGOS-as数据集（11类航空航天与海洋目标）上，全检索模式下mAP达63.3%，Rank-1准确率达77.0%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GeoMamba通过显式引入几何先验和分层约束，有效缓解了光学-SAR跨模态检索中的模态差异与结构不一致问题，为未对齐遥感图像检索提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Multi-source remote sensing enables complementary observation of ground objects, while cross-modal fine-grained object retrieval remains challenging, especially under unaligned optical and SAR conditions. Unlike conventional retrieval settings that rely on paired or spatially aligned samples, practical optical-SAR retrieval is affected by substantial modality discrepancy, speckle noise, and structural inconsistency, which limit robust cross-modal representation learning. To address this problem, we propose GeoMamba, a geometry-driven framework tailored for optical-SAR fine-grained retrieval. Specifically, GeoMamba introduces a Geometric Feature Injection (GFI) module that enhances cross-modal feature interaction and incorporates structural priors, thereby improving the robustness of SAR representations and promoting geometry-consistent feature learning. In addition, a Geometric Consistency Constraint (GCC) module, together with a Deep Supervision (DS) strategy, imposes hierarchical geometric constraints using classical operators, which helps preserve informative object structures during representation learning. We further construct a new dataset, FGOS-as, containing 11 aerospace and maritime categories for evaluating unaligned cross-modal fine-grained object retrieval in realistic remote sensing scenarios. Extensive experiments on FGOS-as demonstrate that GeoMamba outperforms existing methods, achieving 63.3% mAP and 77.0% Rank-1 accuracy in all-to-all retrieval setting.

中文摘要翻译: 多源遥感技术能够实现对地物的互补观测，但跨模态细粒度目标检索仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在未对齐的光学与SAR条件下。与依赖配对或空间对齐样本的传统检索设置不同，实际的光学-SAR检索受到显著的模态差异、散斑噪声和结构不一致的影响，限制了鲁棒的跨模态表征学习。为解决这一问题，我们提出GeoMamba，一种专为光学-SAR细粒度检索设计的几何驱动框架。具体而言，GeoMamba引入了几何特征注入（GFI）模块，该模块增强跨模态特征交互并引入结构先验，从而提升SAR表征的鲁棒性并促进几何一致的 feature 学习。此外，几何一致性约束（GCC）模块结合深度监督（DS）策略，利用经典算子施加分层几何约束，有助于在表征学习过程中保留信息丰富的目标结构。我们进一步构建了新数据集FGOS-as，包含11个航空航天与海洋类别，用于评估真实遥感场景中未对齐的跨模态细粒度目标检索。在FGOS-as上的大量实验表明，GeoMamba优于现有方法，在全检索模式下实现了63.3%的mAP和77.0%的Rank-1准确率。

</details>


### [62] [FlowErase-RL: Rethinking Concept Erasure as Reward Optimization in Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19739)
*Yi Sun,Zhiqi Zhang,Xinhao Zhong,Yimin Zhou,Shuoyang Sun,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowErase-RL 是首个基于 GRPO 的流匹配模型概念擦除框架，通过动态双路径奖励机制（概念擦除奖励+非目标空间奖励）联合优化，在抑制目标概念的同时保持生成保真度，在多个擦除任务上达到 SOTA 性能，且能抵抗对抗攻击并扩展到多概念场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流匹配模型在文本到图像生成中面临安全风险，而现有概念擦除方法要么是推理时干预（效果有限），要么依赖监督微调（需要精确对齐数据，难以扩展和多概念设置），因此需要一种无需显式监督、可扩展且高效的概念擦除方法。

Method: 提出 FlowErase-RL 框架，将概念擦除重述为奖励优化问题，引入动态双路径奖励机制：(i) 概念擦除（CE）奖励用于抑制目标概念，(ii) 非目标空间（NS）奖励用于保持生成保真度。两条路径通过性能驱动的切换策略在训练中自适应平衡，实现无需显式监督的稳定优化。

Result: 在裸露、物体和艺术风格擦除任务上，该方法实现了最先进的擦除性能，同时保持强图像质量和语义对齐；具备鲁棒的对抗攻击抵抗力，并能有效扩展到多概念场景。

Conclusion: FlowErase-RL 为流匹配模型中的安全可控生成建立了新范式，通过强化学习奖励优化方式有效解决了概念擦除问题。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in flow matching models have significantly improved text-to-image generation quality, but also introduce growing safety risks due to the generation of harmful or undesirable content. Existing concept erasure methods are either inference-time interventions with limited effectiveness or rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which requires precisely aligned data and struggles with scalability and multi-concept settings. In this paper, we propose \emph{FlowErase-RL}, the first GRPO-based framework for concept erasure in flow matching models. We reformulate concept erasure as a reward optimization problem and introduce a \textbf{dynamic dual-path reward mechanism} that jointly optimizes (i) a Concept Erasure (CE) reward to suppress target concepts and (ii) a Non-target Space (NS) reward to preserve generative fidelity. The two reward paths are adaptively balanced during training via a performance-driven switching strategy, enabling stable optimization without explicit supervision. Extensive experiments on nudity, object, and artistic style erasure demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art erasure performance while maintaining strong image quality and semantic alignment. Moreover, it exhibits robust resistance to adversarial attacks and scales effectively to multi-concept scenarios. Our results establish a new paradigm for safe and controllable generation in flow matching models.

中文摘要翻译: 近期流匹配模型的进展显著提升了文本到图像生成的质量，但也因生成有害或不期望的内容而带来了日益严峻的安全风险。现有的概念擦除方法要么是效果有限的推理时干预，要么依赖监督微调（SFT），后者需要精确对齐的数据，且难以应对可扩展性和多概念场景。本文提出 FlowErase-RL，这是首个基于 GRPO 的流匹配模型概念擦除框架。我们将概念擦除重述为奖励优化问题，并引入一种**动态双路径奖励机制**，该机制联合优化：(i) 抑制目标概念的概念擦除（CE）奖励，以及 (ii) 保持生成保真度的非目标空间（NS）奖励。两条奖励路径通过一种性能驱动的切换策略在训练过程中自适应平衡，从而实现无需显式监督的稳定优化。在裸露、物体和艺术风格擦除任务上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在保持强大图像质量和语义对齐的同时，实现了最先进的擦除性能。此外，该方法还展现出对对抗攻击的鲁棒抵抗能力，并能有效扩展至多概念场景。我们的结果为流匹配模型中的安全可控生成建立了一种新范式。

</details>


### [63] [Real-World On-Vehicle Evaluation of Embedding-Based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19744)
*Albert Schotschneider,Daniel Bogdoll,Svetlana Pavlitska,Ahmed Abouelazm,Johann Marius Zoellner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预训练ViT嵌入和最近邻相似度的实时异常检测方法，仅需单张参考图像即可建模常态，无需监督训练即可实现像素级异常定位，在Road Anomaly基准上表现良好，并在真实自动驾驶场景中验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有交通场景异常检测方法高度专化，依赖于Cityscapes抽象语义类别定义的常态，难以适应多样化真实场景；同时收集代表性异常数据成本高昂。

Method: 利用预训练视觉Transformer（ViT）作为基础模型提取语义嵌入，通过逐块（patch-wise）处理在潜在语义特征空间中计算与单张参考图像的最近邻相似度，生成稠密异常掩码实现异常定位，无需显式监督和数据集特定训练。

Result: 在Road Anomaly基准上取得良好性能，并在真实自动驾驶车辆上表现出一致的定性行为，成功突出多样场景中的语义异常物体。

Conclusion: 简单、基于参考的方法在实际运行条件下能够提供有效的异常信号，适用于真实世界的自动驾驶部署。

Original Abstract: Detecting anomalies in traffic scenes is crucial for ensuring safety in autonomous driving, yet collecting representative anomalous data remains challenging. Existing anomaly detection methods are highly specialized and rely on normality as defined by the abstract semantic Cityscapes classes, making it difficult to adapt to diverse real-world scenarios. We propose an adaptable real-time anomaly detection method that leverages foundation models in the form of pretrained vision transformer embeddings to detect deviations via nearest-neighbor similarity in the latent semantic feature space. Based on patch-wise processing, the algorithm produces dense anomaly masks, allowing for the localization of detected anomalies. The method robustly models normality through a single reference image. This formulation avoids explicit supervision and dataset-specific training, making it suitable for real-world deployment. We evaluate the method on standard benchmarks and on an automated vehicle in real-world scenarios. Despite its simplicity, the method achieves good performance on the Road Anomaly benchmark and demonstrates consistent qualitative behavior in practice, successfully highlighting semantically unusual objects in diverse scenes. These results suggest that simple, reference-based methods can provide useful anomaly signals under realistic operating conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 检测交通场景中的异常对于确保自动驾驶安全性至关重要，然而收集具有代表性的异常数据仍然具有挑战性。现有的异常检测方法高度专化，且依赖于抽象语义Cityscapes类别所定义的常态，难以适应多样化的真实场景。我们提出了一种可自适应的实时异常检测方法，该方法利用预训练视觉Transformer嵌入形式的基础模型，通过潜在语义特征空间中的最近邻相似度来检测偏差。基于逐块处理，该算法生成稠密异常掩码，从而实现对检测到的异常进行定位。该方法通过单张参考图像即可稳健地建模常态。这一形式避免了显式监督和数据集特定训练，使其适用于真实世界部署。我们在标准基准测试和真实场景中的自动驾驶车辆上对该方法进行了评估。尽管方法简单，它在Road Anomaly基准上取得了良好性能，并在实践中表现出一致的定性行为，成功突出多样场景中语义异常的物体。这些结果表明，简单、基于参考的方法在实际运行条件下能够提供有用的异常信号。

</details>


### [64] [CPC-VAR:Continual Personalized and Compositional Generation in Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19750)
*Junhao Li,Xinhao Zhong,Yi sun,Yuxia Qiao,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉自回归（VAR）模型在持续个性化生成中的两大挑战（灾难性遗忘和概念纠缠），提出了一个统一框架，包含基于梯度的概念神经元选择（GCNS）用于持续单概念学习，以及上下文感知的组合策略用于多概念合成，实验证明了方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于VAR模型的个性化生成方法局限于静态设置，无法适应不断变化的用户需求：顺序学习多个概念时存在严重的灾难性遗忘，多概念合成时存在特征纠缠和属性不一致问题。因此需要首次系统研究VAR模型中的持续个性化生成。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 持续单概念学习：基于梯度的概念神经元选择（GCNS），识别与概念相关的神经元并仅约束任务间冲突的参数，无需额外模型扩展即可缓解遗忘；(2) 多概念合成：上下文感知的组合策略，进行多分支特征建模和由空间条件引导的局部交叉注意力融合，实现精确且解耦的概念组合。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在长序列持续个性化任务中显著提升了性能，在多概念图像合成方面相比现有基线取得了更优的结果。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了VAR模型在可扩展且可控的个性化生成方面的潜力，为持续个性化生成提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Visual autoregressive (VAR) models have recently emerged as an efficient paradigm for text-to-image generation. Despite their strong generative capability, existing VAR-based personalization methods remain limited to static settings, failing to accommodate evolving user demands. In particular, sequential concept learning leads to severe catastrophic forgetting, while multi-concept synthesis often suffers from feature entanglement and attribute inconsistency. In this work, we present the first systematic study of continual personalized generation in VAR models. We identify two key challenges: (i) preserving previously learned concepts during sequential customization, and (ii) composing multiple personalized concepts in a controllable manner. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework with two core components. For continual single-concept learning, we introduce Gradient-based Concept Neuron Selection (GCNS), which identifies concept-relevant neurons and constrains only conflicting parameters across tasks, effectively mitigating forgetting without additional model expansion. For multi-concept synthesis, we propose a context-aware composition strategy that performs multi-branch feature modeling and localized cross-attention fusion guided by spatial conditions, enabling precise and disentangled concept composition. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly improves performance in long-sequence continual personalization while achieving superior results in multi-concept image synthesis compared to existing baselines. These findings highlight the potential of VAR models for scalable and controllable personalized generation.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉自回归（VAR）模型最近已成为文本到图像生成的一种高效范式。尽管其生成能力强，但现有基于VAR的个性化生成方法仍局限于静态设置，无法适应不断变化的用户需求。具体而言，顺序概念学习会导致严重的灾难性遗忘，而多概念合成常常面临特征纠缠和属性不一致的问题。本文首次对VAR模型中的持续个性化生成进行了系统性研究。我们识别出两个关键挑战：(i) 在顺序定制过程中保持先前学习的概念，以及(ii) 以可控方式组合多个个性化概念。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一个包含两个核心组件的统一框架。对于持续单概念学习，我们引入了基于梯度的概念神经元选择（GCNS），该方法识别概念相关神经元并仅约束跨任务中的冲突参数，从而在不进行额外模型扩展的情况下有效缓解遗忘。对于多概念合成，我们提出了一种上下文感知的组合策略，该策略执行多分支特征建模和由空间条件引导的局部交叉注意力融合，从而实现精确且解耦的概念组合。大量实验表明，与现有基线相比，我们的方法在长序列持续个性化任务中显著提升了性能，同时在多概念图像合成方面取得了更优的结果。这些发现揭示了VAR模型在可扩展且可控的个性化生成方面的潜力。

</details>


### [65] [Preferences Order, Ratings Anchor: From Fused Expert Aesthetic Ground Truth to Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19776)
*Yuanpei Zhao,Jie Lin,Chao Zhang,Yilin Wang,Mao Li,Chenhui Li,Jie Hou,Tangjie Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了匹配双协议图像美学评估基准PPaint，揭示成对偏好与点式评分的互补性，并提出PSDistill方法将偏好信号转化为校准伪分数，仅用单类别训练即实现SOTA跨域美学评分性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像美学评估基准仅采用成对偏好或点式评分单一协议，缺乏在受控条件下对两者互补性的定量测量，无法充分利用两类标注信号的优势。

Method: 1) 构建PPaint基准：15位领域专家分5个类别对150幅中国画在五个美学维度进行双协议标注，收集45,900个成对偏好判断和匹配评分。2) 提出PSDistill方法：利用Elo参考池将VLM的成对判断转化为校准伪分数，通过置信度加权排序优化训练同一VLM，使其成为单遍美学评分器。

Result: 1) 偏好信号产生更一致的序数排名，评分信号锚定绝对分数尺度。2) 融合两种信号得到的专家真值两种构造方式几乎一致。3) PSDistill训练的Qwen3-VL-8B平均SRCC从0.504提升至0.709，超越所有开源基线（包括专用美学模型ArtiMuse），以单遍推理成本匹配闭源Gemini-3.1-Pro（差0.04 SRCC），并在APDDv2上验证了跨域迁移能力。

Conclusion: 成对偏好与点式评分在图像美学评估中具有互补优势，通过偏好到分数的转化可以有效融合两者信号；基于此原理的PSDistill方法能够仅用单类别训练数据显著提升VLM的美学评分能力，达到甚至接近闭源模型水平。

Original Abstract: Pairwise preferences and pointwise ratings are the two dominant annotation protocols in image aesthetic assessment (IAA), yet existing benchmarks adopt only one, leaving their complementarity unmeasured under controlled conditions. We introduce PPaint, a matched dual-protocol benchmark in which 15 domain experts, 5 per category, annotate 150 Chinese paintings under both protocols across five aesthetic dimensions, collecting 45,900 pairwise expert judgments through a locally dense preference design alongside the matched ratings. The matched design reveals complementary strengths: preferences yield more consistent ordinal rankings, while ratings anchor the absolute score scale. Fusing both signals via two independent preference-to-score methods yields a fused expert ground truth on which the two constructions converge to nearly identical scores. The same preference-to-score principle extends to label-free VLM training. PSDistill converts VLM pairwise judgments into calibrated pseudo-scores via an Elo reference pool, and trains the same VLM with confidence-weighted ranking optimization to produce a single-pass aesthetic scorer. Trained on a single painting category, the distilled Qwen3-VL-8B improves mean SRCC from 0.504 to 0.709 across all three categories, outperforming all open-source baselines including the dedicated aesthetic model ArtiMuse and matching closed-source Gemini-3.1-Pro within 0.04 SRCC at single-pass inference cost, with cross-domain transfer further validated on APDDv2. We will release the full PPaint dataset and training code.

中文摘要翻译: 成对偏好和点式评分是图像美学评估（IAA）中两种主流的标注协议，然而现有基准仅采用其中一种，导致在受控条件下无法衡量它们的互补性。我们提出PPaint，一个匹配的双协议基准，其中15位领域专家（每个类别5位）在五个美学维度上对150幅中国画进行双协议标注，通过局部密集偏好设计收集了45,900个成对专家判断，同时获得匹配的评分。该匹配设计揭示了互补优势：偏好产生更一致的序数排名，而评分锚定了绝对分数尺度。通过两种独立的偏好到分数方法融合两种信号，得到融合后的专家真值，两种构造方式收敛到几乎相同的分数。同样的偏好到分数原理可扩展至无标签的VLM训练。PSDistill通过Elo参考池将VLM成对判断转化为校准的伪分数，并通过置信度加权排序优化训练同一VLM，使其成为单遍美学评分器。在单一绘画类别上训练的蒸馏Qwen3-VL-8B，在所有三个类别上的平均SRCC从0.504提升至0.709，超越了所有开源基线，包括专用美学模型ArtiMuse，并以单遍推理成本匹配闭源Gemini-3.1-Pro（相差0.04 SRCC），跨域迁移能力在APDDv2上得到进一步验证。我们将发布完整的PPaint数据集和训练代码。

</details>


### [66] [When Preference Labels Fall Short: Aligning Diffusion Models from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19839)
*Weiyan Chen,Weijian Deng,Yao Xiao,Weijie Tu,ZiYi Dong,Ibrahim Radwan,Liang Lin,Pengxu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究将真实数据作为偏好对齐的监督源，通过对比真实图像与生成/扰动样本构建偏好信号，无需人工标注，在扩散模型对齐中取得与现有偏好方法相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有偏好对齐方法依赖模型生成图像构成的偏好对，这种相对监督在双方图像均存在伪影时具有歧义性，难以确定真正理想的输出。因此，本文探索真实数据能否作为偏好对齐的替代监督源。

Method: 采用数据中心的视角，将真实图像作为参考点，通过对比真实图像与生成样本或扰动样本来构建偏好信号，无需人工标注偏好对，以此指导扩散模型的对齐。

Result: 实验分析表明，基于真实数据的监督能够为扩散模型对齐提供有效指导，其性能与现有基于偏好的方法相当。

Conclusion: 真实数据为偏好对齐提供了一种实用且互补的监督来源，为标签高效的偏好对齐策略指明了方向。

Original Abstract: Preference alignment aims to guide generative models by learning from comparisons between preferred and non-preferred samples. In practice, most existing approaches rely on preference pairs constructed from model-generated images. Such supervision is inherently relative and can be ambiguous when both samples exhibit artifacts or limited visual quality, making it difficult to infer what constitutes a truly desirable output. In this work, we investigate whether real data can serve as an alternative source of supervision for preference alignment. We adopt a data-centric perspective and study a curation strategy that treats real images as reference points and constructs preference signals by contrasting them with generated or perturbed samples, without requiring manually annotated preference pairs. Through empirical analysis, we show that real-data-based supervision provides effective guidance for aligning diffusion models and achieves performance comparable to existing preference-based methods. Our results suggest that real data offers a practical and complementary source of supervision for preference alignment and highlight directions of label-efficient alignment strategies. Code and models are available at https://cwyxx.github.io/RealAlign.

中文摘要翻译: 偏好对齐旨在通过从偏好样本和非偏好样本之间的比较中学习来指导生成模型。在实践中，大多数现有方法依赖于由模型生成的图像构建的偏好对。这种监督本质上是相对的，当两个样本都存在伪影或有限的视觉质量时，可能产生歧义，从而难以推断真正理想的输出。在本文中，我们研究了真实数据能否作为偏好对齐的替代监督源。我们采用数据中心的视角，研究了一种以真实图像为参考点，通过将其与生成样本或扰动样本进行对比来构建偏好信号的策展策略，无需人工标注的偏好对。通过实证分析，我们表明基于真实数据的监督为扩散模型对齐提供了有效的指导，并取得了与现有基于偏好的方法相当的性能。我们的结果表明，真实数据为偏好对齐提供了一种实用且互补的监督来源，并突出了标签高效对齐策略的发展方向。代码和模型可在 https://cwyxx.github.io/RealAlign 获取。

</details>


### [67] [FineBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Vision-Language Models for Fine-grained Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19846)
*Gueter Josmy Faure,Min-Hung Chen,Jia-Fong Yeh,Hung-Ting Su,Winston H. Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出专为细粒度人类中心视频理解设计的VQA基准FineBench（包含199,420个问答对和64段长视频），并针对开源VLM在该基准上表现不佳的问题，提出模块化框架FineAgent（由Localizer和Descriptor组成），显著提升开源VLM在细粒度人机交互理解上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在通用视频理解上表现出色，但在需要精细理解人类动作和交互的真实应用中存在不足；已有的以人为中心的基准要么缺乏长视频、要么缺少密集QA覆盖或帧级时空定位，无法全面评估细粒度理解能力。

Method: 构建FineBench基准：从64段15分钟长视频中密集标注199,420个多项选择QA对，聚焦人物运动、人物交互和物体操作（包括组合动作）。提出FineAgent模块化框架：通过Localizer（定位器）和Descriptor（描述器）增强开源VLM的细粒度推理能力。

Result: GPT-5等闭源模型表现尚可，但当前开源VLM显著落后，尤其在多人物场景的空间推理和细微动作差异区分上困难重重。FineAgent能持续提升多种开源VLM在FineBench上的性能。

Conclusion: FineBench为未来细粒度人类中心视频理解研究提供了严格的测试平台，FineAgent则为增强当前VLM在此类推理能力上提供了实用方案。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in general video understanding, yet they often struggle with the fine-grained comprehension crucial for real-world applications requiring nuanced interpretation of human actions and interactions. While some recent human-centric benchmarks evaluate aspects of model behaviour such as fairness/ethics, emotion perception, and broader human-centric metrics, they do not combine long-form videos, very dense QA coverage, and frame-level spatial/temporal grounding at scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce FineBench, a human-centric video question answering (VQA) benchmark specifically designed to assess fine-grained understanding. FineBench comprises 199,420 multiple-choice QA pairs densely annotated across 64 long-form videos (15 minutes each), focusing on detailed person movement, person interaction, and object manipulation, including compositional actions. Our extensive evaluation reveals that while proprietary models like GPT-5 achieve respectable performance, current open-source VLMs significantly underperform, struggling particularly with spatial reasoning in multi-person scenes and distinguishing subtle differences in human movements and interactions. To address these identified weaknesses, we propose FineAgent, a modular framework that enhances VLMs by leveraging a Localizer and a Descriptor. Experiments show that FineAgent consistently improves the performance of various open VLMs on FineBench. FineBench provides a rigorous testbed for future research into fine-grained human-centric video understanding, while FineAgent offers a practical approach to enhance such reasoning in current VLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在通用视频理解方面展现出了卓越的能力，但在需要精细解读人类动作和交互的真实世界应用中，它们往往难以实现细粒度理解。虽然近期一些以人为中心的基准评估了模型在公平性/伦理、情感感知和更广泛的人类中心指标等方面的行为，但它们并未将长视频、密集的问答覆盖以及大规模的帧级空间/时间定位结合起来。为填补这一空白，我们引入了FineBench，一个专门为评估细粒度理解而设计的人类中心视频问答（VQA）基准。FineBench包含在64段长视频（每段15分钟）上密集标注的199,420个多项选择问答对，重点关注详细的人物运动、人物交互和物体操作（包括组合动作）。我们的广泛评估表明，虽然GPT-5等闭源模型取得了尚可的性能，但当前开源VLM显著落后，特别是在多人物场景中的空间推理以及区分人类动作和交互中的细微差异方面存在困难。为解决这些已识别的弱点，我们提出了FineAgent，一个模块化框架，通过利用定位器（Localizer）和描述器（Descriptor）来增强VLM。实验表明，FineAgent在FineBench上持续提升了多种开源VLM的性能。FineBench为未来细粒度人类中心视频理解的研究提供了严格的测试平台，而FineAgent则为增强当前VLM在此类推理能力上提供了实用的方法。

</details>


### [68] [A Framework for Evaluating Zero-Shot Image Generation in Concept-based Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19855)
*Giacomo Astolfi,Matteo Bianchi,Riccardo Campi,Antonio De Santis,Marco Brambilla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用零样本文本到图像生成模型合成概念数据集用于概念型可解释人工智能的可行性，并通过四项互补分析评估了合成概念与真实概念之间的忠实度，揭示了当前生成模型在该任务中的挑战和局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 概念型XAI方法依赖大量人工标注图像来表征每个概念，限制了其可扩展性。本文旨在探索零样本文本到图像生成模型能否作为合成概念数据集的替代来源，以减少对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 利用零样本T2I生成模型根据预定义提示生成概念图像，并通过四项分析评估合成概念与真实概念的忠实度：(1)概念表示相似性比较；(2)同概念内逐步增大的子集间相似性评估；(3)下游解释任务的性能评估；(4)从测试类图像中移除概念对生成概念解释的影响评估。

Result: 当前T2I生成模型虽有望为概念型XAI提供捷径，但研究发现合成概念与真实概念之间存在明显差距，在使用零样本管道生成的合成数据进行模型分析时存在挑战，并引发了一系列开放性问题。

Conclusion: 零样本T2I生成模型可作为概念型XAI的概念数据来源，但当前模型生成的概念图像在忠实度上仍有不足，无法完全替代真实标注数据，需进一步研究改进。

Original Abstract: Concept-based Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) interprets deep learning models using human-understandable visual features (e.g., textures or object parts) by linking internal representations to class predictions, thereby bridging the gap between low-level image data and high-level semantics. A major challenge, however, is the reliance on large sets of labeled images to represent each concept, which limits scalability. In this work, we investigate the use of zero-shot Text-to-Image (T2I) generative models as a source of synthetic concept datasets for concept-based XAI methods. Specifically, we generate concepts using predefined prompts and evaluate their faithfulness to real ones through four complementary analyses: (1) comparing synthetic vs. real concept images via concept representation similarity; (2) evaluating their intra-similarity by comparing pairs of subsets of the same concept with progressively increasing size; (3) evaluating their performance for downstream explanation tasks using relevant class images; (4) evaluating how removing a concept from tested class images affects explanations of generated concepts. While current T2I generative models promise a shortcut to concept-based XAI, our study highlights challenges and raises open questions about the use of synthetic data generated by zero-shot pipelines in model analyses. The resulting dataset is available at https://github.com/DataSciencePolimi/ZeroShot-T2I-Concepts.

中文摘要翻译: 基于概念的可解释人工智能通过将内部表示与类别预测相关联，利用人类可理解的视觉特征（如纹理或物体部件）来解释深度学习模型，从而弥合底层图像数据与高层语义之间的鸿沟。然而，一个主要挑战是依赖大量标注图像来表征每个概念，这限制了其可扩展性。本文研究了利用零样本文本到图像生成模型作为概念型XAI方法的合成概念数据集来源。具体而言，我们使用预定义提示生成概念，并通过四项互补分析评估其与真实概念的忠实度：(1)通过概念表示相似性比较合成与真实概念图像；(2)通过比较同一概念下逐步增大的子集对来评估其内部相似性；(3)使用相关类别图像评估其在下游解释任务中的性能；(4)评估从测试类别图像中移除某一概念对生成概念解释的影响。尽管当前T2I生成模型有望为概念型XAI提供捷径，但我们的研究揭示了挑战，并引发了关于使用零样本管道生成的合成数据进行模型分析的开放性问题。生成的数据集可在https://github.com/DataSciencePolimi/ZeroShot-T2I-Concepts获取。

</details>


### [69] [Eyes on VLM: Benchmarking Gaze Following and Social Gaze Prediction in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19859)
*Hengfei Wang,Anshul Gupta,Pierre Vuillecard,Jean-Marc Odobez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了EyeVLM评估框架，系统测试了视觉语言模型在视线跟随和社交视线预测两个任务上的能力。结果表明当前VLM在视线理解方面缺乏精确性，虽可通过微调缩小差距，但仍需显著改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型已发展为通用多模态推理器，但在人类视线和注意力理解方面的能力尚未被系统评估。视线理解需要推理物理场景、活动、交互和社会上下文，正好发挥VLM的强项，因此有必要全面评估VLM在此任务上的表现。

Method: 提出了EyeVLM评估框架，从两个维度进行评估：(1) 任务维度——视线跟随（几何与视觉处理）和社交视线预测（社会与关系推理）；(2) 模型维度——零样本设置（多种开源/闭源VLM+不同提示策略）和基于任务特定QA对的微调方法（研究模型规模和数据规模的影响）。使用现有视线理解数据集作为基准，与纯视觉模型进行系统比较。

Result: 当前VLM缺乏精确的视线理解能力；标准训练虽有助于缩小与纯视觉模型的差距，但仍需显著改进。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在视线理解任务上表现不足，无法达到精确理解的水平。虽然通过微调可以部分弥补与纯视觉模型之间的差距，但仍有很大的改进空间。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have rapidly evolved into general-purpose multimodal reasoners with strong zero-shot generalization. In this context, VLMs could greatly benefit the analysis of human gaze and attention, a central task in human behavior understanding that requires reasoning about the physical scene as well as the activity, interactions, and social context. However, the extent to which VLMs can reliably understand human gaze and related attentional behaviors remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present EyeVLM, a systematic evaluation framework for gaze understanding in VLMs across two complementary dimensions: tasks and models. To assess gaze understanding capabilities, we focus on two core tasks. The first, gaze following, i.e., predicting the 2D location where a person is looking, has a geometric and visual processing focus, requiring a precise understanding of the human face, attention direction, 3D scene structure, and spatial grounding of attended targets. The second, social gaze prediction, requires social and relational reasoning over multi-person interactions (e.g., mutual gaze and shared attention), and may benefit more from the LLM semantic reasoning capabilities within VLMs. Regarding models, EyeVLM evaluates these tasks in two ways: a zero-shot setting with a diverse set of state-of-the-art open- and closed-source VLMs, exploring different prompting strategies; and a fine-tuning approach based on task-specific QA pairs, studying the impact of model scale and data scale. As benchmarks, we rely on existing gaze understanding datasets and perform a systematic comparison with state-of-the-art purely visual models. Overall, our results show that current VLMs lack precise gaze understanding capabilities. While standard training helps reduce the gap with visual models, significant improvements are still needed.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）已迅速发展为具备强大零样本泛化能力的通用多模态推理器。在此背景下，VLM有望极大地促进人类视线和注意力分析——这是人类行为理解中的核心任务，需要推理物理场景以及活动、交互和社会上下文。然而，VLM能够在多大程度上可靠地理解人类视线及相关注意行为，尚未得到充分探索。本文提出了EyeVLM，一个针对VLM中视线理解能力的系统化评估框架，涵盖两个互补维度：任务维度和模型维度。为评估视线理解能力，我们聚焦于两个核心任务：第一个任务是视线跟随，即预测一个人正在观看的二维位置，侧重于几何和视觉处理，需要精确理解人脸、注意力方向、三维场景结构以及被关注目标的空间定位。第二个任务是社交视线预测，需要对多人交互进行社会和关系推理（如相互注视和共享注意力），可能更受益于VLM内部的大语言模型语义推理能力。在模型方面，EyeVLM通过两种方式评估这些任务：零样本设置，使用多种最先进的开源和闭源VLM，探索不同的提示策略；以及基于任务特定问答对的微调方法，研究模型规模和数据规模的影响。作为基准，我们依赖现有的视线理解数据集，并与最先进的纯视觉模型进行系统比较。总体而言，我们的结果表明，当前VLM缺乏精确的视线理解能力。虽然标准训练有助于缩小与纯视觉模型的差距，但仍需显著改进。

</details>


### [70] [Landscape-Awareness for Geometric View Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19865)
*Yan-Ting Chen,Hao-Wei Chen,Tsu-Ching Hsiao,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于评分的方法来重塑视角估计的优化景观，解决稀疏视图下因几何歧义导致的非凸损失函数和局部最小值问题，结合扩散模型实现高样本效率的准确两视图估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型（如Zero123）的视角估计方法在稀疏视图下因非凸损失景观存在大量局部最小值，对初始化敏感且依赖暴力多起点策略；几何歧义（如对称性和自相似性）会导致梯度更新被误导至错误视角。

Method: 提出一种基于评分的方法：首先通过评分函数重塑优化景观，引导梯度更新朝向真实视角；然后使用视角条件扩散模型进行细化阶段，提升估计精度。

Result: 实验表明该方法改善了收敛性，减少了对暴力采样的依赖，在保持竞争性精度的同时实现了更高的样本效率。

Conclusion: 通过评分重塑优化景观结合扩散模型细化，能有效克服稀疏视角下的几何歧义问题，实现更鲁棒和样本高效的视角估计。

Original Abstract: Accurate camera viewpoint estimation under sparse-view conditions remains challenging, particularly in two-view scenarios. Recent approaches leverage diffusion models such as Zero123 to synthesize novel views conditioned on relative viewpoint, showing promising results when repurposed for viewpoint estimation via optimization with MSE loss. However, existing methods often suffer from nonconvex loss landscape with numerous local minima, making them sensitive to initialization and reliant on naive multistart strategies. We analyze these optimization challenges and visualize failure cases, showing that geometric ambiguities, such as symmetry and self-similarity, can mislead gradient-based updates toward incorrect viewpoints. To address these limitations, we propose a score-based method that reshapes the optimization landscape to guide updates toward the ground-truth viewpoint, followed by a refinement stage using a viewpoint-conditioned diffusion model. Experiments show that our method improves convergence, reduces reliance on brute-force sampling, and achieves competitive accuracy with higher sample-efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 在稀疏视图条件下，准确的相机视角估计仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在双视图场景中。近期的方法利用扩散模型（如Zero123）合成以相对视角为条件的新视图，并在通过均方误差损失优化以重新用于视角估计时展现出令人期待的结果。然而，现有方法常遭受非凸损失景观的困扰，其中包含大量局部最小值，使其对初始化敏感且依赖于朴素的暴力多起点策略。我们分析了这些优化挑战并可视化失败案例，表明几何歧义（如对称性和自相似性）可能将基于梯度的更新误导至错误视角。为解决这些局限，我们提出了一种基于评分的方法，重塑优化景观以引导更新朝向真实视角，随后使用视角条件扩散模型进行细化阶段。实验表明，我们的方法改善了收敛性，减少了对暴力采样的依赖，并在实现更高样本效率的同时达到了具有竞争力的精度。

</details>


### [71] [Structured Layout Priors for Robust Out-of-Distribution Visual Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19866)
*Peter El Hachem,Ahmed Nassar,A. Said Gurbuz,Christoph Auer,Peter W. J. Staar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出视觉语言模型(VLM)在文档解析中面临一个"两跳瓶颈"——解码器必须先定位布局实体(跳1)再提取内容(跳2)，第一跳失败会导致第二跳崩溃。作者通过在解码器外部使用轻量级RT-DETR检测器预解析布局实体，将其序列化为DocTags词汇注入提示中，在保持全局图像视野的同时消除了两跳瓶颈，在多个基准上取得显著提升（如结构OOD的markdown F1从0.37升至0.92），仅增加15%延迟和74个提示token中位数，且无需修改VLM架构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VLM在端到端解析文档时，面对训练中未见过的布局会频繁失效。作者认为这是由于存在一个"两跳瓶颈"：解码器必须先分类和定位外围的布局实体（跳1），然后才能提取内容（跳2）；当跳1失败时，跳2就会崩溃，导致遗漏、结构畸形或自回归重复。

Method: 在VLM解码器外部预先解决"跳1"问题：使用轻量级RT-DETR检测器检测文档布局实体，将其输出序列化为解析器原生DocTags词汇，连同完整页面图像一起注入到提示中。与先分析后解析（裁剪页面）或纯文本提示先验不同，该方法将先验置于解码器的生成空间中，同时在检测噪声时保留全局图像作为回退。

Result: 在10k页结构OOD基准上，markdown F1从0.37升至0.92；在OmniDocBench中文子集上，表格TEDS从0.01升至0.36；在26k页ViDoRe V3基准上，所有工业领域的无限循环解码故障均降低。开销仅为15%的墙上时钟延迟和74个token中位数。注意力分析揭示了解码器在输出结构时关注注入的布局token、在输出内容时关注图像块的双峰相位转移，证实了两跳瓶颈得到缓解。

Conclusion: 通过在解码器外部预解析布局实体并注入到提示中，可以有效消除VLM文档解析中的两跳瓶颈，显著提升跨布局泛化能力，且无需修改基础VLM架构。模型权重将开源以支持可重复性。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) parse documents end-to-end but frequently break down on layouts unlike those seen in training. We attribute this to a two-hop bottleneck: before the decoder can extract content (Hop 2), it must first classify and localize the enclosing layout entity (Hop 1), and when the first hop fails the second collapses into omissions, malformed structure, or autoregressive repetition. We pre-resolve Hop 1 outside the decoder by running a lightweight RT-DETR detector, serializing its outputs in the parser's native DocTags vocabulary, and injecting them into the prompt alongside the full page image. Unlike analyze-then-parse approaches that crop the page, or prior prompt-level priors written in plain text, our prior shares the decoder's generation space and leaves the global image in view as a fallback when detections are noisy. On a 10k-page structural out-of-distribution benchmark, markdown F1 rises from $0.37$ to $0.92$; on the Chinese subset of OmniDocBench, table TEDS rises from $0.01$ to $0.36$; and on the 26k-page ViDoRe V3 benchmark, infinite-loop decoding failures drop across every industrial domain tested. These gains cost $15\%$ wall-clock latency and a median of $74$ prompt tokens, with no architectural change to the base VLM. An attention-level analysis further reveals a bimodal phase shift in which the decoder attends to injected layout tokens when emitting structure and to image patches when emitting content, consistent with the two-hop bottleneck being alleviated. Model weights will be released to support reproducibility.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型(VLM)能够端到端地解析文档，但在处理训练中未见的布局时经常失效。我们认为这归因于一个"两跳瓶颈"：在解码器能够提取内容（跳2）之前，它必须首先分类并定位外围的布局实体（跳1），而当第一跳失败时，第二跳便会崩溃为遗漏、畸形结构或自回归重复。我们在解码器外部通过运行轻量级RT-DETR检测器预先解决跳1问题，将其输出序列化为解析器原生的DocTags词汇，并与完整页面图像一同注入提示中。与先分析后解析（裁剪页面）的方法，或先前以纯文本形式编写的提示级先验不同，我们的先验共享解码器的生成空间，并在检测存在噪声时保留全局图像作为回退。在一个10k页的结构性分布外基准测试中，markdown F1从0.37提升至0.92；在OmniDocBench的中文子集上，表格TEDS从0.01提升至0.36；在26k页的ViDoRe V3基准测试中，所有测试工业领域的无限循环解码故障均有所下降。这些提升仅带来了15%的墙上时钟延迟和74个提示token的中位数开销，且无需对基础VLM进行任何架构更改。注意力层面的分析进一步揭示了一种双模态相位转移：解码器在生成结构时关注注入的布局token，在生成内容时关注图像块，这与两跳瓶颈得到缓解的结论一致。模型权重将发布以支持可重复性。

</details>


### [72] [WoundFormer: Multi-Scale Spatial Feature Fusion for Multi-Class Wound Tissue Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19868)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Rabin Dulal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WoundFormer，一种基于Transformer的多类伤口组织分割框架，通过空间保留的多尺度聚合头改进分层特征融合，在WoundTissueSeg数据集上达到81.9%的Dice分数，比基线方法高出最多4.3个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习方法多集中于二值分割，无法有效建模伤口组织中异质性的组织成分，因为存在高类内变异性和有限的标注数据。多类伤口组织分割仍然是临床上重要但具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出WoundFormer框架，核心是用一个空间保留的多尺度聚合头替换标准SegFormer解码器，该模块在跨尺度集成时保持特征拓扑结构，并通过卷积融合增强上下文交互，从而改善边界定位和视觉相似组织类别的判别能力。

Result: 在WoundTissueSeg数据集（147张图像，6个组织类别）上，总体Dice分数达到81.9%，比强CNN和Transformer基线高出最多4.3个Dice点，在少数组织类别上也取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模分层空间交互能够增强Transformer在异质性伤口组织分割中的表示能力，为更可靠的定量伤口评估提供支持。

Original Abstract: Chronic wounds such as diabetic foot ulcers and pressure injuries require accurate tissue-level assessment to guide treatment planning and monitor healing progression. While deep learning methods have advanced automated wound analysis, most existing approaches focus on binary segmentation and inadequately model heterogeneous tissue composition due to high intra-class variability and limited annotated data. Multi-class wound tissue segmentation, therefore, remains a challenging and clinically relevant problem. We propose WoundFormer, a transformer-based framework that enhances hierarchical spatial feature fusion for multi-class wound tissue segmentation. Specifically, we replace the standard SegFormer decoder with a spatially-preserving multi-scale aggregation head that maintains feature topology during cross-scale integration and strengthens contextual interactions through convolutional fusion. This design improves boundary localization and discrimination between visually similar tissue categories while preserving transformer efficiency. We evaluate WoundFormer on the WoundTissueSeg dataset (147 images, six tissue classes) and a second benchmark (DFUTissue dataset). The proposed method achieves an overall Dice score of 81.9%, outperforming strong CNN- and transformer-based baselines by up to 4.3 Dice points on the WoundTissueSeg benchmark, with consistent improvements across minority tissue classes. These results indicate that explicit modeling of hierarchical spatial interactions enhances transformer representations for heterogeneous wound tissue segmentation and supports more reliable quantitative wound assessment.

中文摘要翻译: 慢性伤口（如糖尿病足溃疡和压力性损伤）需要精确的组织级评估来指导治疗计划和监测愈合进程。虽然深度学习方法已经推进了自动化伤口分析，但大多数现有方法聚焦于二值分割，由于高类内变异性和有限的标注数据，无法充分建模异质性的组织组成。因此，多类伤口组织分割仍然是一个具有挑战性且临床相关的问题。我们提出WoundFormer，一个基于Transformer的框架，用于增强多类伤口组织分割中的分层空间特征融合。具体来说，我们用一个空间保留的多尺度聚合头替换标准SegFormer解码器，该模块在跨尺度集成期间维持特征拓扑结构，并通过卷积融合增强上下文交互。这种设计改善了边界定位以及视觉相似组织类别之间的判别能力，同时保留Transformer的效率。我们在WoundTissueSeg数据集（147张图像，六个组织类别）和第二个基准数据集（DFUTissue数据集）上评估WoundFormer。所提出的方法在WoundTissueSeg基准上实现了81.9%的总体Dice分数，优于强CNN和Transformer基线方法最多4.3个Dice点，在少数组织类别上也取得一致的改进。这些结果表明，显式建模分层空间交互能够增强Transformer在异质性伤口组织分割中的表示能力，并支持更可靠的定量伤口评估。

</details>


### [73] [Passive Construction Site Safety Monitoring via Persona-Scaffolded Adversarial Chain-of-Thought VLM Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19869)
*Ananth Sriram,Neel Mokaria,Rajveer Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的被动式建筑安全监测流程，利用穿戴式与固定摄像头视频，通过三阶段架构（YOLO11检测、SAM 3分割去重、Qwen3-VL多轮对抗思维链验证）实现 OSHA 违规识别与人体工学风险评分，在12段视频上未经正式大规模评估即观察到12%的精确度提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 建筑行业是美国最致命的行业，现有监测方法成本高、需实时人工操作或仅覆盖少数违规类型，亟需一种低成本、被动式、覆盖全面的自动化安全监测方案。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：(1) 微调 YOLO11 进行主要 PPE 与危险检测；(2) SAM 3 进行分割细化与工人去重；(3) Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 结合方法提示、角色脚手架与三轮对抗思维链协议（生成器-判别器-调和器，不对称规则编码人类观察与自动检测可靠性先验）进行合规验证与幻觉控制。系统映射违规至 OSHA 标准，利用姿态关键点进行 REBA 人体工学风险评分，生成含时间戳证据的工人安全报告。

Result: 在12段视频的 Ironsite 开发集上，非正式的三作者评审表明：方法提示+角色脚手架+三轮对抗思维链相比单轮提示在精确度上提升12%，在易产生幻觉的违规类别上提升最大。

Conclusion: 所提出的被动式端到端安全监测流程在合规验证中有效抑制幻觉并提升精确度，可映射至 OSHA 标准并完成人体工学评分，为建筑安全自动化监测提供了新范式；已发布评估工具以供复现。

Original Abstract: Construction remains the deadliest industry sector in the United States, with 1,055 fatal worker injuries recorded in 2023, and the majority preventable. Existing monitoring approaches are expensive, require real-time human operators, or address only a narrow subset of violations. This paper presents a passive, end-of-shift construction safety monitoring pipeline processing video from POV body-worn and fixed wall-mounted cameras through a three-stage architecture: (1) fine-tuned YOLO11 for primary PPE and hazard detection, (2) SAM 3 for segmentation refinement and worker deduplication, and (3) Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct with a method-prompted, persona-scaffolded three-pass adversarial chain-of-thought protocol for compliance verification and hallucination control. The principal contribution is the Stage 3 prompt design: professional persona backstories following the method-actor framing drive an observed 12% precision improvement over single-pass prompting in an informal three-author review of the 12-video Ironsite development corpus, with the largest gains on hallucination-prone violation categories. Structural message isolation enforces observational independence between a generator, discriminator, and reconciliation pass governed by asymmetric rules encoding priors about human observation versus automated detection reliability. The system maps violations to OSHA standards, performs REBA-inspired ergonomic risk scoring from pose keypoints, and produces per-worker safety reports with timestamped evidence. An evaluation harness is released for future reproduction.

中文摘要翻译: 建筑业仍然是美国最致命的行业，2023年记录了1055起致命工伤事故，其中大多数本可预防。现有的监测方法成本高昂、需要实时人工操作，或者仅覆盖一小部分违规行为。本文提出了一种被动式的、班次结束后的建筑安全监测流程，该流程处理来自随身佩戴式（POV）和固定壁挂式摄像头的视频，采用三阶段架构：（1）微调后的YOLO11用于主要个人防护装备（PPE）与危险检测；（2）SAM 3用于分割细化与工人去重；（3）Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct结合方法提示、角色脚手架式的三轮对抗思维链协议，用于合规验证与幻觉控制。主要贡献在于第三阶段的提示设计：基于方法-行动者框架构建的专业角色背景故事，在12段视频的Ironsite开发集上，经非正式的三作者评审，相比单轮提示观察到12%的精确度提升，其中对易产生幻觉的违规类别提升最大。结构性消息隔离强制执行生成器、判别器与调和轮次之间的观测独立性，并由编码人类观测与自动检测可靠性先验的不对称规则所支配。该系统将违规行为映射至OSHA标准，利用姿态关键点进行REBA启发的人体工学风险评分，并生成带有时间戳证据的每工人安全报告。我们已发布评估工具以供后续复现。

</details>


### [74] [Structural Energy Guidance for View-Consistent Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19876)
*Qing Zhang,Jinguang Tong,Jing Zhang,Jie Hong,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对文本到3D生成中常见的Janus问题，提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的结构能量引导采样框架（SEGS），通过在U-Net特征的PCA子空间中构建结构能量并将其梯度注入去噪过程，有效缓解了多视角不一致问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的文本到3D生成方法常因2D扩散先验中的视角偏差导致Janus问题（多视角几何不一致），亟需一种无需重新训练且能灵活集成到现有SDS/VSD管线中的方法来消除视角伪影。

Method: 在U-Net特征的PCA子空间中构建结构能量（structural energy），并将其梯度注入到扩散模型的去噪采样过程中，以引导生成保持多视角几何一致性；该方法无需重新训练，可即插即用地集成到SDS/VSD等现有管线中。

Result: 在DreamFusion、Magic3D、LucidDreamer等多个基线上，SEGS将Janus率平均降低约10%，并提升了View-CS分数；在有效缓解视角伪影的同时保持了外观保真度。

Conclusion: SEGS是一种灵活、高效的免训练框架，能够显著提升文本到3D生成的多视角一致性，为高质量3D内容生成提供了实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Text-to-3D generation based on diffusion models often suffers from the Janus problem, leading to inconsistent geometry across viewpoints. This work identifies viewpoint bias in 2D diffusion priors as the main cause and proposes Structural Energy-Guided Sampling (SEGS), a training-free and plug-and-play framework to improve multi-view consistency. SEGS constructs a structural energy in the PCA subspace of U-Net features and injects its gradient into the denoising process. It can be easily integrated into SDS/VSD pipelines without retraining. Experiments show that SEGS reduces the Janus Rate by about 10% on average and improves View-CS scores across multiple baselines, including DreamFusion, Magic3D, and LucidDreamer. This method effectively alleviates viewpoint artifacts while preserving appearance fidelity, providing a flexible solution for high-quality text-to-3D content generation.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散模型的文本到3D生成方法常常面临Janus问题，导致不同视角下的几何结构不一致。本工作指出2D扩散先验中的视角偏差是其主要原因，并提出结构能量引导采样（SEGS）——一种无需训练、即插即用的框架，用于提升多视角一致性。SEGS在U-Net特征的PCA子空间中构建结构能量，并将其梯度注入去噪过程。该方法无需重新训练即可轻松集成到SDS/VSD管线中。实验表明，SEGS在多个基线方法（包括DreamFusion、Magic3D和LucidDreamer）上将Janus率平均降低约10%，并提升了View-CS分数。该方法在缓解视角伪影的同时保持了外观保真度，为高质量文本到3D内容生成提供了一种灵活有效的解决方案。

</details>


### [75] [GoTTA be Diverse: Rethinking Memory Policies for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19890)
*Shyma Alhuwaider,Yasmeen Alsaedy,Merey Ramazanova,Silvio Giancola,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了测试时适应（TTA）中的内存管理策略，将内存从适应算法中解耦出来进行基准测试，发现类内多样性是核心设计原则，并提出了多样性感知的内存策略GOTTA，在非独立同分布和资源受限场景下显著提升适应性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TTA研究大多关注适应目标函数，而内存管理（即选择哪些测试样本来驱动适应）作为关键组件却缺乏系统研究，且往往与特定算法耦合，难以分离出哪些内存设计选择真正重要及其适用条件。

Method: 提出了Guided Observational Test-Time Adaptation (GOTTA)，一类多样性感知的内存策略族，将类别均衡分配与特征空间多样性相结合。GOTTA可作为现有缓冲区的即插即用替代品，兼容不同的TTA目标函数。

Result: 在多种损坏基准和视频流场景下，多样性感知的内存策略在受限内存预算和具有挑战性的非独立同分布流中提升最为显著，且在内存容量增大时仍保持竞争力。有效内存管理不仅需要保留近期样本或类别均衡样本，类内多样性是避免冗余缓冲区并维持代表性适应信号的关键因素。

Conclusion: 内存管理应被视为鲁棒测试时适应的一等组件，多样性是实现实际TTA的核心原则。

Original Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) enables a pre-trained model to adapt online to an unlabeled test stream under distribution shift. While most TTA research focuses on the adaptation objective, practical streams also depend critically on the memory used to select which test samples drive adaptation. Existing memory mechanisms are usually evaluated as components of specific TTA algorithms, making it difficult to isolate which memory design choices matter and when they matter. In this work, we provide a systematic benchmark that decouples memory from the adaptation algorithm and evaluates memory policies under unified conditions across i.i.d., non-i.i.d., continual, and practical test streams. Our study shows that effective memory management requires more than retaining recent or class-balanced samples. In particular, intra-class diversity is a key factor for avoiding redundant buffers and maintaining representative adaptation signals under temporally correlated and label-skewed streams. Motivated by this finding, we introduce Guided Observational Test-Time Adaptation (GOTTA), a family of diversity-aware memory policies that combine class-balanced allocation with feature-space diversity. GOTTA memories act as drop-in replacements for existing buffers and can be paired with different TTA objectives. Across corruption benchmarks and video-stream settings, diversity-aware memory improves adaptation most clearly under constrained memory budgets and challenging non-i.i.d. streams, while remaining competitive as memory capacity increases. These results highlight memory management as a first-class component of robust test-time adaptation and identify diversity as a central principle for practical TTA.

中文摘要翻译: 测试时适应（TTA）使预训练模型能够在分布偏移下在线适应未标注的测试流。虽然大多数TTA研究关注适应目标函数，但实际测试流的关键还取决于用于选择哪些测试样本驱动适应的内存。现有的内存机制通常作为特定TTA算法的组成部分进行评估，这使得难以分离出哪些内存设计选择重要以及何时重要。在这项工作中，我们提供了一个系统性的基准，将内存从适应算法中解耦，并在独立同分布、非独立同分布、持续性和实际测试流的统一条件下评估内存策略。我们的研究表明，有效的内存管理需要的不仅仅是保留近期样本或类别均衡样本。特别是，类内多样性是避免冗余缓冲区并在时序相关和标签偏斜流中维持代表性适应信号的关键因素。受此发现启发，我们引入了引导式观测测试时适应（GOTTA），这是一类结合类别均衡分配与特征空间多样性的多样性感知内存策略。GOTTA内存可作为现有缓冲区的即插即用替代品，并与不同的TTA目标函数配对使用。在多种损坏基准和视频流场景中，多样性感知内存在受限内存预算和具有挑战性的非独立同分布流中提升最为显著，且在内存容量增大时仍保持竞争力。这些结果凸显了内存管理作为鲁棒测试时适应的一等组件，并确定了多样性作为实际TTA的核心原则。

</details>


### [76] [Breaking Modality Heterogeneity in Low-Bit Quantization for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19929)
*Yi Zhong,Haotong Qin,Xindong Zhang,Lei Zhang,Guolei Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplitQ提出了一种基于通道分离的通道分离驱动后训练量化框架，通过模态特定异常通道解耦（MOCD）和自适应跨模态校准（ACC）模块，有效解决了视觉-语言模型在多模态量化中因异构激活分布导致的精度下降问题，在W3A3等极端量化设置下仍能保持FP16性能的93.5%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PTQ方法在量化视觉-语言模型时，由于文本和视觉模态的激活分布存在异构性，导致模型精度严重下降。作者发现这种跨模态异构性在通道上分布不均：少量通道集中了大多数模态特定的异常值，且不同模态的异常值位于不同的通道中。现有方法未能有效处理这一问题。

Method: 提出SplitQ框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 模态特定异常通道解耦（MOCD）模块，以最小开销隔离显著的模态特定异常通道；(2) 自适应跨模态校准（ACC）模块，采用双轻量级可学习分支动态缓解模态引起的量化误差。

Result: 在6个流行多模态数据集上，SplitQ在所有评估量化设置（W4A8、W4A4、W3A3、W3A2）下均显著优于现有方法。在W3A3极端量化下，SplitQ保持FP16性能的93.5%（69.5 vs 74.3），突破了高效部署先进VLM的效率边界。

Conclusion: SplitQ通过通道分离策略有效解决了跨模态异构分布带来的量化精度损失，为在资源受限设备上高效部署视觉-语言模型提供了一种新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Low-bit post-training quantization (PTQ) is a pivotal technique for deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTQ methods often degrade VLMs' accuracy due to the heterogeneous activation distributions of text and vision modalities during quantization. We find that this cross-modal heterogeneity is distributed unevenly across channels: a small subset of channels contains most modality-specific outliers, and these outliers typically reside in different channels for each modality. Motivated by this, we propose SplitQ, a channel-Splitting-driven post-training Quantization framework. At its core, SplitQ introduces a novel Modality-specific Outlier Channel Decoupling (MOCD) module that effectively isolates salient modality-specific outlier channels with minimal overhead. To further address the remaining cross-modal distribution discrepancies, we design an Adaptive Cross-Modal Calibration (ACC) module that employs dual lightweight learnable branches to dynamically mitigate modality-induced quantization errors. Extensive experiments on popular VLMs demonstrate that SplitQ significantly outperforms existing approaches across 6 popular multi-modal datasets under all evaluated quantization settings, including W4A8, W4A4, W3A3, and W3A2. Notably, SplitQ preserves 93.5% of FP16 performance under the challenging W3A3 setting (69.5 vs. 74.3), pushing the efficiency frontier for deploying advanced VLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/EMVision-NK/SplitQ

中文摘要翻译: 低比特后训练量化（PTQ）是在资源受限设备上部署视觉-语言模型（VLM）的关键技术。然而，现有的PTQ方法在量化过程中常因文本和视觉模态的异构激活分布而导致VLM精度下降。我们发现这种跨模态异构性在通道上分布不均：少量通道集中了大多数模态特定的异常值，且这些异常值在不同模态中通常位于不同的通道。受此启发，我们提出SplitQ，一种通道分离驱动的后训练量化框架。其核心是引入了一个新颖的模态特定异常通道解耦（MOCD）模块，能够以最小开销有效隔离显著的模态特定异常通道。为进一步解决剩余的跨模态分布差异，我们设计了自适应跨模态校准（ACC）模块，采用双轻量级可学习分支动态缓解模态引起的量化误差。在流行VLM上的大量实验表明，SplitQ在6个流行的多模态数据集上，在所有评估的量化设置（包括W4A8、W4A4、W3A3和W3A2）下均显著优于现有方法。值得注意的是，在具有挑战性的W3A3设置下，SplitQ保持了FP16性能的93.5%（69.5 vs 74.3），推动了先进VLM高效部署的效率边界。我们的代码可在https://github.com/EMVision-NK/SplitQ获取。

</details>


### [77] [StruMPL: Multi-task Dense Regression under Disjoint Partial Supervision and MNAR Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19931)
*Reza M. Asiyabi,Juan Alberto Molina-Valero,The SEOSAW Partnership,Steven Hancock,Casey M. Ryan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StruMPL框架，在多任务密集回归中处理异构不完整监督、MNAR标签和任务间物理约束，在森林生物量估计中显著降低高AGB偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 星载激光雷达提供结构数据但无生物量，地面样地提供生物量但有偏且无结构指标，两类数据源结构不兼容且标签缺失非随机，无法直接联合训练。

Method: 构建共享编码器，分别接入各变量回归头、插补头、倾向性校正头（处理MNAR），以及可学习的物理模块（施加任务间约束）。监督损失使用增强逆概率加权（AIPW）伪结果，并引入倾向性和插补基线的停止梯度。

Result: 在两个生态区上，StruMPL在AGB的RMSE和偏差上优于消融变体和现有方法，分层分析显示AIPW将高AGB偏差降低约54%。

Conclusion: 联合优化具备停止梯度的AIPW损失可恢复IPW加权驻点并保持有界损失，为异构不完整监督下的多任务物理约束回归提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Estimating forest aboveground biomass (AGB) from Earth observation combines two structurally incompatible label sources: spaceborne lidar provides canopy structure at millions of locations but no biomass estimate, and ground-based plots provide biomass at thousands of biased locations but no metrics of structure. No single training sample carries labels for all target variables, plot labels are missing not at random (MNAR), and biomass is linked to the structural variables by known but biome-specific allometric laws. We formalise this as multi-task dense regression under heterogeneous disjoint partial supervision with MNAR labels and inter-task physical constraints, and propose StruMPL to address it jointly. A shared encoder feeds per-variable regression, imputation, and propensity heads for spatial MNAR correction, and a learnable physics module that evaluates the inter-task constraint on the model's own predictions at every pixel. The supervised loss uses an Augmented IPW (AIPW) pseudo-outcome with stop-gradients on the propensity and on the imputation baseline; we show analytically and empirically that both are necessary for joint optimisation to recover IPW-weighted stationary points while keeping the loss bounded. On two ecologically distinct biomes, StruMPL outperforms ablation variants and the closest published method on AGB RMSE and bias, with a stratified analysis showing AIPW reduces high-AGB bias by ~54%.

中文摘要翻译: 从地球观测数据估算森林地上生物量（AGB）面临两个结构不兼容的标签来源：星载激光雷达在数百万个位置提供冠层结构但无生物量估计，地面样地在数千个有偏位置提供生物量但无结构指标。没有任何单一训练样本拥有所有目标变量的标签，样地标签存在非随机缺失（MNAR），而生物量通过已知但依生物群落而异的异速生长定律与结构变量相关联。我们将此形式化为在异构不完整部分监督、MNAR标签和任务间物理约束下的多任务密集回归问题，并提出StruMPL来联合解决它。共享编码器分别接入各变量回归头、插补头和倾向性校正头（用于空间MNAR校正），以及一个可学习的物理模块，该模块在模型自身预测的每个像素上评估任务间约束。监督损失使用增强逆概率加权（AIPW）伪结果，并在倾向性和插补基线上设置停止梯度；我们从分析和实证两方面证明，两者对于联合优化恢复IPW加权驻点同时保持有界损失都是必要的。在两个生态学上不同的生物群落中，StruMPL在AGB的RMSE和偏差上优于消融变体和最接近的已发表方法，分层分析显示AIPW将高AGB偏差降低了约54%。

</details>


### [78] [Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Aerial Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19949)
*Dongli Wu,Zhuoxiao Li,Tongyan Hua,Yinrui Ren,Xiaobao Wei,Rongjun Qin,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyCity提出了一种观测驱动的生成式重建框架，用于从稀疏航拍视角重建大规模城市场景，通过先预测观测支持的几何潜码，再利用条件化航拍补全令牌进行门控残差更新，实现单次前馈高质量3D高斯场景重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从稀疏航拍视角重建大规模城市场景面临严重的证据不平衡问题（屋顶和开放区域被反复观测，而立面、远处建筑和遮挡结构缺乏多视角支持）。现有前馈3D高斯泼溅方法直接回归确定性表示，导致鬼影、立面融合和纹理拉伸；伪视图和视频生成方法缺乏观测几何与先验驱动内容之间的清晰分离，产生看似合理但不一致的场景结构。

Method: AnyCity首先预测观测支持的几何潜码以锚定可靠结构，然后使用脚手架条件化的航拍补全令牌预测弱约束内容的门控残差更新，再进行高斯解码。训练阶段采用密集到稀疏的蒸馏技术传递结构线索，航空适应的视频扩散先验通过门控令牌条件化提供细粒度城市外观线索，观测保持目标确保精化表示与输入支持的几何一致。推理时从稀疏航拍视角单次前馈重建最终3D高斯场景。

Result: 在合成场景、航拍领域、无人机纹理和真实世界场景上的实验表明，AnyCity相较于前馈基线方法取得一致改进，实现了具有秒级推理速度的连贯城市新视角合成。

Conclusion: AnyCity通过观测驱动的生成式重建框架有效解决了稀疏航拍城市场景重建中的证据不平衡问题，在重建质量和推理效率上均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing large-scale urban scenes from sparse aerial views is a crucial yet challenging task. Due to biased top-down and shallow-oblique camera poses, sparse aerial captures exhibit strong evidence imbalance: roofs and open regions are repeatedly observed, while facades, distant buildings, and occluded structures receive little multi-view support. Existing feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting methods directly regress a deterministic representation from sparse inputs, but this often leads to ghosting, melted facades, and stretched textures. Recent pseudo-view and video-based generative reconstruction methods use additional supervision or generative priors. However, they often lack a clear separation between observed geometry and prior-driven content, which can lead to plausible but inconsistent structures. We propose AnyCity, an observation-grounded generative reconstruction framework for sparse aerial urban scenes. AnyCity first predicts an observation-supported geometry latent to anchor reliable structures, and then uses scaffold-conditioned aerial completion tokens to predict a gated residual update for weakly constrained content before Gaussian decoding. During training, dense-to-sparse distillation transfers structural cues from dense-view reconstruction, while an aerial-adapted video diffusion prior provides fine-grained urban appearance cues through gated token conditioning. Observation-preserving objectives keep the refined representation consistent with input-supported geometry. At inference time, AnyCity reconstructs the final 3D Gaussian scene from sparse aerial views in a single feed-forward pass, achieving coherent urban novel-view synthesis with second-level inference. Experiments on synthetic, aerial-domain, UAV-textured, and real-world scenes show consistent improvements over feed-forward baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 从稀疏航拍视角重建大规模城市场景是一项关键但具有挑战性的任务。由于自上而下和浅倾斜相机姿态的偏差，稀疏航拍捕获表现出强烈的证据不平衡：屋顶和开放区域被反复观测，而立面、远处建筑和遮挡结构缺乏多视角支持。现有的前馈式3D高斯泼溅方法直接从稀疏输入回归确定性表示，但这通常导致鬼影、立面融合和纹理拉伸。最近的伪视图和基于视频的生成式重建方法使用了额外的监督或生成先验。然而，它们往往缺乏观测几何与先验驱动内容之间的清晰分离，这可能导致看似合理但不一致的场景结构。我们提出AnyCity，一种面向稀疏航拍城市场景的观测驱动生成式重建框架。AnyCity首先预测一个观测支持的几何潜码以锚定可靠结构，然后使用脚手架条件化的航拍补全令牌在Gaussian解码前预测弱约束内容的门控残差更新。在训练过程中，密集到稀疏的蒸馏将结构线索从密集视图重建中传递，而航空适应的视频扩散先验通过门控令牌条件化提供细粒度的城市外观线索。观测保持目标确保精化后的表示与输入支持的几何结构保持一致。在推理时，AnyCity通过单次前馈传递从稀疏航拍视角重建最终的3D高斯场景，实现具有秒级推理的连贯城市新视角合成。在合成场景、航空领域、无人机纹理和真实世界场景上的实验表明，相较于前馈基线方法取得了一致的改进。

</details>


### [79] [AffectVerse: Emotional World Models for Multimodal Affective Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19950)
*Bo Zhao,Fanghua Ye,Yixin Ji,Sicheng Zhao,Xiaojiang Peng,Zitong YU*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AffectVerse，一个基于Qwen2.5-Omni的情感计算模型，通过情感世界模块（EWM）进行短时程潜在情感预测，在不依赖未来信号的情况下利用自监督预测信号增强情感理解，在9个基准上提升至少2.57%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型将情感识别视为对完整音视频-文本输入的静态融合，忽略了情感状态随时间展开的动态特性。因此需要一种能够对情感动态变化进行建模，同时不依赖未来观测信号的方法。

Method: 基于Qwen2.5-Omni构建，包含情感世界模块（EWM），由三个子模块组成：1）跨模态时间想象——从过去token预测未来音视频表示并进行多步展开；2）MAMA信念聚合——将想象的token压缩成模态感知的信念token；3）信念注入——将信念token注入大语言模型进行情感推理。使用未来预测作为以过去为条件的自监督信号，强制当前信念状态编码预测后续情感变化的转换线索。

Result: 在9个基准上，AffectVerse相比其他模型提升至少2.57%；消融实验表明时间想象、跨模态展开和信念聚合均带来叠加增益。

Conclusion: 预测性信念状态建模是情感计算中一种实用的替代方案，能够有效捕捉情感动态变化并提升多模态情感理解性能。

Original Abstract: Humans infer emotions by integrating observed multimodal cues with expectations about how affective states may unfold. Existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs), however, often treat emotion recognition as static fusion over complete audiovisual-text inputs, leaving affective dynamics implicit. We propose AffectVerse, a Qwen2.5-Omni-based model equipped with an Emotion World Module (EWM), an action-free representation-level module for short-horizon latent affective prediction. \rev{EWM contains three modules: 1) Cross-Modal Temporal Imagination predicts future video/audio representations from past tokens with multi-step rollout. 2) MAMA(Modality-Aware Multi-step Attention) Belief Aggregation compresses imagined tokens into modality-aware belief tokens. 3) Belief Injection inserts these belief tokens into the LLM for affective reasoning.} AffectVerse uses future prediction as a past-conditioned self-supervised signal: it does not replace modeling observed history or require unseen signals at inference, but forces the current belief state to encode transition cues that are predictive of subsequent affective change. Across nine benchmarks, AffectVerse improves at least 2.57\% over other models, while controlled ablations show additive gains from temporal imagination, cross-modal rollout, and belief aggregation. These results suggest predictive belief-state modeling is a practical alternative for affective computing.

中文摘要翻译: 人类通过整合观察到的多模态线索与对情感状态如何展开的预期来推断情感。然而，现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通常将情感识别视为对完整音视频-文本输入的静态融合，忽略了情感动态信息。我们提出AffectVerse，一个基于Qwen2.5-Omni的模型，配备情感世界模块（EWM），这是一种无动作的表示级模块，用于短时程潜在情感预测。EWM包含三个子模块：1）跨模态时间想象——从过去token通过多步展开预测未来的视频/音频表示；2）MAMA（模态感知多步注意力）信念聚合——将想象的token压缩成模态感知的信念token；3）信念注入——将这些信念token注入大语言模型进行情感推理。AffectVerse将未来预测作为以过去为条件的自监督信号：它不替代对观测历史的建模，也不需要在推理时使用未见信号，而是强制当前信念状态编码能够预测后续情感变化的转换线索。在九个基准上，AffectVerse相比其他模型提升至少2.57%，而控制消融实验显示时间想象、跨模态展开和信念聚合均带来叠加增益。这些结果表明，预测性信念状态建模是情感计算中一种实用的替代方案。

</details>


### [80] [Towards Fine-Grained Robustness: Attention-Guided Test-Time Prompt Tuning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19956)
*Jia-Wei Hai,Yijun Wang,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: A-TPT通过注意力引导的测试时提示调优，在对抗攻击下保留语义区域，提升VLM的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法依赖多视图增强，难以识别语义信息且易破坏细粒度场景中的判别性区域，导致在对抗攻击下性能下降。

Method: 首先改进梯度注意力展开机制以识别对抗攻击下存活的语义区域，然后利用这些区域引导空间变化的增强强度和多视图集成，用于提示调优和推理。

Result: 在对抗数据和干净数据上均优于现有测试时自适应方法。

Conclusion: A-TPT是一种有效的语义保持方法，能够提升VLM在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，同时不牺牲干净数据上的性能。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs), such as CLIP, have achieved significant zero-shot performance on downstream tasks with various fine-tuning adaptation methods. However, recent studies have proven that adversarial attacks can significantly degrade the inference ability of VLMs, posing substantial risks to their practical applications. Prevalent test-time adaptation methods typically rely on multi-view augmentation to implement various fine-tuning strategies, which struggle to identify semantic information and are prone to destroying discriminative regions in fine-grained scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose Attention-Guided Test-Time Prompt Tuning (A-TPT), a semantics-preserving method designed for test-time adaptation. We first refine the gradient attention rollout mechanism to identify semantically meaningful regions surviving under adversarial attacks. Furthermore, we leverage them to guide the spatially varying augmentation intensities and multi-view ensemble for prompt tuning and inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that A-TPT outperforms existing test-time adaptation methods on both adversarial and clean data. Codes are available at https://github.com/SEU-VIPGroup/A-TPT .

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）通过多种微调自适应方法在下游任务上取得了显著的零样本性能。然而，近期研究表明，对抗攻击会显著降低视觉-语言模型的推理能力，对其实际应用构成重大风险。现有的测试时自适应方法通常依赖多视图增强来实现各种微调策略，但这些方法难以识别语义信息，并且在细粒度场景中容易破坏判别性区域。为解决这些局限，我们提出了注意力引导的测试时提示调优（A-TPT），这是一种专为测试时自适应设计的语义保持方法。我们首先改进梯度注意力展开机制，以识别在对抗攻击下存活的语义显著区域。随后，利用这些区域指导空间变化的增强强度和用于提示调优与推理的多视图集成。大量实验表明，A-TPT在对抗数据和干净数据上均优于现有的测试时自适应方法。代码已开源在 https://github.com/SEU-VIPGroup/A-TPT。

</details>


### [81] [World-Ego Modeling for Long-Horizon Evolution in Hybrid Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19957)
*Zuyao Lin,Jianhui Zhang,Peidong Jia,Xiaoguang Zhao,Shanghang Zhang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出世界-自我建模（World-Ego Modeling）新范式，将未来演化分解为世界（场景规律）和自我（机器人条件动力学）两部分，并实例化为WEM模型（隐式分离规划器+CP-MoE扩散生成器），在构建的长时域混合导航-操作基准HTEWorld上取得了SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界模型将世界演化与自我演化混为一谈（world-ego entanglement），导致在长时域混合任务（导航与操作交错）中性能下降。因此需要将世界与自我解耦，分别建模场景规律和机器人条件动力学。

Method: 提出World-Ego Modeling范式，从运动、语义、意图三个视角定义世界-自我边界，分析后解耦、前解耦、全解耦三种策略。实例化为WEM模型：采用隐式分离的世界-自我规划器（implicit separate world-ego planner）结合级联并行混合专家（CP-MoE）扩散生成器，实现解耦的未来演化预测。

Result: 构建了首个长时域混合导航-操作世界模型基准HTEWorld（125K视频片段/450万帧+300条多轮评估轨迹）。WEM在该基准上达到SOTA，同时在现有纯操作基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 世界-自我解耦建模能有效提升长时域混合具身任务中的世界模型性能，WEM验证了该范式的有效性。

Original Abstract: World models are widely explored in embodied intelligence, yet they typically predict distinct evolutions of the world and the ego within a single stream, where the world captures persistent instruction-agnostic scene regularities and the ego captures robot-centric instruction-conditioned dynamics. This world-ego entanglement leads to a degradation in long-horizon embodied scenarios, particularly in hybrid tasks with interleaved navigation and manipulation behaviors. In this paper, we introduce \emph{World-Ego Modeling}, a new conceptual paradigm that decomposes future evolution into world and ego components. We define the world-ego boundary from three perspectives, i.e., motion-, semantic-, and intention-based views, and analyze three disentanglement strategies with post-, pre-, and full disentanglement. Further, we instantiate this paradigm as the World-Ego Model (WEM), a unified embodied world model that couples an implicit separate world-ego planner with a cascade-parallel mixture-of-experts (CP-MoE) diffusion generator. To enable rigorous evaluation, we further construct HTEWorld, the first benchmark for long-horizon world modeling with hybrid navigation-manipulation tasks, providing 125K video clips (over 4.5M frames) with fine-grained action annotations and 300 multi-turn evaluation trajectories (over 2K instructions). Extensive experiments show that WEM achieves state-of-the-art performance on HTEWorld while remaining competitive on existing manipulation-only benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 世界模型在具身智能中被广泛探索，但它们通常在一个单一流中预测世界和自我的不同演化，其中世界捕获与指令无关的持久场景规律，而自我捕获以机器人为中心、受指令调节的动态变化。这种世界-自我纠缠导致在长时域具身场景中性能下降，尤其是在导航和操作行为交错的混合任务中。本文提出**世界-自我建模（World-Ego Modeling）**，一种将未来演化分解为世界和自我组件的新概念范式。我们从运动、语义和意图三个视角定义世界-自我边界，并分析了后解耦、前解耦和全解耦三种解耦策略。进一步，我们将该范式实例化为**世界-自我模型（WEM）**，一个统一的具身世界模型，它结合了隐式分离的世界-自我规划器与级联并行混合专家（CP-MoE）扩散生成器。为进行严格评估，我们进一步构建了**HTEWorld**，首个用于长时域混合导航-操作任务的世界模型基准，提供125K视频片段（超过450万帧）的细粒度动作标注以及300条多轮评估轨迹（超过2000条指令）。大量实验表明，WEM在HTEWorld上取得了最先进的性能，同时在现有的纯操作基准上保持竞争力。

</details>


### [82] [SphericalDreamer: Generating Navigable Immersive 3D Worlds with Panorama Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19974)
*Antoine Schnepf,Karim Kassab,Flavian Vasile,Andrew Comport*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SphericalDreamer通过生成多张全景图像并将其提升为3D环境，实现了从文本提示创建完全沉浸式、可长距离导航的360°户外3D场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法无法同时实现长距离导航和覆盖完整的全方位视场（水平360°、垂直180°），限制了3D环境的沉浸感和可探索性。

Method: 首先生成多张全景图像，然后将这些图像提升到3D空间，并在保持视觉和几何一致性的前提下将它们融合在一起，构建完整的3D环境。

Result: SphericalDreamer生成了高度详细、完全沉浸式的3D环境，在规模和可导航性方面相比现有方法有显著提升。

Conclusion: SphericalDreamer成功解决了同时实现长距离导航和全覆盖视场的难题，为文本驱动的沉浸式3D环境生成提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: The generation of immersive and navigable 3D environments is increasingly prevalent with the growing adoption of virtual reality and 3D content. However, recent methods face a fundamental limitation: they cannot produce 3D worlds that simultaneously (i) are navigable over long-range spatial extents and (ii) cover the complete omnidirectional field of view ($360^\circ$ horizontally and $180^\circ$ vertically). To address this challenge, we introduce SphericalDreamer, a method for generating fully immersive and long-range 3D outdoor environments from textual prompts. Our approach is built on the generation of multiple panoramic images, which are subsequently lifted into 3D and fused together while maintaining visual and geometric consistency. SphericalDreamer produces highly detailed, fully immersive 3D environments, while substantially improving scale and navigability compared to prior approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 随着虚拟现实和3D内容的日益普及，沉浸式且可导航的3D环境生成变得越来越重要。然而，现有方法面临一个根本性限制：它们无法同时生成长距离可导航且覆盖完整全方位视场（水平360°、垂直180°）的3D世界。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了SphericalDreamer，一种从文本提示生成完全沉浸式且可长距离导航的3D户外环境的方法。我们的方法基于多张全景图像的生成，随后将这些图像提升到3D空间，并在保持视觉和几何一致性的前提下将它们融合在一起。SphericalDreamer生成了高度详细、完全沉浸式的3D环境，同时在规模和可导航性方面相比现有方法有显著提升。

</details>


### [83] [RECIPE: Procedural Planning via Grounding in Instructional Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19976)
*Luigi Seminara,Antonino Furnari,Lorenzo Torresani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RECIPE框架，利用视频语料库中的ASR转录文本作为验证器而非标注源，通过GRPO强化学习将接地质量作为奖励信号，在无需人工标注的情况下显著提升视觉规划任务的性能，并保持生成多样性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉规划任务受限于人工标注数据规模小、领域窄、编码单一执行轨迹；大规模教学视频的ASR噪声伪标签会传播分割和对齐错误且同样为单轨迹。关键不对称性在于从噪声视频中提取干净步骤标签困难，但验证生成步骤序列是否在ASR转录中有时序接地则廉价且可规模化。

Method: 提出RECIPE框架，利用ASR转录的接地质量作为GRPO的奖励信号，将噪声语料库转化为验证器而非标签源。该框架统一适用于两种规划器输入配置（Socratic和Video）以及有标注和弱监督场景。

Result: 在7个过程基准上，RECIPE-RL在0.5B、3B、7B三种规模下均优于基线和有监督微调；域内宏准确率提升+7至+8个点，零样本场景提升高达+16个点；在Visual Planning for Assistance任务中超越最强零样本基线，在COIN上保持SFT所破坏的生成多样性。

Conclusion: RECIPE通过将噪声语料库用作验证器而非标注源，有效利用大规模弱监督数据提升视觉规划能力，无需人工标注即可超越有监督微调，并保持生成多样性。

Original Abstract: Visual planning asks a model to generate the remaining steps of a procedure in natural language given a partial video context and a goal. Progress on this task is bottlenecked by annotation: clean labeled datasets are small, domain-narrow, and encode a single execution trajectory per example, even though many valid orderings exist. Large-scale instructional video corpora offer orders of magnitude more procedural content, but supervised fine-tuning on pseudo-labels from their noisy ASR narrations propagates segmentation and alignment errors and stays single-trajectory. We identify a key asymmetry: extracting clean step labels from noisy video is hard, but verifying whether a generated step sequence is temporally grounded in ASR transcripts is cheap and scales to millions of videos via precomputed text embeddings. We exploit this asymmetry in RECIPE, which uses grounding quality as a reward for GRPO, turning the noisy corpus into a verifier rather than a label source. The framework applies uniformly to two planner input configurations (Socratic, with a textual history extracted by a frozen VLM, and Video, consuming video tokens directly) and to annotated and weakly supervised regimes. We evaluate on 7 procedural benchmarks using a reference-based LLM-as-judge protocol scoring plans across 6 procedural criteria. RECIPE-RL improves over the base checkpoint at all scales (0.5B, 3B, 7B) and every benchmark, with macro-accuracy gains of +7 to +8 points in-domain and up to +16 points zero-shot. It outperforms supervised fine-tuning on both annotated and pseudo-labeled plans (the latter degrades the base) and remains robust without human annotations. Used as the proposal stage of a prior propose-assess-search planner, it improves over the strongest zero-shot baseline at every horizon on Visual Planning for Assistance, and on COIN it preserves the generation diversity that SFT collapses.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉规划要求模型在给定部分视频上下文和目标的情况下，以自然语言生成过程剩余的步骤。该任务的进展受到标注瓶颈的制约：干净标注数据集规模小、领域窄，且每个示例仅编码单一执行轨迹，尽管实际上存在许多有效的排序方式。大规模教学视频语料库提供了数量级更多的过程内容，但在其含噪ASR转录上通过伪标签进行监督微调会传播分割和对齐错误，且仍然局限于单轨迹。我们识别出一个关键不对称性：从含噪视频中提取干净步骤标签是困难的，但验证生成的步骤序列是否在ASR转录中有时序接地则是廉价的，并且可以通过预计算文本嵌入扩展到数百万视频。我们在RECIPE中利用了这一不对称性，将接地质量用作GRPO的奖励，从而将含噪语料库转化为验证器而非标签源。该框架统一适用于两种规划器输入配置（Socratic配置，使用冻结VLM提取的文本历史；以及Video配置，直接消费视频令牌），并适用于有标注和弱监督场景。我们在7个过程基准上采用基于参考的LLM-as-Judge评估协议，对规划在6个过程标准上进行评分。RECIPE-RL在0.5B、3B、7B所有尺度和每个基准上均优于基线检查点，域内宏准确率提升+7至+8个点，零样本场景提升高达+16个点。它同时优于在有标注计划和伪标签计划上的监督微调（后者会降低基线性能），并且在无需人工标注的情况下保持鲁棒。当用作先前提议-评估-搜索规划器的提议阶段时，它在Visual Planning for Assistance任务的每个规划长度上均超越最强零样本基线；在COIN上，它保持了SFT所破坏的生成多样性。

</details>


### [84] [InterLight: Leveraging Intrinsic Illumination Priors for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19982)
*Ziqi Wang,Xu Zhang,Laibin Chang,Shi Chen,Jiaqi Ma,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterLight提出了一个系统性挖掘和利用内在光照先验的低光照图像增强框架，通过物理引导增强、自适应提示、亮度门控记忆机制和自监督一致性约束，有效解决了过增强和颜色失真问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的Retinex方法在低光照图像增强中常出现过增强或颜色失真，且通常假设均匀噪声或理想光照，缺乏对光照先验的深度利用。

Method: 1) 物理引导数据增强注入传感器级光照响应先验；2) 自适应提示根据场景潜在光照状态表示退化；3) 亮度门控内在记忆机制选择性补偿暗区信息损失，同时保持亮区保真度；4) 自监督一致性目标蒸馏光照不变特征进行整体正则化。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验证明了方法的有效性，实现了更清晰的纹理和视觉上更一致的增强结果。

Conclusion: 通过深度挖掘内在光照先验，InterLight能够构建光照感知的增强流程，有效提升低光照图像增强的质量和视觉一致性。

Original Abstract: Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) has long been a challenging problem in low-level vision, as insufficient illumination often leads to low contrast, detail loss, and noise. Recent studies show that deep learning-based Retinex theory can effectively decouple illumination and reflectance. However, existing methods frequently suffer from over-enhancement or color distortion, and often assume uniform noise or ideal lighting. To address these limitations, we propose InterLight, a novel framework that systematically excavates and operationalizes intrinsic illumination priors for LLIE.Our core insight is that robust enhancement requires not just estimating illumination, but constructing an illumination-aware pipeline. We first inject sensor-level illumination-response priors via physics-guided augmentation, then represent the degradation through adaptive prompts conditioned on the scene's latent illumination state. This explicit representation directly guides a luminance-gated intrinsic memory mechanism to selectively compensate for information loss, prioritizing reconstruction in dark regions while preserving fidelity in bright ones. Finally, the entire process is regularized by a self-supervised consistency objective that distills illumination-invariant features. By deeply exploiting intrinsic illumination priors, our method achieves clearer textures and more visually coherent enhancement results. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Code is available at: https://github.com/House-yuyu/InterLight.

中文摘要翻译: 低光照图像增强（LLIE）长期以来一直是低级视觉中的一个挑战性问题，因为光照不足常常导致对比度低、细节丢失和噪声。近年来的研究表明，基于深度学习的Retinex理论可以有效解耦光照和反射率。然而，现有方法经常出现过增强或颜色失真，并且通常假设均匀噪声或理想光照。为解决这些局限，我们提出了InterLight，一个系统性挖掘和利用内在光照先验的低光照图像增强框架。我们的核心洞察是，鲁棒的增强不仅需要估计光照，更需要构建一个光照感知的流程。我们首先通过物理引导的数据增强注入传感器级的光照响应先验，然后通过基于场景潜在光照状态的自适应提示来表示退化。这种显式表示直接引导一个亮度门控的内在记忆机制，选择性地补偿信息损失，在优先重建暗区的同时保持亮区的保真度。最后，整个过程通过一个自监督的一致性目标进行正则化，该目标蒸馏出光照不变的特征。通过深度挖掘内在光照先验，我们的方法实现了更清晰的纹理和视觉上更一致的增强结果。在多个基准数据集上的大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性。代码已开源：https://github.com/House-yuyu/InterLight。

</details>


### [85] [A Nash Equilibrium Framework For Training-Free Multimodal Step Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20033)
*Rohit Sinha,Kunal Tilaganji,Tanuja Ganu,Nagarajan Natarajan,Amit Sharma,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的验证方法，将多模态大语言模型的逐步推理验证建模为专家裁判间的纳什均衡博弈，利用裁判间的分歧信号而非简单平均分数来检测推理步骤的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有验证方法存在局限：基于学习的评判器需要大量标注数据且跨任务表现不一致；现有无训练方法仅简单平均不同来源的分数，忽略了当分数存在分歧时，分歧本身蕴含了推理步骤是否真正有效的关键信息。

Method: 将逐步验证建模为多个专门化裁判之间的纳什均衡博弈——裁判间的一致信号表示有效步骤，分歧则揭示不稳定性；通过封闭解计算均衡分数，实现分歧感知的过滤和稳定性感知的推理步骤排序。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，该方法相比基线模型取得2.4%至5.2%的一致提升，且性能与基于学习的评判器具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 跨模态的一致信号（而非简单的平均置信度）无需任务特定的适配即可提供鲁棒的验证信号。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Learned critics need extensive labeled data and show inconsistent performance across different tasks. Meanwhile, existing training-free methods simply average scores from different sources, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasoning step is truly valid or not. We propose a training-free verification approach that treats step-wise verification as a coordination problem among specialized judges. We formalize these judges' interaction as a Nash equilibrium game where agreement signals valid steps while disagreement reveals instability. Our method computes equilibrium scores through a closed-form solution, enabling both disagreement-aware filtering and stability-conscious ranking of reasoning steps. Evaluated across six benchmarks, our approach achieves consistent improvements of 2.4% to 5.2% over baseline models and shows competitive performance against learned critics, demonstrating that cross-modal agreement (not just average confidence) provides robust verification signals without task-specific adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型生成的推理链通常包含细微错误，导致最终答案不正确。当前的验证方法存在显著局限：基于学习的评判器需要大量标注数据，且在不同任务上表现不一致；而现有的无训练方法仅简单平均不同来源的分数，忽略了一个关键洞见——当这些分数存在分歧时，分歧本身蕴含了推理步骤是否真正有效的重要信息。我们提出了一种无需训练的验证方法，将逐步验证建模为多个专门化裁判之间的协调问题。我们将这些裁判的交互形式化为纳什均衡博弈，其中一致性表示有效步骤，而分歧则揭示不稳定性。我们的方法通过封闭解计算均衡分数，从而实现对推理步骤的分歧感知过滤和稳定性感知排序。在六个基准测试上的评估表明，我们的方法相比基线模型取得了2.4%至5.2%的一致提升，并与基于学习的评判器展现出竞争力，证明了跨模态一致信号（而非简单的平均置信度）无需任务特定适配即可提供鲁棒的验证信号。

</details>


### [86] [Stage-adaptive Token Selection for Efficient Omni-modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20035)
*Zijie Xin,Jie Yang,Ruixiang Zhao,Tianyi Wang,Fengyun Rao,Jing Lyu,Xirong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEATS是一种无需训练的、阶段自适应的token选择方法，通过跨模态依赖分析在全模态大语言模型中渐进式剪枝冗余的视觉和音频token，在仅保留10%非文本token的情况下实现9.3倍FLOPs缩减和4.8倍预填充加速，同时保持96.3%的原始性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的全模态大语言模型处理密集的非文本token计算开销巨大，而现有的无训练token选择方法要么只关注纯视觉输入，要么仅在LLM之前以固定模态比率剪枝token，未能捕捉跨模态token重要性在模型各层之间的演变规律。

Method: SEATS方法包含三个阶段：1) LLM之前，通过注意力加权多样性选择去除时空冗余；2) LLM内部，跨模块逐步剪枝token，并使用查询相关性得分动态分配保留预算；3) 后期层，跨模态融合完成后移除所有剩余非文本token。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni和Qwen3-Omni上的实验表明，仅保留10%的视觉和音频token，SEATS实现了9.3倍FLOPs减少和4.8倍预填充加速，同时保持96.3%的原始性能。

Conclusion: 通过分析全模态大语言模型的逐层token依赖关系，发现视觉和音频依赖呈现块状模式并随深度逐渐减弱，验证了后期层非文本token的冗余性，SEATS方法有效提升了推理效率。

Original Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (om-LLMs) achieve unified audio-visual understanding by encoding video and audio into temporally aligned token sequences interleaved at the window level. However, processing these dense non-textual tokens throughout the LLM incurs substantial computational overhead. Although training-free token selection can reduce this cost, existing methods either focus on visual-only inputs or prune om-LLM tokens only before the LLM with fixed per-modality ratios, failing to capture how cross-modal token importance evolves across layers. To address this limitation, we first analyze the layer-wise token dependency of om-LLMs. We find that visual and audio dependencies follow a block-wise pattern and gradually weaken with depth, indicating that many late-layer non-textual tokens become redundant after cross-modal fusion. Motivated by this observation, we propose SEATS, a training-free, stage-adaptive token selection method for efficient om-LLM inference. Before the LLM, SEATS removes spatiotemporal redundancy via attention-weighted diversity selection. Inside the LLM, it progressively prunes tokens across blocks and dynamically allocates the retention budget from temporal windows to modalities using query relevance scores. In late layers, it removes all remaining non-textual tokens once cross-modal fusion is complete. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni demonstrate that SEATS effectively improves inference efficiency. Retaining only 10% of visual and audio tokens, it achieves a 9.3x FLOPs reduction and a 4.8x prefill speedup while preserving 96.3% of the original performance.

中文摘要翻译: 全模态大语言模型通过将视频和音频编码为时间对齐的、在窗口级别交错的token序列，实现了统一的音视频理解。然而，在LLM中处理这些稠密的非文本token带来了巨大的计算开销。虽然无需训练的token选择可以降低这一成本，但现有方法要么只关注纯视觉输入，要么仅在LLM之前以固定的模态比率剪枝全模态LLM的token，未能捕捉跨模态token重要性在各层之间的演变规律。为解决这一局限性，我们首先分析了全模态LLM的逐层token依赖关系。我们发现视觉和音频依赖遵循块状模式，并随深度逐渐减弱，这表明在跨模态融合后，许多后期层的非文本token变得冗余。受此观察启发，我们提出了SEATS，一种无需训练的、阶段自适应的token选择方法，用于高效的全模态LLM推理。在LLM之前，SEATS通过注意力加权多样性选择去除时空冗余。在LLM内部，它跨模块逐步剪枝token，并使用查询相关性得分从时间窗口向模态动态分配保留预算。在后期层，一旦跨模态融合完成，它移除所有剩余的非文本token。在Qwen2.5-Omni和Qwen3-Omni上的实验表明，SEATS有效提升了推理效率。仅保留10%的视觉和音频token，它实现了9.3倍的FLOPs减少和4.8倍的预填充加速，同时保持了96.3%的原始性能。

</details>


### [87] [OP2GS: Object-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting with Dual-Opacity Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20044)
*Guiyu Liu,Niklas Vaara,Janne Mustaniemi,Juho Kannala,Janne Heikkilä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OP2GS提出了一种面向对象的3D高斯表示方法，通过双不透明度机制（视觉不透明度σ和实例不透明度σ*）解耦视觉重建与实例分割，实现高效开放词汇场景理解，避免了高维特征存储和标签污染问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在开放词汇场景理解中存在两个主要问题：基于特征蒸馏的方法需要为每个高斯存储高维特征，导致巨大的存储和解码开销；基于2D掩码提升的方法在投影过程中容易产生标签污染，即对视觉重建必要的高斯可能被赋予错误的对象标签。这些问题阻碍了3DGS在下游任务中的应用。

Method: 提出OP2GS表示，为每个高斯体素引入显式实例身份和专用的实例不透明度σ*用于对象掩码渲染。原始不透明度σ负责视觉重建，σ*决定高斯是否贡献于特定对象掩码，实现双不透明度解耦。提出随机对象损失，利用3DGS的标准透射率可见性优化一维实例占用场。通过多视角聚合在对象级别附加语义描述符，避免逐高斯特征存储。

Result: 与特征训练方法相比，OP2GS在达到竞争性开放词汇性能的同时显著降低了计算开销；与无训练流水线相比，通过物理一致的占用学习解决了可见性歧义问题。

Conclusion: OP2GS通过双不透明度机制有效地解耦了视觉重建与实例占用，既避免了标签污染，又消除了逐高斯的特征存储需求，为3DGS在开放词汇场景理解任务中提供了一种高效且性能优越的解决方案。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an explicit and efficient scene representation, but its primitives lack inherent object-level identity, hindering downstream tasks such as open-vocabulary scene understanding. Existing methods typically address this by either distilling high-dimensional feature embeddings into Gaussians or by lifting 2D mask labels into 3D via heuristic refinement. However, feature-based approaches incur heavy storage and decoding overhead, while lifting-based pipelines remain vulnerable to label contamination: Gaussians necessary for appearance reconstruction often receive incorrect object labels during 2D-to-3D projection. We propose OP2GS, an object-aware Gaussian representation that augments each primitive with an explicit instance identity and a dedicated instance opacity $σ^{*}$ for object-mask rendering. The original opacity $σ$ remains responsible for visual reconstruction, while $σ^{*}$ models whether a Gaussian should contribute to a particular object mask. This dual-opacity formulation decouples visual existence from instance occupancy: mislabeled Gaussians can remain available for image rendering while becoming transparent in the object-mask branch. To learn this representation, we introduce a random object loss that optimizes the 1D instance occupancy field using the standard transmittance-based visibility of 3DGS. Semantic descriptors are then attached at the object level through multi-view aggregation, eliminating per-Gaussian feature storage. Compared with feature-training approaches, OP2GS achieves competitive open-vocabulary performance while significantly reducing computational overhead. Compared with training-free pipelines, it leverages physically consistent occupancy learning to resolve visibility ambiguities.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）提供了一种显式且高效的场景表示，但其基本单元缺乏固有的对象级身份标识，阻碍了开放词汇场景理解等下游任务。现有方法通常通过将高维特征嵌入蒸馏到高斯中，或通过启发式细化将2D掩码标签提升到3D来解决这一问题。然而，基于特征的方法会带来沉重的存储和解码开销，而基于提升的流水线仍然容易受到标签污染的影响：在2D到3D投影过程中，对外观重建所必需的高斯常常会接收到错误的对象标签。我们提出OP2GS，一种面向对象的感知高斯表示，为每个基本单元增补了一个显式的实例身份和一个专用的实例不透明度σ*，用于对象掩码渲染。原始不透明度σ仍然负责视觉重建，而σ*则建模高斯是否应贡献于特定对象掩码。这种双不透明度公式将视觉存在与实例占用解耦：被错误标注的高斯可以保持可用于图像渲染，同时在对象掩码分支中变为透明。为了学习这种表示，我们引入了一种随机对象损失，利用3DGS标准透射率可见性来优化一维实例占用场。然后通过多视角聚合在对象级别附加语义描述符，消除了逐高斯的特征存储。与特征训练方法相比，OP2GS在达到竞争性开放词汇性能的同时显著降低了计算开销。与无训练流水线相比，它利用物理一致的占用学习来解决可见性歧义问题。

</details>


### [88] [X-Ray cardiac angiographic vessel segmentation based on pixel classification using machine learning and region growing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20073)
*E O Rodrigues,L O Rodrigues,J J Lima,D Casanova,F Favarim,E R Dosciatti,V Pegorini,L S N Oliveira,F F C Morais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于像素分类的X射线血管造影图像血管分割方法，结合纹理特征、ELEMENT方法论和随机森林分类器，达到了95.48%的准确率，超越了现有无监督方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为了在X射线血管造影图像中实现更准确的血管分割，解决现有无监督方法性能不足的问题。

Method: 采用像素分类方法，提取各向异性扩散、Hessian矩阵特征、数学形态学和统计量等纹理特征；应用ELEMENT方法论（由区域生长控制的像素分类，分类结果影响后续分类）；使用随机森林分类器预测像素是否属于血管结构。

Result: 在文献中取得了最佳准确率（95.48%），优于无监督的现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的像素分类方法结合纹理特征、ELEMENT方法论和随机森林，在X射线血管造影图像血管分割任务上表现优异，达到了文献最佳性能。

Original Abstract: This work proposes a pixel-classification approach for vessel segmentation in x-ray angiograms. The proposal uses textural features such as anisotropic diffusion, features based on the Hessian matrix, mathematical morphology and statistics. These features are extracted from the neighborhood of each pixel. The approach also uses the ELEMENT methodology, which consists of creating a pixel-classification controlled by region-growing where the result of the classification affects further classifications of pixels. The Random Forests classifier is used to predict whether the pixel belongs to the vessel structure. The approach achieved the best accuracy in the literature (95.48%) outperforming unsupervised state-of-the-art approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种用于X射线血管造影图像中血管分割的像素分类方法。该方法使用纹理特征，如各向异性扩散、基于Hessian矩阵的特征、数学形态学和统计量。这些特征从每个像素的邻域中提取。该方法还采用了ELEMENT方法论，即创建由区域生长控制的像素分类，分类结果影响后续像素的分类。使用随机森林分类器预测像素是否属于血管结构。该方法取得了文献中最高的准确率（95.48%），优于无监督的现有方法。

</details>


### [89] [Spatially Prompted Visual Trajectory Prediction for Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20085)
*Yifan Li,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhao Ge,Yu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了空间提示视觉轨迹预测（SP-VTP）新任务，通过初始空间提示（如边界框或点）来定义机器人操作任务的目标，并构建了EgoSPT数据集和SPOT模型，实验表明该方法在跨场景轨迹预测上优于非提示或单源提示基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在杂乱环境中，通过语言指令或任务标识符指定机器人操作不够精确，空间指示（如指出移动什么、放在哪里）更有效。现有方法缺乏对空间提示下视觉轨迹预测问题的正式定义，因此本文旨在形式化这一新问题并建立基准。

Method: 提出SPOT模型，包含三个模块：任务编码器（处理第一帧视觉特征和坐标空间提示）、观测编码器（处理当前视觉和历史上下文信息）、轨迹生成器（预测未来末端执行器运动轨迹）。

Result: 在严格场景级划分的实验设置下，SPOT在跨场景轨迹预测任务上优于非提示或单源提示的基线方法。

Conclusion: EgoSPT数据集和SPOT模型共同建立了SP-VTP这一新的空间提示问题，为基于自我中心视角的操作任务提供了一种简单且可扩展的任务条件设定方式。

Original Abstract: Robotic manipulation is often specified through language instructions or task identifiers, yet cluttered environments with similar objects are better handled by spatially indicating what to move and where to place it. Addressing the vision-centric challenge of object and goal specification, we present, to the best of our knowledge, the first formalization of Spatially Prompted Visual Trajectory Prediction (SP-VTP). This novel setting utilizes initial spatial prompts (like bounding boxes or points) to define task objectives, tasking the model with forecasting future end-effector trajectories from egocentric streams. To study this problem, we collect and annotate EgoSPT, a dataset of egocentric spatially prompted manipulation trajectories with first-frame object and target grounding annotations and recovered 3D end-effector motion. SP-VTP is challenging because the task specification is static, while the scene configuration evolves over time. To solve this problem, we propose SPOT(Spatially Prompted Object-Target Policy), which combines a task encoder for first-frame visual and coordinate spatial prompts, an observation encoder for current visual and history context, and a trajectory generator for future end-effector motion. Experiments under strict scene-level splits show that SPOT improves cross-scene trajectory prediction over non-prompted or single-source prompted baselines. Together, EgoSPT and SPOT establish a new spatial prompting problem SP-VTP, as a simple and scalable task condition for egocentric manipulation.

中文摘要翻译: 机器人操作通常通过语言指令或任务标识符来指定，然而在存在相似物体的杂乱环境中，通过空间指示来指明移动什么以及放置在哪里更为有效。针对物体和目标规范这一以视觉为中心的挑战，我们提出了据我们所知首个空间提示视觉轨迹预测（SP-VTP）的形式化定义。这一新颖设定利用初始空间提示（如边界框或点）来定义任务目标，要求模型从自我中心视频流中预测未来的末端执行器轨迹。为研究此问题，我们收集并标注了EgoSPT——一个自我中心空间提示操作轨迹数据集，包含首帧物体与目标定位标注以及恢复的3D末端执行器运动。SP-VTP具有挑战性，因为任务规范是静态的，而场景配置随时间演化。为解决此问题，我们提出了SPOT（空间提示物体-目标策略），它结合了用于首帧视觉和坐标空间提示的任务编码器、用于当前视觉和历史上下文的观测编码器，以及用于未来末端执行器运动的轨迹生成器。在严格场景级划分下的实验表明，SPOT在跨场景轨迹预测上优于非提示或单源提示的基线方法。EgoSPT和SPOT共同建立了SP-VTP这一新的空间提示问题，作为自我中心操作的一种简单且可扩展的任务条件。

</details>
