### [230] [A Nonlinear Complexity Index for Wearable PPG Cardiovascular Stability: Multiscale Validation, Systematic Evaluation Correction, and Bayesian Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18802)
*Timothy Oladunni,Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于心脏稳定性理论的稳定性约束心血管稳定性指数（SCSI），通过识别并纠正三种评估伪像，在四个PPG数据集上验证了其性能，并提出了最小可部署的三组件稀疏架构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可穿戴光电容积描记法（PPG）的心血管稳定性评估缺乏原则性的非线性框架，启发式参数选择和评估协议存在严重缺陷，导致报告的性能被夸大。

Method: 基于心脏稳定性理论构建SCSI；在四个异构PPG数据集的176,742个片段上验证；识别并纠正三种评估伪像（段级交叉验证泄漏、测试集归一化泄漏、池化AUC加权过度掩盖患者个体失败）；采用贝叶斯优化调优15个联合参数；进行消融分析识别主导组件。

Result: 跨数据集分析显示大效应量（eta2=0.351, p<0.001）；跨尺度一致性高（kappa>0.97）；与呼吸率显著相关（Spearman r=0.346, p=0.011）；纠正伪像后AUC从0.573提升至0.720；在18个留出记录上池化AUC为0.757，阴性预测值0.966；外部验证AUC为0.621；非线性复杂度模块为消融分析中的主导组件。

Conclusion: SCSI在纠正评估伪像后提供了可重复的心血管稳定性评估基准，非线性复杂度模块是核心组件，稀疏三组件架构可作为最小可部署配置。

Original Abstract: Cardiovascular stability estimation from wearable photoplethysmography (PPG) requires a principled nonlinear framework, yet major gaps persist in heuristic parameter selection and evaluation protocols that inflate reported performance. We introduce a Stability-Constrained Cardiovascular Stability Index (SCSI) grounded in Cardiac Stability Theory and validate it across 176,742 segments from four heterogeneous PPG datasets at three temporal scales. Cross-dataset analysis demonstrates a large Kruskal-Wallis effect size (eta2 = 0.351, p < 0.001), strong cross-scale consistency (kappa > 0.97), and significant correlation with respiratory rate across 53 ICU records (Spearman r = 0.346, p = 0.011). We identify three evaluation artifacts that inflate heuristic AUC from a true baseline of 0.573 to 0.752: segment-level cross-validation leakage, test-set normalization leakage, and pooled-AUC overweighting that conceals per-patient failure. Correcting these artifacts and applying Bayesian optimization over 15 joint parameters yields SCSI with cross-validation AUC of 0.720. On 18 held-out records, SCSI achieves pooled AUC of 0.757 (95% CI: 0.686-0.828) and negative predictive value of 0.966 for tachypnea screening, while per-record AUC of 0.497 +/- 0.207 is disclosed for transparency. External validation on 42 elective-surgery records yields AUC of 0.621, confirming cross-population generalization. Ablation analysis identifies the nonlinear complexity module as the dominant component. A sparse three-component architecture is proposed as the minimal deployable configuration. The corrected protocol provides a reproducible benchmark for future wearable cardiovascular stability indices.

中文摘要翻译: 从可穿戴光电容积描记法（PPG）估计心血管稳定性需要一个有原则的非线性框架，然而启发式参数选择和评估协议中存在的重大差距导致了报告性能的夸大。本文提出了一种基于心脏稳定性理论的稳定性约束心血管稳定性指数（SCSI），并在四个异构PPG数据集的176,742个片段上，于三个时间尺度上进行了验证。跨数据集分析显示出较大的Kruskal-Wallis效应量（eta2=0.351, p<0.001）、强跨尺度一致性（kappa>0.97），以及53个ICU记录中与呼吸率的显著相关性（Spearman r=0.346, p=0.011）。我们识别出三种将启发式AUC从真实基线0.573提升至0.752的评估伪像：段级交叉验证泄漏、测试集归一化泄漏，以及池化AUC加权过度掩盖患者个体失败。纠正这些伪像并应用贝叶斯优化对15个联合参数进行调优后，SCSI的交叉验证AUC达到0.720。在18个留出记录上，SCSI的池化AUC为0.757（95% CI: 0.686-0.828），用于呼吸急促筛查的阴性预测值为0.966，同时公开透明地披露了每记录AUC为0.497±0.207。在42个择期手术记录上的外部验证AUC为0.621，确认了跨人群泛化能力。消融分析识别出非线性复杂度模块为主导组件。本文提出了一种稀疏三组件架构作为最小可部署配置。修正后的协议为未来的可穿戴心血管稳定性指数提供了可重复的基准。

</details>


### [231] [Cross-Subject Intracranial EEG Reconstruction from Scalp Recordings Using Multi-Scale Cross-Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18897)
*Tien-Dat Pham,Xuan-The Tran*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出CAST模型，利用跨注意力时空Transformer从头皮脑电中重建未见受试者的颅内脑电信号，仅需少量校准数据即可适应新受试者，在皮层区域达到较高重建相关性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有从无创头皮脑电估计颅内脑电的研究大多依赖患者特定模型，形成循环依赖：如果仍需手术收集训练数据，无创模型的实际价值有限。因此需要实现跨受试者的颅内脑电重建。

Method: 提出CAST（Cross-Attention Spatial-Temporal Transformer）框架，采用两阶段迁移学习策略：首先通过时序编码器以三种不同分辨率提取多尺度神经表征；然后利用仅几分钟的目标受试者数据，通过通道感知解码器校准以适应不同受试者间差异巨大的电极放置位置。

Result: 在包含1,282个颅内脑电通道的两个公开数据集上进行留一受试者交叉验证，结果表明：皮层表面附近的信号重建效果明显优于深部皮层下活动；在可观测性高的感觉运动区域，中央前回峰值相关性达r=0.864；通过通道选择策略，可行受试者平均相关性达r=0.545，超越以往的受试者内基线。

Conclusion: 皮层颅内脑电信号可在无需大量受试者特定训练的情况下从未见受试者的头皮脑电中重建，仅需短暂校准即可使模型适应新的硬件配置。

Original Abstract: Intracranial EEG (iEEG) provides high-fidelity neural recordings essential for clinical and brain-computer interface applications, but acquiring these signals requires invasive surgery. While recent studies have attempted to estimate iEEG from non-invasive scalp EEG, most rely on patient-specific models, creating a circular dependency: if surgery is required to collect training data, the non-invasive model offers limited practical benefit. In this study, we address the challenge of cross-subject iEEG reconstruction by predicting intracranial signals for unseen patients using models trained on other individuals. We propose CAST (Cross-Attention Spatial-Temporal Transformer), a machine learning framework that translates scalp EEG into multi-channel iEEG waveforms through a two-stage transfer learning strategy. First, a temporal encoder extracts multi-scale neural representations at three different resolutions. Then, because electrode placements vary substantially across patients, a channel-aware decoder is calibrated using only a few minutes of data from the target subject. We evaluated the proposed method using leave-one-subject-out cross-validation on two public datasets comprising 1,282 iEEG channels. Experimental results demonstrate that CAST reconstructs cortical signals located near the scalp surface substantially better than deep subcortical activity. In highly observable sensorimotor regions, the model achieved peak correlations of up to r=0.864 in the precentral gyrus. Furthermore, with a channel selection strategy, CAST obtained a mean correlation of r=0.545 on viable subjects, outperforming previous within-subject baselines. These findings indicate that cortical iEEG signals can be reconstructed for unseen subjects from scalp EEG without extensive patient-specific training, and that only a brief calibration phase is sufficient to adapt the model to new hardware configurations.

中文摘要翻译: 颅内脑电图（iEEG）提供了临床和脑机接口应用所必需的高保真神经记录，但获取这些信号需要侵入性手术。虽然近期研究尝试从无创的头皮脑电图估计颅内脑电，但大多数依赖于患者特定模型，形成了一种循环依赖：如果仍需手术来收集训练数据，则无创模型的实际效益有限。在本研究中，我们通过利用在其他个体上训练的模型来预测未见患者的颅内信号，从而应对跨受试者颅内脑电重建的挑战。我们提出CAST（Cross-Attention Spatial-Temporal Transformer），一种通过两阶段迁移学习策略将头皮脑电图转换为多通道颅内脑电波形的机器学习框架。首先，时序编码器以三种不同分辨率提取多尺度神经表征。然后，由于电极放置位置在不同患者间差异显著，通道感知解码器仅使用目标受试者几分钟的数据进行校准。我们采用留一受试者交叉验证方法在两个包含1,282个颅内脑电通道的公开数据集上评估了所提方法。实验结果表明，CAST对位于皮层表面附近的皮层信号的重建效果显著优于深部皮层下活动。在可观测性高的感觉运动区域，模型在中央前回的峰值相关性达到r=0.864。此外，通过通道选择策略，CAST在可行受试者上获得了r=0.545的平均相关性，超越了以往的受试者内基线。这些发现表明，皮层颅内脑电信号可在无需大量受试者特定训练的情况下从未见受试者的头皮脑电中重建，并且仅需短暂的校准阶段即可使模型适应新的硬件配置。

</details>


### [232] [Measurement Selection Strategies for Position Estimation in Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19406)
*Neetu R. R,Shrihari Vasudevan,Ranjani H. G*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于测量选择的策略，利用射线追踪模拟器获取AP邻域信息，通过邻域基数选择、交集/并集及固定选择等方法，在严重NLoS条件下提升室内定位精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 密集室内环境中，遮挡导致的非视距（NLoS）传播会引入显著延迟，降低基于时间测量的定位精度，因此需要有效的测量选择策略来缓解这一问题。

Method: 使用射线追踪（RT）模拟器表征传播环境并推导AP邻域信息，基于此设计和评估多种测量选择策略，包括AP邻域基数选择、AP邻域测量的交集与并集以及固定测量选择。

Result: 实验证明了所提出的测量选择策略在严重NLoS环境下的有效性，能够提升位置估计精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于AP邻域的测量选择策略能够有效缓解NLoS传播带来的延迟影响，从而提高室内定位精度。

Original Abstract: Time-based indoor positioning techniques rely on multiple access points (APs) and measurements between the user equipment (UE) and the APs. In dense indoor environments, occlusion-induced non-line-of-sight (NLoS) propagation introduces significant delays in these measurements, thereby degrading position estimation accuracy. To address this challenge, this paper proposes measurement selection strategies to improve position estimation accuracy. A ray-tracing (RT) simulator is employed to characterize the propagation environment and derive AP neighborhood information, which is subsequently used to design and evaluate different measurement selection strategies. The approaches explored include AP neighborhood-based cardinality selection, intersection and union of measurements from AP neighborhoods, and fixed measurement selection. Experiments demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed measurement selection strategies in environments under significant NLoS conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 基于时间的室内定位技术依赖于多个接入点（AP）以及用户设备（UE）与AP之间的测量值。在密集的室内环境中，遮挡引起的非视距（NLoS）传播会在这些测量值中引入显著延迟，从而降低位置估计精度。为解决这一挑战，本文提出了测量选择策略以提高位置估计精度。采用射线追踪（RT）模拟器来表征传播环境并推导AP邻域信息，进而用于设计和评估不同的测量选择策略。所探索的方法包括基于AP邻域的基数选择、AP邻域测量的交集与并集以及固定测量选择。实验证明了所提出的测量选择策略在严重NLoS条件下的有效性。

</details>


### [233] [Hyperbolic Frequency Multicarrier Modulation for Wideband Linear Time-Varying Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19515)
*Xuehan Wang,Jinhong Yuan,Jintao Wang,Zhi Sun,Jinxing Hao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对宽带线性时变信道中高移动性带来的时延和尺度效应问题，提出了一种基于仿射域和双曲频率调制的超可靠多载波波形——双曲频率多载波（HFMC），将多普勒尺度因子吸收为等效时延，实现一维联合表征，并通过参数优化平衡效率与可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 已有的多载波调制方案大多针对窄带双弥散信道，但在宽带线性时变信道中，高移动性导致的时间伸缩效应使得基带信号无法假设恒定多普勒频移，因此亟需一种能在宽带场景下实现超可靠传输的调制框架。

Method: 受仿射频分复用（AFDM）启发，利用宽带线性时变信道的通带表示和双曲频率调制（HFM）信号，将多普勒尺度因子吸收为等效时延；构建基于均匀间隔等效时延的HFMC子载波，分析其近似正交性和频谱特性，建立输入-输出表征，并基于此进行参数优化。

Result: 揭示了多普勒尺度因子可被吸收为等效时延；证明了HFMC子载波间的近似正交性；观察到频谱在频域上的重叠特性；确认了每条路径的时间延迟与多普勒尺度因子的1D联合集成，表明能利用潜在的多径分集增益。

Conclusion: HFMC波形能够在宽带线性时变信道中实现超可靠传输，通过参数优化可在效率与可靠性之间取得平衡，为高移动性宽带通信提供了一种有效的调制方案。

Original Abstract: Numerous multicarrier modulation schemes have been proposed recently to enhance the performance in narrowband doubly dispersive channels for emerging high-mobility applications. However, the ultra-reliable modulation framework in wideband linear time-varying (LTV) channels remains an open problem, where the time dilations and contractions brought by the high mobility cannot be ignored for the baseband signal to obtain the constant Doppler shift across the whole transmission band. To solve this problem, we propose the hyperbolic frequency multicarrier (HFMC) waveform in this paper based on the inspiration from affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) modulation, where the delay and Doppler shift are absorbed into a 1D shift in the affine domain to provide a compact characterization of doubly dispersive discrete-time channels. By adopting the passband representation of wideband LTV channels and hyperbolic frequency modulated (HFM) signals, we reveal that the Doppler scaling factor brought by the relative mobility can be absorbed into an equivalent delay. The basic principle of HFMC modulation is established by investigating the approximate orthogonality among HFMC subcarriers, which are generated from a basic HFM signal by utilizing uniformly spaced equivalent delay. The spectrum of HFMC subcarriers is also analyzed to evaluate the system capacity, where the overlapping nature in the frequency domain can be observed. The input-output characterization in wideband LTV channels is then executed to confirm the 1D integration of time delay and Doppler scaling factor for each path, which demonstrates the ability to exploit potential multipath diversity. The parameter optimization based on the input-output relation and spectrum analysis is finally developed to balance the efficiency and reliability.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，为提升新兴高移动性应用中窄带双弥散信道的性能，研究者提出了多种多载波调制方案。然而，宽带线性时变（LTV）信道中的超可靠调制框架仍是一个开放问题——在该信道中，高移动性带来的时间压缩与展宽效应使得基带信号无法在整个传输频带上获得恒定多普勒频移。为解决该问题，本文受仿射频分复用（AFDM）调制启发，提出双曲频率多载波（HFMC）波形，将时延和多普勒频移吸收为仿射域中的一维偏移，从而为双弥散离散时间信道提供紧凑的表征。通过采用宽带LTV信道的通带表示和双曲频率调制（HFM）信号，我们发现相对移动性带来的多普勒尺度因子可被吸收为等效时延。利用均匀间隔等效时延从基本HFM信号生成HFMC子载波，通过研究子载波间的近似正交性，建立了HFMC调制的基本原理。通过分析HFMC子载波频谱以评估系统容量，观察到频域上的重叠特性。进一步在宽带LTV信道中执行输入-输出表征，确认了每条路径的时间延迟与多普勒尺度因子的一维集成，证明了利用潜在多径分集的能力。最后，基于输入-输出关系和频谱分析进行参数优化，以平衡效率与可靠性。

</details>


### [234] [DAFT-s-AFDM Enabled ISAC Systems: Ambiguity Function Analysis and Waveform Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19759)
*Shiqi Cui,Tianqi Mao,Fan Zhang,Zeping Sui,Christos Masouros,Zhaocheng Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文对离散仿射傅里叶变换扩频仿射频分复用（DAFT-s-AFDM）波形进行了全面的模糊函数（AF）分析，推导了AF幅度期望的闭式表达式，并在此基础上提出了一种概率星座整形（PCS）框架，通过多目标优化联合提升通信吞吐量与感知AF特性，实现了通感一体化性能的显著提升与可控权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: DAFT-s-AFDM波形在通感一体化（ISAC）中具有低峰均比、抗多普勒频移、降低上行多用户干扰等优势，但其模糊函数特性尚缺乏系统分析，且如何针对ISAC进行波形优化设计仍有待探索。

Method: 1. 推导了DAFT-s-AFDM模糊函数幅度期望的闭式表达式；2. 分析了波形参数对ISAC性能的影响；3. 提出概率星座整形（PCS）框架，构建联合优化通信吞吐量与感知AF特征的多目标问题；4. 基于闭式误码率表达式开发高效算法求解帕累托最优解。

Result: 理论推导得到了AF幅度期望的闭式解，揭示了DAFT-s-AFDM参数对ISAC性能的关键影响；仿真验证了理论结果的正确性，所提PCS增强型DAFT-s-AFDM显著优于经典方案，实现了通感双功能性能的优越且高度可控的权衡。

Conclusion: DAFT-s-AFDM是一种极具潜力的ISAC波形，通过所提出的PCS框架可有效增强其通感一体化性能，为波形设计提供了理论指导与实用优化算法。

Original Abstract: Discrete affine Fourier transform spread affine frequency division multiplexing (DAFT-s-AFDM) is a promising waveform for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) due to its low peak-to-average power ratio, robustness to Doppler shifts, and reduced multiuser interference in the uplink transmission. This paper presents a comprehensive ambiguity function (AF) analysis of DAFT-s-AFDM and derives the closed-form expression for the AF magnitude expectation. Several key insights into the impact of DAFT-s-AFDM parameters on ISAC performance are revealed, thus providing concrete guidance for the subsequent waveform design. Building on these insights, a novel probabilistic constellation shaping (PCS) framework is proposed for ISAC waveform enhancement, where the communication throughput and the sensing AF characteristics are jointly optimized by addressing a multi-objective problem. An efficient algorithm based on a closed-form bit error rate expression is developed to obtain the Pareto-optimal solutions. Extensive simulations validate the theoretical results and that the proposed PCS-enhanced DAFT-s-AFDM can significantly outperform the classical counterparts, achieving a superior and highly controllable tradeoff between the dual-functional performances.

中文摘要翻译: 离散仿射傅里叶变换扩频仿射频分复用（DAFT-s-AFDM）因其低峰均功率比、对多普勒频移的鲁棒性以及在上行传输中减少多用户干扰等优势，成为一种有前景的通感一体化（ISAC）波形。本文对DAFT-s-AFDM进行了全面的模糊函数（AF）分析，推导了AF幅度期望的闭式表达式。研究揭示了DAFT-s-AFDM参数对ISAC性能影响的若干关键见解，从而为后续波形设计提供了具体指导。基于这些见解，本文提出了一种新颖的概率星座整形（PCS）框架用于ISAC波形增强，该框架通过求解多目标问题，联合优化通信吞吐量与感知AF特性。本文基于闭式误码率表达式开发了一种高效算法，用于获得帕累托最优解。大量仿真验证了理论结果的正确性，并表明所提出的PCS增强型DAFT-s-AFDM在双功能性能上显著优于经典方案，实现了优越且高度可控的权衡。

</details>


### [235] [Practical RIS Gain without the Pain via Randomization and Opportunistic Scheduling in 5G~NR Wireless Systems: Theory and Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19787)
*Nekkanti Guna Sai Kiran,L. Yashvanth,Raju Malleboina,Venkatareddy Akumalla,Debdeep Sarkar,Chandra R. Murthy*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种将内部构建的可重构智能表面（RIS）与基于OpenAirInterface（OAI）的5G新空口（NR）系统集成的方法。不同于传统依赖显式信道状态信息（CSI）估计和RIS相位优化的方案，该方法采用随机切换RIS相位配置的低复杂度策略，利用5G NR固有的比例公平（PF）调度机制来利用信道波动。论文建立了理论框架来刻画RIS切换动态与PF调度之间的交互，并给出了选择RIS切换时间Ts和PF吞吐量平均窗口Tc的设计准则，以最大化系统吞吐量。实验结果表明，随机配置的RIS在适当参数选择下能达到与优化RIS设计相当的性能，且无需额外的系统开销或协调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统RIS辅助系统通常需要显式的CSI估计和复杂的相位优化，这带来了较高的计算开销和系统复杂度，且需要RIS与通信系统之间进行协调。本文旨在探索一种低复杂度的RIS集成方案，通过随机切换RIS配置，无需CSI估计和优化，也无需RIS与5G系统之间的协调，同时利用5G NR已有的调度机制来获得性能增益。

Method: 1) 使用内部构建的RIS与基于OAI软件栈的5G NR测试台集成；2) 采用低复杂度方法，随机切换RIS相位状态（在预定义的配置之间切换）；3) 利用5G NR系统的比例公平（PF）调度机制来机会性地利用RIS随机切换引入的信道波动；4) 建立理论框架分析RIS切换动态与PF调度之间的交互；5) 基于该框架给出设计准则，选择最优的RIS切换时间Ts和PF吞吐量平均窗口Tc；6) 在5G NR实验台上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验评估表明，所提方法在参考信号接收功率（RSRP）、块错误率（BLER）、调制与编码方案（MCS）索引和吞吐量等关键性能指标上均有提升。随机配置的RIS在适当选择系统参数（Ts和Tc）后，能够达到与优化RIS设计相当的性能。

Conclusion: 随机配置的RIS操作，在恰当选择系统参数的情况下，可以实现与优化RIS设计相近的性能，且相比传统5G NR系统无需额外开销，更重要的是不需要RIS与5G NR系统之间的任何协调。这为低复杂度、即插即用的RIS部署提供了一种可行方案。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we theoretically analyze and experimentally demonstrate the performance gains achievable by integrating an in-house built reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) with a 5G new radio (NR) system implemented using the OpenAirInterface (OAI) software stack. Unlike conventional RIS-assisted systems that rely on explicit channel state information (CSI) estimation followed by RIS phase configuration optimization, we adopt a low-complexity approach in which the RIS phase states are randomly switched among predefined configurations. The resulting channel fluctuations are opportunistically exploited by the inherent proportional fair (PF) scheduling mechanism of 5G NR. We develop a theoretical framework that characterizes the interaction between RIS switching dynamics and PF scheduling. Based on this framework and the associated analysis, we provide design guidelines for selecting the RIS switching time $T_s$ and the PF throughput averaging window $T_c$ that maximize the system throughput. Experimental evaluations on the 5G NR testbed demonstrate improvements in key performance metrics, including reference signal received power (RSRP), block error rate (BLER), modulation and coding scheme (MCS) index, and throughput. Our key takeaway is that randomly configured RIS operation with appropriately chosen system parameters can achieve performance comparable to optimized RIS designs, with no additional overhead compared to a conventional 5G NR system. More importantly, it requires no coordination between the RIS and the 5G NR system.

中文摘要翻译: 本文从理论上分析并通过实验验证了将内部构建的可重构智能表面（RIS）与基于OpenAirInterface（OAI）软件栈实现的5G新空口（NR）系统集成所能带来的性能增益。与依赖显式信道状态信息（CSI）估计然后进行RIS相位配置优化的传统RIS辅助系统不同，我们采用了一种低复杂度的方法，即在预定义的配置之间随机切换RIS相位状态。由此产生的信道波动被5G NR固有的比例公平（PF）调度机制机会性地利用。我们建立了一个理论框架来描述RIS切换动态与PF调度之间的交互。基于该框架及相关分析，我们提供了选择RIS切换时间$T_s$和PF吞吐量平均窗口$T_c$的设计准则，以最大化系统吞吐量。在5G NR测试台上的实验评估表明，关键性能指标（包括参考信号接收功率（RSRP）、块错误率（BLER）、调制与编码方案（MCS）索引和吞吐量）均有改善。我们的主要结论是：在适当选择系统参数的情况下，随机配置的RIS操作可以达到与优化RIS设计相当的性能，且相比传统5G NR系统无需额外开销。更重要的是，该方法不需要RIS与5G NR系统之间的任何协调。

</details>


### [236] [ADOPT: Analytical Demodulation of Periodic Textures for In-Plane Wave Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19963)
*Jalal Jouidi,Florent Chatelain,Lucie Bailly,Pierre Granjon,Nicolas Le Bihan,Stefan Catheline*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出ADOPT方法，利用周期性表面图案从图像序列中跟踪面内波，通过分析二维解析信号进行相位解调，实现对波致位移的稳定估计，在精度和计算效率上优于传统数字图像相关方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数字图像相关（DIC）方法在小位移和高信噪比场景下精度受限且计算效率不高，需要一种更鲁棒高效的波致位移估计方法。

Method: 提出ADOPT方法，基于面向二维解析信号（oriented 2D analytic signal）对周期性载波的空间相位调制进行解析解调，通过方向选择性滤波分离相关谱分量，提取相位以重建位移场，并结合纵向和横向面内波的物理模型进行估计。

Result: 理论分析通过Cramér-Rao界评估性能极限；仿真表明ADOPT在高信噪比下优于DIC，尤其在小位移场景；计算效率更高；在硅胶膜周期性图案上的实验验证了波场和色散曲线的准确估计。

Conclusion: ADOPT为波致位移估计提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案，在精度和计算成本上均具有优势。

Original Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of tracking in-plane waves from image sequences using periodic surface patterns. Wave-induced deformation is modeled as a spatial phase modulation of a periodic carrier. We propose ADOPT (Analytical Demodulation of Periodic Texture), a method based on an oriented two-dimensional analytic signal to estimate displacement phase and orientation. The approach relies on a physical model describing longitudinal and transverse in-plane waves. Orientation-selective filtering isolates relevant spectral components, and phase extraction provides a stable reconstruction of the displacement field. A theoretical analysis using the Cramer--Rao bound evaluates performance limits of ADOPT. Simulations show that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art Digital Image Correlation (DIC) at high signal-to-noise ratios, especially for small displacements where DIC becomes limited. Moreover, ADOPT is more computationally efficient. Experiments on silicone membranes with periodic patterns confirm accurate estimation of wave fields and dispersion curves under impulsive excitation. Overall, the proposed framework provides a robust and efficient solution for wave-induced displacement estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了利用周期性表面图案从图像序列中跟踪面内波的问题。波引起的变形被建模为周期性载波的空间相位调制。我们提出ADOPT（周期性纹理的解析解调），一种基于面向二维解析信号的方法，用于估计位移相位和方向。该方法依赖于描述纵向和横向面内波的物理模型。方向选择性滤波隔离了相关的频谱分量，相位提取提供了位移场的稳定重建。利用Cramér-Rao界进行的理论分析评估了ADOPT的性能极限。仿真表明，所提出的方法在高信噪比下优于最先进的数字图像相关（DIC）方法，尤其是在DIC受限的小位移场景中。此外，ADOPT的计算效率更高。在具有周期性图案的硅胶膜上进行的实验证实了在脉冲激励下波场和色散曲线的准确估计。总体而言，所提出的框架为波致位移估计提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [SpecX: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Multi-Modal Spectroscopy and Cross-Paradigm Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18791)
*Chengrui Xiang,Tengfei Ma,Yujie Chen,Tong Wang,Haowen Chen,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SpecX，一个大规模多模态光谱基准，包含 170 万分子和 7 种光谱模态，支持跨范式评估（专用模型与多模态大语言模型），并揭示了专用模型在信号级建模上更强，而 MLLM 在高层次推理方面有优势但缺乏精确光谱基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有光谱基准在规模、模态对齐和评估范围上存在局限，且通常只关注专用模型或多模态语言模型（MLLMs）中的某一类，缺乏统一评估框架。

Method: 构建 SpecX 基准，包含 170 万分子及 NMR（1H, 13C, HSQC）、IR、MS、UV、Raman 和 FL 七种光谱模态，组织为三个层级（大规模预训练集、对齐多光谱子集、高质量实验子集），支持分子解析、光谱模拟和光谱理解等任务，并实现跨专用模型与 MLLM 的统一评估。

Result: 实验表明专用模型在信号级建模上表现优异，而多模态大语言模型在高层次推理上具有优势，但缺乏精确的光谱基础能力。

Conclusion: SpecX 为光谱智能建立了统一基准，并凸显了构建光谱原生基础模型的必要性。

Original Abstract: Existing spectral benchmarks are limited in scale, modality alignment, and evaluation scope, and typically focus on either specialized models or multimodal language models (MLLMs). We introduce SpecX, a large-scale benchmark for multi-modal spectroscopy with cross-paradigm evaluation. SpecX contains 1.7M molecules with diverse spectral modalities, including NMR (1H, 13C, HSQC), IR, MS,UV,Raman and FL, and is organized into three tiers: a large-scale dataset for pretraining, an aligned multi-spectral subset for benchmarking, and a high-quality experimental subset for evaluation. SpecX supports a range of tasks such as molecular elucidation, spectrum simulation, and spectral understanding, and enables unified evaluation across both specialized spectral models and MLLMs. Experiments show that specialized models excel at signal-level modeling, while MLLMs exhibit strengths in high-level reasoning but lack precise spectral grounding. SpecX establishes a unified benchmark for spectral intelligence and highlights the need for spectrum-native foundation models.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的光谱基准在规模、模态对齐和评估范围上存在局限，且通常只关注专用模型或多模态语言模型（MLLMs）中的某一类。我们提出了 SpecX，一个面向多模态光谱的大规模跨范式评估基准。SpecX 包含 170 万个分子，涵盖 NMR（1H, 13C, HSQC）、IR、MS、UV、Raman 和 FL 等多种光谱模态，并组织为三个层级：大规模预训练数据集、对齐的多光谱子集以及高质量实验子集。SpecX 支持分子解析、光谱模拟和光谱理解等一系列任务，并能同时对专用光谱模型和多模态语言模型进行统一评估。实验结果表明，专用模型在信号级建模方面表现出色，而多模态语言模型在高层次推理上具有优势，但缺乏精确的光谱基础能力。SpecX 为光谱智能建立了统一基准，并凸显了构建光谱原生基础模型的必要性。

</details>


### [238] [From Division to Decision: Leveraging Temporal Cell-Stage Segmentation for Embryo Transferability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18923)
*Yasmine Hachani,Patrick Bouthemy,Elisa Fromont,Véronique Duranthon,Ludivine Laffont,Alline de Paula Reis*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: TransFACT是一个基于Transformer的框架，利用前四天发育的2D延时视频来预测牛胚胎的可移植性，通过结合帧级时间特征和阶段级表示，并使用时序动作识别方法实现了优于现有方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前牛胚胎选择依赖第七天的单一专家评估，导致高妊娠失败率；虽然延时显微摄影能提供早期发育的详细信息，但由于复杂的运动模式和耗时的分析过程，难以有效利用。

Method: 提出TransFACT框架，基于Transformer架构，使用前四天发育的2D延时视频，结合帧级时间特征与阶段级表示，并以发育阶段作为辅助监督信号，在第四天预测胚胎可移植性。

Result: 通过利用现有的动作识别方法，TransFACT在预测胚胎可移植性任务上取得了优于竞争对手的性能。

Conclusion: TransFACT能够有效利用早期发育延时视频中的时序信息，在更早的时间点（第四天）对牛胚胎可移植性进行准确预测，优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Accurate selection of bovine embryos is a challenging task, as current practice relies on a single expert assessment on the seventh day after insemination, resulting in high rates of pregnancy loss. Time-lapse videomicroscopy provides detailed information on early development, but is difficult to exploit because of complex motion patterns and time-consuming analysis. We propose TransFACT, a transformer-based framework for modeling early developmental stages and embryo transferability using 2D time-lapse videos from the first four days of development. TransFACT combines frame-level temporal features with stage-level representations, using developmental stages as auxiliary supervision to predict transferability on day four. Our experiments demonstrate that TransFACT, by leveraging an existing method designed for action recognition, achieves superior performance than its competitor in predicting embryo transferability.

中文摘要翻译: 牛胚胎的准确选择是一项具有挑战性的任务，因为当前的做法依赖于授精后第七天单一专家的评估，导致较高的妊娠失败率。延时显微摄影提供了早期发育的详细信息，但由于复杂的运动模式和耗时的分析过程，难以充分利用。我们提出了TransFACT，这是一个基于Transformer的框架，利用发育前四天的2D延时视频对早期发育阶段和胚胎可移植性进行建模。TransFACT结合了帧级时间特征与阶段级表示，以发育阶段作为辅助监督信号，在第四天预测胚胎可移植性。我们的实验表明，通过利用现有的动作识别方法，TransFACT在预测胚胎可移植性方面取得了优于竞争对手的性能。

</details>


### [239] [Next-Acceleration-Scale Prediction for Autoregressive MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19354)
*Yilmaz Korkmaz,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出将MRI重建任务转化为离散多尺度潜空间中的自回归下一加速尺度预测，并引入基于策略的特权信息蒸馏技术，在极端欠采样条件下实现了更锐利、更高质量的重建结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统MRI重建在高度加速下因像素域连续预测器会平均化可行重建结果并抑制高频解剖细节，导致重建模糊；为解决此问题，需将重建迁移到离散多尺度潜空间。

Method: 将MRI重建建模为离散多尺度潜空间中的自回归下一加速尺度预测；利用码本令牌的紧凑序列约束解空间；引入基于策略的特权信息蒸馏（OPD），在训练时为教师模型提供完整采样数据作为特权上下文，监督在其自身rollout上训练的学生模型。

Result: 在fastMRI基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在极端欠采样下、多种采样模式下均实现了更优的重建性能。

Conclusion: 离散自回归公式能够与大语言模型的后训练技术自然对齐，结合基于策略的特权信息蒸馏可显著提升重建质量。

Original Abstract: MRI reconstruction is an inherently ill-posed inverse problem, since incomplete measurements admit many plausible solutions. This ambiguity becomes more severe under high acceleration, where pixel-domain continuous predictors tend to average over feasible reconstructions and suppress high-frequency anatomy. We address this limitation by moving reconstruction to discrete multi-scale latent space and posing it as autoregressive next-acceleration-scale prediction. Leveraging discrete priors proven effective in visual autoregressive modeling, our method restricts the solution to compact sequences of codebook tokens, enabling sharp reconstructions even from extremely sparse measurements. This discrete autoregressive formulation also aligns naturally with modern large language model post-training techniques. Building on this observation, we introduce on-policy privileged information distillation for visual autoregressive modeling, where a teacher is provided training only privileged context that is unavailable at inference, in our case fully sampled acquisitions, and supervises a student trained on its own rollouts, leading to consistent reconstruction gains. Through extensive experiments on the fastMRI benchmark, we show that our approach delivers improved reconstruction performance across diverse sampling patterns under extreme undersampling. Project website is \hyperlink{https://github.com/yilmazkorkmaz1/discrete-mri-reconstruction-opd}{here}.

中文摘要翻译: MRI重建本质上是一个不适定的逆问题，因为不完整的测量允许存在许多合理的解。这种歧义在高加速采样下变得更加严重，此时像素域的连续预测器倾向于平均化可行的重建结果并抑制高频解剖结构。我们通过将重建迁移到离散多尺度潜空间并将其建模为自回归的下一加速尺度预测来应对这一局限。利用在视觉自回归建模中已被证明有效的离散先验，我们的方法将解空间限制为码本令牌的紧凑序列，从而即使在极稀疏的测量下也能实现锐利重建。这种离散自回归公式自然符合现代大语言模型的后训练技术。基于这一观察，我们引入了用于视觉自回归建模的基于策略的特权信息蒸馏方法，其中教师模型在训练时仅获取推理阶段不可用的特权上下文（在本研究中为完整采样的采集数据），并监督在其自身rollout上训练的学生模型，从而带来一致的重建性能提升。通过在fastMRI基准上进行的大量实验，我们表明我们的方法在极端欠采样下、多种采样模式中均能提供更优的重建性能。项目网站见：\hyperlink{https://github.com/yilmazkorkmaz1/discrete-mri-reconstruction-opd}{此处}。

</details>


### [240] [Diffusion Graph Posterior Sampling for Nonlinear Inverse Problems with Application to Electrical Impedance Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19621)
*Giovanni S. Alberti,Damiana Lazzaro,Serena Morigi,Matteo Santacesaria,Shibo Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散后验采样（DPS）扩展到图结构数据，用于解决非结构化网格上的逆问题（如电阻抗成像）。通过构建基于三角网格的无条件分数扩散模型作为先验，并引入正则化变体RDPS（结合全变分和广义Tikhonov正则化），在合成和真实数据集上取得了优于现有最先进方法的稳定、物理合理的重建结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 物理域通常离散为非结构化网格（如三角网格），而非规则网格，导致标准卷积架构无法直接适用。现有扩散模型主要面向规则网格数据，难以处理基于网格的PDE逆问题（如EIT）。

Method: 提出图结构数据上的扩散后验采样框架：1) 在2D三角网格上构建无条件分数扩散模型，学习物理解空间上的准确先验；2) 提出正则化变体RDPS，引入明确的先验正则化项（如全变分、广义Tikhonov），以弥补隐式扩散先验的不足并缓解严重不适定性。

Result: 在合成和真实2D EIT数据集上的实验表明：RDPS能产生稳定、物理合理的重建结果；对未见过的外分布包含几何形状具有良好泛化能力；对测量噪声高度鲁棒；在重建精度和伪影减少方面优于当前最先进方法（如GPnP-BM3D、DP-SGS）。

Conclusion: 基于图结构的扩散后验采样框架能有效处理物理域上非结构化网格的逆问题，正则化变体RDPS进一步提升了在严重不适定场景下的重建稳定性和质量。

Original Abstract: Deep generative models have emerged as state-of-the-art for solving inverse problems, but applying them to inverse problems for PDEs, like electrical impedance tomography (EIT) remains challenging. Because physical domains are naturally discretized as unstructured meshes rather than regular grids, standard convolutional architectures are often inadequate. In this paper, we propose a novel framework that extends diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) to graph-structured data. We develop an unconditional score-based diffusion model directly on a 2D triangular mesh to learn an accurate prior over the physical solution space. Furthermore, we introduce a regularized variant, RDPS, which incorporates explicit regularization terms, such as total variation and generalized Tikhonov, to complement the implicit diffusion prior and mitigate severe ill-posedness. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real 2D EIT datasets demonstrate that RDPS produces stable, physically plausible reconstructions. Our approach generalizes well to out-of-distribution inclusion geometries, is highly robust to measurement noise, and outperforms current state-of-the-art solvers (e.g., GPnP-BM3D, DP-SGS) in reconstruction accuracy and artifact reduction.

中文摘要翻译: 深度生成模型已成为解决逆问题的最先进方法，但将其应用于偏微分方程的逆问题（如电阻抗成像）仍然具有挑战性。由于物理域自然地离散为非结构化网格而非规则网格，标准的卷积架构通常不适用。本文提出了一种新颖的框架，将扩散后验采样（DPS）扩展到图结构数据。我们直接在二维三角网格上开发了一个无条件分数扩散模型，以学习物理解空间上的准确先验。此外，我们引入了正则化变体RDPS，该变体结合了明确的先验正则化项（如全变分和广义Tikhonov正则化），以补充隐式扩散先验并缓解严重的不适定性。在合成和真实二维EIT数据集上的广泛实验表明，RDPS能够产生稳定、物理合理的重建结果。我们的方法对未见过的外分布包含几何形状具有良好的泛化能力，对测量噪声高度鲁棒，并且在重建精度和伪影减少方面优于当前最先进的求解器（如GPnP-BM3D、DP-SGS）。

</details>


### [241] [FGSVQA: Frequency-Guided Short-form Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20016)
*Xinyi Wang,Angeliki Katsenou,Junxiao Shen,David Bull*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文针对短视频质量评估中的复杂生成管道、快速内容变化和混合失真等挑战，提出了一种基于CLIP密集视觉编码器的端到端VQA框架，通过频域压缩先验生成伪影和结构感知权重图，并利用可学习的门控模块自适应融合伪影、结构和原始视觉特征分支，实现了准确高效的短视频质量预测，在短视频数据集上SRCC达0.736，PLCC达0.787。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 短视频因其复杂的生成管道、快速的内容变化和混合的失真类型，给用户生成内容（UGC）的质量评估带来了新的挑战，现有方法难以有效应对这些问题。

Method: 提出一个端到端的视频质量评估框架，采用基于CLIP的密集视觉编码器，结合频域导出的压缩先验生成伪影和结构感知权重图用于特征聚合；显式分解伪影、结构和原始视觉特征三个分支，并通过可学习的门控模块在时间维度上自适应融合这些分支。

Result: 在短视频数据集上取得了优异的性能，SRCC达到0.736，PLCC达到0.787，同时保持高效的推理时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够准确高效地预测短视频质量，在性能与效率之间取得了良好的平衡。

Original Abstract: Short-form video poses new challenges to the quality assessment of user-generated content (UGC) due to its complex generation pipeline, rapid content variation, and mixed distortions. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end video quality assessment (VQA) framework that employs a dense visual encoder based on CLIP, and incorporates compression priors derived from the frequency domain to generate artifact- and structure-aware weight maps for feature aggregation. By explicitly decomposing artifact, structure, and original visual feature branches and adaptively fusing them over time through a learned gating module, the proposed method achieves accurate and efficient quality prediction. Experimental results show that our method achieves strong performance on short-form video datasets in terms of average rank and linear correlation (SRCC: 0.736, PLCC: 0.787), while maintaining efficient inference runtime. The code and additional results are available at: https://github.com/xinyiW915/FGSVQA.

中文摘要翻译: 短视频因其复杂的生成管道、快速的内容变化以及混合的失真类型，给用户生成内容（UGC）的质量评估带来了新的挑战。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种端到端的视频质量评估（VQA）框架，该框架采用基于CLIP的密集视觉编码器，并结合从频域导出的压缩先验，生成伪影感知和结构感知的权重图用于特征聚合。通过显式分解伪影、结构和原始视觉特征分支，并利用可学习的门控模块在时间维度上自适应地融合它们，所提出的方法实现了准确且高效的质量预测。实验结果表明，我们的方法在短视频数据集上取得了优异的性能，平均秩相关系数（SRCC）达到0.736，线性相关系数（PLCC）达到0.787，同时保持了高效的推理运行时间。代码和更多结果可在 https://github.com/xinyiW915/FGSVQA 获取。

</details>
